========== FILE: .\prisma\schema.prisma ==========
generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client"

  output = "../generated/prisma"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"

  url = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

// ==========================================
// ENUMS
// ==========================================
enum ItemType {
  RAW_MATERIAL

  COMPONENT

  FINISHED_GOOD

  SEMI_FINISHED

  PACKAGING

  CONSUMABLE
}

enum UnitOfMeasure {
  PIECE

  METER

  KILOGRAM

  GRAM

  LITER

  BOX

  ROLL

  SET
}

enum StockStatus {
  AVAILABLE

  QUARANTINE

  BLOCKED
}

enum BatchQualityStatus {
  PENDING

  APPROVED

  REJECTED
}

enum MovementType {
  RECEIPT

  ISSUE

  TRANSFER

  ADJUSTMENT

  PRODUCTION_CONSUMPTION

  PRODUCTION_OUTPUT

  SALES_SHIPMENT

  RETURN

  REVERSAL
}

enum DocumentStatus {
  DRAFT

  POSTED

  CANCELLED
}

enum VersionStatus {
  DRAFT

  APPROVED

  OBSOLETE
}

enum WorkOrderStatus {
  DRAFT

  PLANNED

  RELEASED

  IN_PROGRESS

  PAUSED

  COMPLETED

  CANCELLED
}

enum ProductionRunStatus {
  IN_PROGRESS

  PAUSED

  COMPLETED

  CANCELLED
}

enum OperationStatus {
  PENDING

  IN_PROGRESS

  PAUSED

  COMPLETED

  SKIPPED
}

enum QCStage {
  INCOMING

  IN_PROCESS

  FINAL
}

enum QCResult {
  PENDING

  PASSED

  FAILED

  CONDITIONAL
}

enum QuotationStatus {
  DRAFT

  SENT

  ACCEPTED

  REJECTED

  EXPIRED

  CONVERTED
}

enum SalesOrderStatus {
  DRAFT

  CONFIRMED

  IN_PRODUCTION

  PARTIALLY_DELIVERED

  DELIVERED

  CANCELLED
}

enum DeliveryStatus {
  DRAFT

  POSTED

  CANCELLED
}

// ==========================================
// MASTER DATA
// ==========================================
model Item {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  sku String @unique

  name String

  description String?

  itemType ItemType

  baseUnit UnitOfMeasure

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
  // Inventory
  batches   Batch[]

  balances StockBalance[]

  movements StockMovement[]

  reservations StockReservation[]

  receiptLines   GoodsReceiptLine[]
  // Production
  productProfile ProductProfile?

  bomComponents BomLine[]

  materialConsumptions MaterialConsumption[]

  productionOutputs ProductionOutput[]
  // Sales
  quotationLines    QuotationLine[]

  salesOrderLines SalesOrderLine[]

  deliveryLines DeliveryLine[]

  @@index([name])
  @@index([itemType])
}

// ==========================================
// SUPPLIERS
// ==========================================
model Supplier {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  code String @unique

  name String

  nationalId String?

  phone String?

  email String?

  address String?

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  goodsReceipts GoodsReceipt[]
}

// ==========================================
// WAREHOUSE
// ==========================================
model Warehouse {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  code String @unique

  name String

  description String?

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  locations StockLocation[]
}

model StockLocation {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  warehouseId String

  code String

  name String

  description String?

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  warehouse Warehouse @relation(fields: [warehouseId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  balances StockBalance[]

  movementsFrom StockMovement[] @relation("MovementSource")

  movementsTo StockMovement[] @relation("MovementDestination")

  reservations StockReservation[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([warehouseId, code])
  @@index([warehouseId])
}

// ==========================================
// BATCH TRACEABILITY
// ==========================================
model Batch {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  itemId String

  batchNumber String

  supplierBatchNumber String?

  manufacturingDate DateTime?

  expiryDate DateTime?

  qualityStatus BatchQualityStatus @default(PENDING)

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  balances StockBalance[]

  movements StockMovement[]

  reservations StockReservation[]

  receiptLines GoodsReceiptLine[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([itemId, batchNumber])
  @@index([batchNumber])
  @@index([qualityStatus])
}

// ==========================================
// INVENTORY BALANCE
// ==========================================
model StockBalance {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  itemId String

  locationId String

  batchId String?

  status StockStatus @default(AVAILABLE)

  quantity Decimal @default(0) @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  location StockLocation @relation(fields: [locationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  batch Batch? @relation(fields: [batchId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  // ØªÙˆØ¬Ù‡: ÛŒÚ©ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒ batchId ØªÙ‡ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± PostgreSQL Ù†ÛŒØ§Ø²Ù…Ù†Ø¯ Ø§ÛŒÙ†Ø¯Ú©Ø³ Ø¬Ø²Ø¦ÛŒ Ø§Ø³Øª.
  // Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ù…Ø­Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ÛŒØª Ø¯Ø± Ù…Ø±Ø­Ù„Ù‡ Migration Ø¨Ø§ SQL ØªÚ©Ù…ÛŒÙ„ÛŒ Ø§Ø¹Ù…Ø§Ù„ Ø®ÙˆØ§Ù‡Ø¯ Ø´Ø¯.
  @@unique([itemId, locationId, batchId, status])
  @@index([itemId, locationId])
  @@index([batchId])
}

// ==========================================
// INVENTORY MOVEMENTS
// ==========================================
model StockMovement {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  itemId String

  batchId String?

  sourceLocationId String?

  destinationLocationId String?

  movementType MovementType

  quantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  unitCost Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  referenceType String?

  referenceId String?

  description String?

  status DocumentStatus @default(DRAFT)

  postedAt DateTime?

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  batch Batch? @relation(fields: [batchId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  sourceLocation StockLocation? @relation("MovementSource", fields: [sourceLocationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  destinationLocation StockLocation? @relation("MovementDestination", fields: [destinationLocationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([itemId])
  @@index([batchId])
  @@index([movementType])
  @@index([referenceType, referenceId])
  @@index([postedAt])
}

// ==========================================
// STOCK RESERVATIONS
// ==========================================
model StockReservation {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  itemId String

  locationId String

  batchId String?

  quantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  referenceType String

  referenceId String

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  location StockLocation @relation(fields: [locationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  batch Batch? @relation(fields: [batchId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([itemId, locationId])
  @@index([referenceType, referenceId])
  @@index([batchId])
}

// ==========================================
// GOODS RECEIPTS
// ==========================================
model GoodsReceipt {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  receiptNumber String @unique

  supplierId String

  status DocumentStatus @default(DRAFT)

  receiptDate DateTime @default(now())

  description String?

  supplier Supplier @relation(fields: [supplierId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  lines GoodsReceiptLine[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model GoodsReceiptLine {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  receiptId String

  itemId String

  batchId String?

  lineNumber Int

  quantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  unitCost Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  description String?

  receipt GoodsReceipt @relation(fields: [receiptId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  batch Batch? @relation(fields: [batchId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@unique([receiptId, lineNumber])
  @@index([itemId])
  @@index([batchId])
}

// ==========================================
// PRODUCT TECHNICAL PROFILE
// ==========================================
model ProductProfile {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  itemId String @unique

  connectorFrom String?

  connectorTo String?

  cableLengthMm Int?

  conductorMaterial String?

  jacketMaterial String?

  ratedPowerW Decimal? @db.Decimal(10, 2)

  ratedCurrentA Decimal? @db.Decimal(10, 2)

  supportsData Boolean @default(false)

  hasChip Boolean @default(false)

  chipModel String?

  technicalNotes String?

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  bomVersions BomVersion[]

  routingVersions RoutingVersion[]

  workOrders WorkOrder[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

// ==========================================
// BILL OF MATERIALS
// ==========================================
model BomVersion {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  productProfileId String

  versionNumber Int

  status VersionStatus @default(DRAFT)

  effectiveFrom DateTime?

  effectiveUntil DateTime?

  notes String?

  productProfile ProductProfile @relation(fields: [productProfileId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  lines BomLine[]

  workOrders WorkOrder[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([productProfileId, versionNumber])
  @@index([productProfileId, status])
}

model BomLine {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  bomVersionId String

  componentItemId String

  quantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  wastePercentage Decimal @default(0) @db.Decimal(8, 4)

  sequence Int

  isOptional Boolean @default(false)

  notes String?

  bomVersion BomVersion @relation(fields: [bomVersionId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  componentItem Item @relation(fields: [componentItemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@unique([bomVersionId, sequence])
  @@index([componentItemId])
}

// ==========================================
// ROUTING
// ==========================================
model RoutingVersion {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  productProfileId String

  versionNumber Int

  status VersionStatus @default(DRAFT)

  notes String?

  productProfile ProductProfile @relation(fields: [productProfileId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  steps RoutingStep[]

  workOrders WorkOrder[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([productProfileId, versionNumber])
  @@index([productProfileId, status])
}

model RoutingStep {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  routingVersionId String

  sequence Int

  operationCode String

  operationName String

  workCenterCode String?

  setupTimeMinutes Decimal? @db.Decimal(10, 2)

  standardTimeMinutes Decimal? @db.Decimal(10, 2)

  instructions String?

  requiresQC Boolean @default(false)

  routingVersion RoutingVersion @relation(fields: [routingVersionId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@unique([routingVersionId, sequence])
  @@index([operationCode])
}

// ==========================================
// WORK ORDERS
// ==========================================
model WorkOrder {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  orderNumber String @unique

  productProfileId String

  bomVersionId String

  routingVersionId String

  salesOrderLineId String?

  plannedQuantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  status WorkOrderStatus @default(DRAFT)

  plannedStartAt DateTime?

  plannedEndAt DateTime?

  actualStartAt DateTime?

  actualEndAt DateTime?

  productSnapshot Json?

  bomSnapshot Json?

  routingSnapshot Json?

  notes String?

  productProfile ProductProfile @relation(fields: [productProfileId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  bomVersion BomVersion @relation(fields: [bomVersionId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  routingVersion RoutingVersion @relation(fields: [routingVersionId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  salesOrderLine SalesOrderLine? @relation(fields: [salesOrderLineId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  productionRuns ProductionRun[]

  inspections QCInspection[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([status])
  @@index([productProfileId])
  @@index([salesOrderLineId])
}

// ==========================================
// PRODUCTION RUNS
// ==========================================
model ProductionRun {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  runNumber String @unique

  workOrderId String

  status ProductionRunStatus @default(IN_PROGRESS)

  startedAt DateTime @default(now())

  finishedAt DateTime?

  notes String?

  workOrder WorkOrder @relation(fields: [workOrderId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  operations ProductionOperation[]

  outputs ProductionOutput[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([workOrderId])
  @@index([status])
}

// ==========================================
// PRODUCTION OPERATIONS
// ==========================================
model ProductionOperation {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  productionRunId String

  sequence Int

  operationCode String

  operationName String

  workCenterCode String?

  status OperationStatus @default(PENDING)

  plannedQuantity Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  completedQuantity Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  rejectedQuantity Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  startedAt DateTime?

  finishedAt DateTime?

  operatorUserId String?

  notes String?

  productionRun ProductionRun @relation(fields: [productionRunId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  consumptions MaterialConsumption[]

  inspections QCInspection[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([productionRunId, sequence])
  @@index([status])
}

// ==========================================
// MATERIAL CONSUMPTION
// ==========================================
model MaterialConsumption {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  productionOperationId String

  itemId String

  batchId String?

  quantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  unitCost Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  stockMovementId String?

  notes String?

  productionOperation ProductionOperation @relation(fields: [productionOperationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([itemId, batchId])
  @@index([productionOperationId])
}

// ==========================================
// PRODUCTION OUTPUT
// ==========================================
model ProductionOutput {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  productionRunId String

  itemId String

  batchId String

  quantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  rejectedQuantity Decimal @default(0) @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  stockMovementId String?

  productionRun ProductionRun @relation(fields: [productionRunId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([itemId, batchId])
  @@index([productionRunId])
}

// ==========================================
// QUALITY CONTROL
// ==========================================
model QCInspection {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  inspectionNumber String @unique

  workOrderId String

  productionOperationId String?

  stage QCStage

  result QCResult @default(PENDING)

  batchId String?

  inspectedQuantity Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  passedQuantity Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  failedQuantity Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  inspectorUserId String?

  inspectionDate DateTime @default(now())

  defectDescription String?

  correctiveAction String?

  notes String?

  workOrder WorkOrder @relation(fields: [workOrderId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  productionOperation ProductionOperation? @relation(fields: [productionOperationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([workOrderId, stage])
  @@index([batchId])
  @@index([result])
}

// ==========================================
// CUSTOMERS
// ==========================================
model Customer {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  code String @unique

  name String

  nationalId String?

  phone String?

  mobile String?

  email String?

  address String?

  postalCode String?

  description String?

  paymentTermsDays Int @default(0)

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  quotations Quotation[]

  salesOrders SalesOrder[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([name])
  @@index([nationalId])
}

// ==========================================
// QUOTATIONS
// ==========================================
model Quotation {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  quoteNumber String @unique

  customerId String

  status QuotationStatus @default(DRAFT)

  issueDate DateTime @default(now())

  validUntil DateTime?

  currencyCode String @default("IRR")

  notes String?

  customer Customer @relation(fields: [customerId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  lines QuotationLine[]

  salesOrder SalesOrder?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([customerId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([issueDate])
}

model QuotationLine {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  quotationId String

  itemId String

  lineNumber Int

  description String?

  quantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  unitPrice Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  discountPercent Decimal @default(0) @db.Decimal(8, 4)

  quotation Quotation @relation(fields: [quotationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@unique([quotationId, lineNumber])
  @@index([itemId])
}

// ==========================================
// SALES ORDERS
// ==========================================
model SalesOrder {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  orderNumber String @unique

  customerId String

  quotationId String? @unique

  status SalesOrderStatus @default(DRAFT)

  orderDate DateTime @default(now())

  requestedDeliveryDate DateTime?

  currencyCode String @default("IRR")

  shippingAddress String?

  billingAddress String?

  notes String?

  customer Customer @relation(fields: [customerId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  quotation Quotation? @relation(fields: [quotationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  lines SalesOrderLine[]

  deliveries Delivery[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([customerId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([orderDate])
}

model SalesOrderLine {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  salesOrderId String

  itemId String

  lineNumber Int

  description String?

  quantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  unitPrice Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  discountPercent Decimal @default(0) @db.Decimal(8, 4)

  salesOrder SalesOrder @relation(fields: [salesOrderId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  workOrders WorkOrder[]

  deliveryLines DeliveryLine[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([salesOrderId, lineNumber])
  @@index([itemId])
}

// ==========================================
// DELIVERIES
// ==========================================
model Delivery {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  deliveryNumber String @unique

  salesOrderId String

  status DeliveryStatus @default(DRAFT)

  deliveryDate DateTime @default(now())

  trackingNumber String?

  shippingNotes String?

  notes String?

  salesOrder SalesOrder @relation(fields: [salesOrderId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  lines DeliveryLine[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([salesOrderId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([deliveryDate])
}

model DeliveryLine {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  deliveryId String

  salesOrderLineId String

  itemId String

  batchId String?

  sourceLocationId String?

  quantity Decimal @db.Decimal(18, 4)

  stockMovementId String?

  notes String?

  delivery Delivery @relation(fields: [deliveryId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  salesOrderLine SalesOrderLine @relation(fields: [salesOrderLineId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  item Item @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([deliveryId])
  @@index([salesOrderLineId])
  @@index([itemId, batchId])
}

enum DataScope {
  ALL

  OWN

  DEPARTMENT

  ASSIGNED
}

enum EmployeeEmploymentStatus {
  ACTIVE

  ON_LEAVE

  TERMINATED

  SUSPENDED
}

model User {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  username String @unique

  email String? @unique

  phone String? @unique

  fullName String

  passwordHash String

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  accessLevel UserAccessLevel @default(ERP)

  isSystem Boolean @default(false)

  lastLoginAt DateTime?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  roles UserRole[]

  sessions UserSession[]

  auditLogs            AuditLog[]
  // Recruitment relations
  candidateApplication CandidateApplication? @relation("CandidateApplicant")

  employeeProfile EmployeeProfile?

  applicationReviews ApplicationApproval[] @relation("ApplicationReviewer")

  uploadedCandidateDocuments CandidateDocument[]  @relation("CandidateDocumentUploader")
  //chart sazmani
  employeeAssignments        EmployeeAssignment[] @relation("EmployeeAssignments")

  attendanceApprovals          AttendanceRecord[]              @relation("AttendanceRecordApprover")
  attendanceAdjustmentRequests AttendanceAdjustment[]          @relation("AttendanceAdjustmentRequester")
  attendanceAdjustmentReviews  AttendanceAdjustment[]          @relation("AttendanceAdjustmentReviewer")
  attendanceChallenges         AttendanceChallenge[]
  faceIdentityProfile          FaceIdentityProfile?
  weeklyShiftSchedules         EmployeeShiftSchedule[]         @relation("ShiftScheduleEmployee")
  createdShiftSchedules        EmployeeShiftSchedule[]         @relation("ShiftScheduleCreator")
  submittedShiftSchedules      EmployeeShiftSchedule[]         @relation("ShiftScheduleSubmitter")
  reviewedShiftSchedules       EmployeeShiftSchedule[]         @relation("ShiftScheduleReviewer")
  shiftScheduleRevisions       EmployeeShiftScheduleRevision[] @relation("ShiftScheduleRevisionActor")

  @@index([fullName])
  @@index([isActive])
}

model Role {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  key String @unique

  name String

  description String?

  isSystem Boolean @default(false)

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  users UserRole[]

  permissions RolePermission[]

  assignmentRoles EmployeeAssignmentRole[]

  @@index([name])
  @@index([isActive])
}

model Module {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  key String @unique

  name String

  description String?

  parentId String?

  sortOrder Int @default(0)

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  parent Module? @relation("ModuleTree", fields: [parentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  children Module[] @relation("ModuleTree")

  permissions Permission[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([parentId])
  @@index([sortOrder])
}

model Permission {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  key String @unique

  name String

  description String?

  moduleId String

  module Module @relation(fields: [moduleId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  resource String

  action String

  roles RolePermission[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  assignmentPermissions EmployeeAssignmentPermission[]

  @@unique([moduleId, resource, action])
  @@index([moduleId])
}

model UserRole {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  userId String

  roleId String

  user User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  role Role @relation(fields: [roleId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  assignedById String?

  expiresAt DateTime?

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@unique([userId, roleId])
  @@index([roleId])
  @@index([userId, isActive])
  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model RolePermission {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  roleId String

  permissionId String

  role Role @relation(fields: [roleId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  permission Permission @relation(fields: [permissionId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  isAllowed Boolean @default(true)

  scope DataScope @default(OWN)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([roleId, permissionId])
  @@index([permissionId])
}

model UserSession {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  userId String

  user User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  tokenHash String @unique

  expiresAt DateTime

  revokedAt DateTime?

  ipAddress String?

  userAgent String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([userId])
  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model AuditLog {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  actorUserId String?

  actor User? @relation(fields: [actorUserId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  action String

  entityType String

  entityId String?

  details Json?

  ipAddress String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([actorUserId])
  @@index([entityType, entityId])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model CandidateApplication {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  applicationNumber String @unique

  userId String @unique

  user      User   @relation("CandidateApplicant", fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  // Personal information
  firstName String

  lastName String

  fatherName String

  birthCertificateNumber String

  nationalId String @unique

  gender CandidateGender

  maritalStatus CandidateMaritalStatus

  nationality String

  birthPlace String

  dateOfBirth DateTime @db.Date

  landlinePhone String?

  mobilePhone String

  email String?

  address String

  militaryStatus    CandidateMilitaryStatus?
  // Other initial application information
  currentlyEmployed Boolean                  @default(false)

  currentEmployer String?

  hasExternalCommitment Boolean @default(false)

  hasInsuranceHistory Boolean @default(false)

  minimumEmploymentPeriod String?

  childrenCount Int @default(0)

  requestedSalary Decimal? @db.Decimal(18, 2)

  additionalNotes String?
  // Stage 2 information
  insuranceNumber String?

  bankSinaIban        String?
  // Applicant declaration
  declarationAccepted Boolean @default(false)

  declarationTextVersion String?

  declarationAcceptedAt DateTime?

  declarationIpAddress String?
  // Initial application workflow
  initialStatus        InitialApplicationStatus @default(DRAFT)

  initialVersion Int @default(1)

  initialSubmittedAt DateTime?

  initialApprovedAt DateTime?
  // Supporting documents workflow
  documentsStatus   DocumentsApplicationStatus @default(LOCKED)

  documentsVersion Int @default(1)

  documentsSubmittedAt DateTime?

  documentsApprovedAt DateTime?
  // Related records
  educationRecords    CandidateEducation[]

  workExperiences CandidateWorkExperience[]

  skills CandidateSkill[]

  references CandidateReference[]

  emergencyContact CandidateEmergencyContact?

  sensitiveInfo CandidateSensitiveInfo?

  familyMembers CandidateFamilyMember[]

  documents CandidateDocument[]

  approvals ApplicationApproval[]

  employeeProfile EmployeeProfile?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([initialStatus, createdAt])
  @@index([documentsStatus, createdAt])
}

  @@index([applicationId, stage, applicationVersion])
}

model EmployeeAssignment {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  userId String

  organizationUnitId String
  // Optional official position. Use responsibilityTitle for a custom responsibility.
  positionId         String?

  responsibilityTitle String?

  locationId String?

  managerAssignmentId String?

  user User @relation("EmployeeAssignments", fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  organizationUnit OrganizationUnit @relation(fields: [organizationUnitId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  position OrganizationPosition? @relation(fields: [positionId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  location OrganizationLocation? @relation(fields: [locationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  managerAssignment EmployeeAssignment? @relation("AssignmentManager", fields: [managerAssignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  subordinateAssignments EmployeeAssignment[] @relation("AssignmentManager")

  status EmployeeAssignmentStatus @default(ACTIVE)

  assignmentType EmployeeAssignmentType @default(PRIMARY)

  isPrimary Boolean @default(true)

  startDate DateTime

  endDate DateTime?

  notes String?

  history OrganizationAssignmentHistory[]

  roles EmployeeAssignmentRole[]

  permissions EmployeeAssignmentPermission[]

  shiftAssignments ShiftAssignment[]

  outgoingRelationships OrganizationRelationship[] @relation("OrganizationRelationshipSource")

  incomingRelationships OrganizationRelationship[] @relation("OrganizationRelationshipTarget")

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  attendanceRecords    AttendanceRecord[]
  attendanceChallenges AttendanceChallenge[]

  @@index([userId, status])
  @@index([assignmentType, status])
  @@index([organizationUnitId, status])
  @@index([positionId])
  @@index([responsibilityTitle])
  @@index([managerAssignmentId])
  @@index([locationId])
}

model EmployeeAssignmentRole {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  assignmentId String

  roleId String

  expiresAt DateTime?

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  assignment EmployeeAssignment @relation(fields: [assignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  role Role @relation(fields: [roleId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([assignmentId, roleId])
  @@index([roleId, isActive])
  @@index([assignmentId, isActive])
  @@index([expiresAt])
}

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([assignmentId, permissionId])
  @@index([permissionId, isActive])
  @@index([assignmentId, isActive])
  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model OrganizationRelationship {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  sourceAssignmentId String

  targetAssignmentId String

  relationshipType OrganizationRelationshipType

  status OrganizationRelationshipStatus @default(ACTIVE)

  endDate DateTime?

  targetAssignment EmployeeAssignment @relation("OrganizationRelationshipTarget", fields: [targetAssignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([sourceAssignmentId, targetAssignmentId, relationshipType])
  @@index([sourceAssignmentId, status])
  @@index([targetAssignmentId, status])
  @@index([relationshipType, status])
  @@index([startDate, endDate])
}

// ==========================================
// WORK SHIFTS AND IRANIAN HOLIDAY CALENDAR
// ==========================================
model WorkShift {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  code String

  name String

  description String?
  // Create a new version instead of overwriting a shift used by historical records.
  version     Int     @default(1)

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  isDefault Boolean @default(false)

  days WorkShiftDay[]

  assignments      ShiftAssignment[]
  scheduleSegments EmployeeShiftScheduleSegment[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([code, version])
  @@index([isActive])
  @@index([isDefault, isActive])
}

model WorkShiftDay {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  shiftId String

  shift     WorkShift @relation(fields: [shiftId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  // Saturday = 0 ... Friday = 6. Multiple segments per day are supported.
  dayOfWeek Int

  segmentOrder Int @default(1)

  dayType WorkShiftDayType @default(NORMAL)

  isWorkingDay Boolean @default(true)
  // Minutes after midnight in the shift's local timezone.
  // endMinute may be less than startMinute for an overnight shift.
  startMinute  Int?

  endMinute       Int?
  // Net paid minutes; the default 480 represents the agreed 7:00-15:00 shift.
  requiredMinutes Int  @default(480)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([shiftId, dayOfWeek, dayType, segmentOrder])
  @@index([shiftId, dayOfWeek, dayType])
}

model ShiftAssignment {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  shiftId String

  shift WorkShift @relation(fields: [shiftId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  targetType ShiftAssignmentTarget

  organizationUnitId String?

  organizationUnit OrganizationUnit? @relation(fields: [organizationUnitId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  employeeAssignmentId String?

  employeeAssignment EmployeeAssignment? @relation(fields: [employeeAssignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  validFrom DateTime @db.Date

  validTo DateTime? @db.Date

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  assignedById String?

  notes String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  attendanceRecords AttendanceRecord[]

  @@index([shiftId, isActive])
  @@index([organizationUnitId, isActive])
  @@index([employeeAssignmentId, isActive])
  @@index([targetType, isActive])
  @@index([validFrom, validTo])
}

model HolidayCalendar {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  jalaliYear Int @unique

  name String

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  days HolidayCalendarDay[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([isActive])
}

model HolidayCalendarDay {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  calendarId String

  calendar HolidayCalendar @relation(fields: [calendarId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  // Gregorian date is used for reliable database filtering; Jalali date is retained for display.
  date     DateTime        @db.Date

  jalaliDate String

  title String

  holidayType HolidayType

  isHoliday            Boolean  @default(true)
  // null: follow weekly shift; true: working day; false: non-working day.
  isWorkingDayOverride Boolean?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([calendarId, date])
  @@index([date])
  @@index([jalaliDate])
}

model EmployeeProfile {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  userId String @unique

  user User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  candidateApplicationId String @unique

  candidateApplication CandidateApplication @relation(fields: [candidateApplicationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  personnelCode String? @unique

  employmentStatus EmployeeEmploymentStatus @default(ACTIVE)

  hireDate DateTime @default(now()) @db.Date

  terminationDate DateTime? @db.Date

  terminationReason String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([employmentStatus, hireDate])
}

enum AttendanceRecordStatus {
  OPEN
  COMPLETED
  PENDING_REVIEW
  APPROVED
  REJECTED
}

enum AttendanceEventType {
  CHECK_IN
  CHECK_OUT
  BREAK_START
  BREAK_END
  AUTO_CHECK_OUT
}

enum AttendanceVerificationType {
  GPS
  FACE
  DEVICE
  LIVENESS
}

enum AttendanceVerificationStatus {
  PENDING
  PASSED
  FAILED
  REVIEW_REQUIRED
}

  PENDING
  APPROVED
  REJECTED
  CANCELLED
}

  CHECK_IN
  CHECK_OUT
}
