  OPEN
  CONSUMED
  FAILED
  EXPIRED
}

// ==========================================
// ATTENDANCE RECORDS AND VERIFICATION
// ==========================================

model AttendanceRecord {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  employeeAssignmentId String
  employeeAssignment   EmployeeAssignment @relation(fields: [employeeAssignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  shiftAssignmentId String?
  shiftAssignment   ShiftAssignment? @relation(fields: [shiftAssignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  checkpointLocationId String?
  checkpointLocation   OrganizationLocation? @relation(fields: [checkpointLocationId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  // Local business date. The application should use the location timezone when creating this value.
  workDate DateTime @db.Date
  timeZone String   @default("Asia/Tehran")

  // Schedule snapshots preserve the original schedule if a shift is later changed.
  scheduledStartAt DateTime?
  scheduledEndAt   DateTime?
  requiredMinutes  Int       @default(480)

  firstCheckInAt DateTime?
  lastCheckOutAt DateTime?

  workedMinutes     Int @default(0)
  breakMinutes      Int @default(0)
  overtimeMinutes   Int @default(0)
  lateMinutes       Int @default(0)
  earlyLeaveMinutes Int @default(0)

  status AttendanceRecordStatus @default(OPEN)
  notes  String?

  approvedByUserId String?
  approvedBy       User?     @relation("AttendanceRecordApprover", fields: [approvedByUserId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  approvedAt       DateTime?

  events      AttendanceEvent[]
  adjustments AttendanceAdjustment[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([employeeAssignmentId, workDate])
  @@index([employeeAssignmentId, workDate])
  @@index([workDate, status])
  @@index([shiftAssignmentId])
  @@index([checkpointLocationId])
  @@index([approvedByUserId])
}

model AttendanceEvent {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  attendanceRecordId String
  attendanceRecord   AttendanceRecord @relation(fields: [attendanceRecordId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  eventType  AttendanceEventType
  occurredAt DateTime
  receivedAt DateTime            @default(now())

  // GPS coordinates and device evidence supplied by the attendance client.
  latitude                     Decimal? @db.Decimal(10, 7)
  longitude                    Decimal? @db.Decimal(10, 7)
  accuracyMeters               Decimal? @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  distanceFromCheckpointMeters Decimal? @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  geofencePassed               Boolean?

  source            String  @default("MOBILE")
  deviceFingerprint String?
  ipAddress         String?
  userAgent         String?
  photoStorageKey   String?
  idempotencyKey    String? @unique
  notes             String?

  verifications AttendanceVerification[]
  adjustments   AttendanceAdjustment[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([attendanceRecordId, occurredAt])
  @@index([eventType, occurredAt])
  @@index([geofencePassed])
}

model AttendanceVerification {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  attendanceEventId String
  attendanceEvent   AttendanceEvent @relation(fields: [attendanceEventId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  verificationType AttendanceVerificationType
  status           AttendanceVerificationStatus @default(PENDING)

  score      Decimal?  @db.Decimal(7, 4)
  provider   String?
  details    Json?
  verifiedAt DateTime?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([attendanceEventId, verificationType])
  @@index([status, createdAt])
}

model AttendanceAdjustment {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  attendanceRecordId String
  attendanceRecord   AttendanceRecord @relation(fields: [attendanceRecordId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  attendanceEventId String?
  attendanceEvent   AttendanceEvent? @relation(fields: [attendanceEventId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  requestedByUserId String
  requestedBy       User   @relation("AttendanceAdjustmentRequester", fields: [requestedByUserId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  reviewedByUserId String?
  reviewedBy       User?   @relation("AttendanceAdjustmentReviewer", fields: [reviewedByUserId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  proposedEventType  AttendanceEventType?
  proposedOccurredAt DateTime?
  reason             String

  status          AttendanceAdjustmentStatus @default(PENDING)
  reviewerComment String?
  reviewedAt      DateTime?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([attendanceRecordId, status])
  @@index([attendanceEventId])
  @@index([requestedByUserId, createdAt])
  @@index([reviewedByUserId, reviewedAt])
}

model AttendanceChallenge {
  id                   String                    @id @default(cuid())
  userId               String
  user                 User                      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  employeeAssignmentId String
  employeeAssignment   EmployeeAssignment        @relation(fields: [employeeAssignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  action               AttendanceChallengeAction
  nonceHash            String                    @unique
  status               AttendanceChallengeStatus @default(OPEN)
  expiresAt            DateTime
  consumedAt           DateTime?
  failedAt             DateTime?
  failureReason        String?
  ipAddress            String?
  userAgentHash        String?
  createdAt            DateTime                  @default(now())
  updatedAt            DateTime                  @updatedAt

  @@index([userId, status, expiresAt])
  @@index([employeeAssignmentId, createdAt])
}

model FaceIdentityProfile {
  id                      String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId                  String    @unique
  user                    User      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  // AES-256-GCM encrypted reference image. Live frames are never stored by the ERP.
  referenceImageEncrypted Bytes
  referenceImageIv        Bytes
  referenceImageAuthTag   Bytes
  referenceImageMimeType  String
  encryptionKeyVersion    String    @default("v1")
  consentAcceptedAt       DateTime?
  consentVersion          String?
  enrolledByUserId        String?
  isActive                Boolean   @default(true)
  enrolledAt              DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt               DateTime  @updatedAt

  @@index([isActive, enrolledAt])
}

// ==========================================
// WEEKLY EMPLOYEE SHIFT PLANNING
// A schedule is per person (User), not per organizational assignment.
// DateTime values are instants; display in Asia/Tehran.
// ==========================================
enum EmployeeShiftScheduleStatus {
  DRAFT
  SUBMITTED
  APPROVED
  REJECTED
  SUPERSEDED
}

model EmployeeShiftSchedule {
  id                String                          @id @default(cuid())
  employeeUserId    String
  employee          User                            @relation("ShiftScheduleEmployee", fields: [employeeUserId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  // Week start is Saturday, represented as a Gregorian calendar date.
  weekStartDate     DateTime                        @db.Date
  version           Int                             @default(1)
  status            EmployeeShiftScheduleStatus     @default(DRAFT)
  createdByUserId   String
  createdBy         User                            @relation("ShiftScheduleCreator", fields: [createdByUserId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  submittedByUserId String?
  submittedBy       User?                           @relation("ShiftScheduleSubmitter", fields: [submittedByUserId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  submittedAt       DateTime?
  reviewedByUserId  String?
  reviewedBy        User?                           @relation("ShiftScheduleReviewer", fields: [reviewedByUserId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  reviewedAt        DateTime?
  reviewComment     String?
  publishedAt       DateTime?
  notes             String?
  segments          EmployeeShiftScheduleSegment[]
  revisions         EmployeeShiftScheduleRevision[]
  createdAt         DateTime                        @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime                        @updatedAt

  @@unique([employeeUserId, weekStartDate, version])
  @@index([employeeUserId, weekStartDate, status])
  @@index([status, weekStartDate])
  @@index([createdByUserId, createdAt])
}

model EmployeeShiftScheduleSegment {
  id              String                @id @default(cuid())
  scheduleId      String
  schedule        EmployeeShiftSchedule @relation(fields: [scheduleId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  workDate        DateTime              @db.Date
  segmentOrder    Int
  // Both timestamps include the real calendar day; overnight shifts are supported.
  startsAt        DateTime
  endsAt          DateTime
  requiredMinutes Int
  // Optional template used as a starting point, never as the sole source of truth.
  workShiftId     String?
  workShift       WorkShift?            @relation(fields: [workShiftId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  notes           String?
  createdAt       DateTime              @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime              @updatedAt

  @@unique([scheduleId, workDate, segmentOrder])
  @@index([workDate, startsAt])
  @@index([workShiftId])
}

model EmployeeShiftScheduleRevision {
  id          String                @id @default(cuid())
  scheduleId  String
  schedule    EmployeeShiftSchedule @relation(fields: [scheduleId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  actorUserId String
  actor       User                  @relation("ShiftScheduleRevisionActor", fields: [actorUserId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  action      String
  beforeData  Json?
  afterData   Json?
  comment     String?
  createdAt   DateTime              @default(now())

  @@index([scheduleId, createdAt])
  @@index([actorUserId, createdAt])
}


========== FILE: .\modules\attendance\attendance-calculator.ts ==========

export type AttendancePunchType =
  | "CHECK_IN"
  | "CHECK_OUT"
  | "BREAK_START"
  | "BREAK_END"
  | "AUTO_CHECK_OUT";

export interface AttendancePunch {
  type: AttendancePunchType;
  occurredAt: Date | string;
}

export interface AttendanceCalculationInput {
  punches: AttendancePunch[];
  requiredMinutes: number;
  scheduledStartAt?: Date | string | null;
  scheduledEndAt?: Date | string | null;
}

export interface AttendanceCalculationResult {
  firstCheckInAt: Date | null;
  lastCheckOutAt: Date | null;
  workedMinutes: number;
  breakMinutes: number;
  requiredMinutes: number;
  deficitMinutes: number;
  overtimeMinutes: number;
  lateMinutes: number;
  earlyLeaveMinutes: number;
  isComplete: boolean;
  requiresReview: boolean;
  warnings: string[];
}

function toDate(value: Date | string): Date {
  const date = value instanceof Date
    ? new Date(value.getTime())
    : new Date(value);

  if (!Number.isFinite(date.getTime())) {
    throw new Error("تاریخ یا ساعت تردد معتبر نیست.");
  }

  return date;
}

function minutesBetween(start: Date, end: Date): number {
  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.floor((end.getTime() - start.getTime()) / 60000)
  );
}

export function calculateAttendance(
  input: AttendanceCalculationInput
): AttendanceCalculationResult {
  if (
    !Number.isInteger(input.requiredMinutes) ||
    input.requiredMinutes < 0 ||
    input.requiredMinutes > 1440
  ) {
    throw new Error("دقایق موظفی معتبر نیست.");
  }

  const scheduledStart = input.scheduledStartAt
    ? toDate(input.scheduledStartAt)
    : null;

  const scheduledEnd = input.scheduledEndAt
    ? toDate(input.scheduledEndAt)
    : null;

  if (
    scheduledStart &&
    scheduledEnd &&
    scheduledEnd <= scheduledStart
  ) {
    throw new Error("پایان شیفت باید بعد از شروع آن باشد.");
  }

  const punches = input.punches
    .map((punch, index) => ({
      type: punch.type,
      occurredAt: toDate(punch.occurredAt),
      index,
    }))
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        a.occurredAt.getTime() - b.occurredAt.getTime() ||
        a.index - b.index
    );

  const warnings: string[] = [];

  let firstCheckInAt: Date | null = null;
  let lastCheckOutAt: Date | null = null;

  let workStart: Date | null = null;
  let breakStart: Date | null = null;

  let workedMilliseconds = 0;
  let breakMilliseconds = 0;

  let requiresReview = false;

  for (const punch of punches) {
    const time = punch.occurredAt;

    switch (punch.type) {
      case "CHECK_IN": {
        if (workStart !== null) {
          warnings.push("ورود تکراری بدون خروج قبلی.");
          requiresReview = true;
          break;
        }

        firstCheckInAt ??= time;
        workStart = time;
        breakStart = null;
        break;
      }

      case "BREAK_START": {
        if (workStart === null || breakStart !== null) {
          warnings.push("شروع استراحت در وضعیت نامعتبر.");
          requiresReview = true;
          break;
        }

        workedMilliseconds +=
          time.getTime() - workStart.getTime();

        workStart = null;
        breakStart = time;
        break;
      }

      case "BREAK_END": {
        if (breakStart === null) {
          warnings.push("پایان استراحت بدون شروع معتبر.");
          requiresReview = true;
          break;
        }

        breakMilliseconds +=
          time.getTime() - breakStart.getTime();

        breakStart = null;
        workStart = time;
        break;
      }

      case "CHECK_OUT":
      case "AUTO_CHECK_OUT": {
        if (breakStart !== null) {
          warnings.push(
            "خروج هنگام استراحت؛ زمان استراحت بسته شد."
          );

          breakMilliseconds +=
            time.getTime() - breakStart.getTime();

          breakStart = null;
          lastCheckOutAt = time;
          requiresReview = true;
          break;
        }

        if (workStart === null) {
          warnings.push("خروج بدون ورود معتبر.");
          requiresReview = true;
          break;
        }

        workedMilliseconds +=
          time.getTime() - workStart.getTime();

        workStart = null;
        lastCheckOutAt = time;

        if (punch.type === "AUTO_CHECK_OUT") {
          warnings.push("خروج خودکار نیازمند بررسی است.");
          requiresReview = true;
        }

        break;
      }

      default: {
        warnings.push("نوع رویداد ناشناخته است.");
        requiresReview = true;
      }
    }
  }

  const isComplete =
    workStart === null &&
    breakStart === null &&
    firstCheckInAt !== null &&
    lastCheckOutAt !== null;

  if (workStart !== null) {
    warnings.push("ورود باز بدون خروج نهایی.");
    requiresReview = true;
  }

  if (breakStart !== null) {
    warnings.push("استراحت باز بدون پایان.");
    requiresReview = true;
  }

  const workedMinutes = Math.floor(
    workedMilliseconds / 60000
  );

  const breakMinutes = Math.floor(
    breakMilliseconds / 60000
  );

  const requiredMinutes = input.requiredMinutes;

  const deficitMinutes = Math.max(
    requiredMinutes - workedMinutes,
    0
  );

  const overtimeMinutes = isComplete
    ? Math.max(workedMinutes - requiredMinutes, 0)
    : 0;

  const lateMinutes =
    scheduledStart && firstCheckInAt
      ? minutesBetween(scheduledStart, firstCheckInAt)
      : 0;

  const earlyLeaveMinutes =
    scheduledEnd && lastCheckOutAt
      ? minutesBetween(lastCheckOutAt, scheduledEnd)
      : 0;

  return {
    firstCheckInAt,
    lastCheckOutAt,
    workedMinutes,
    breakMinutes,
    requiredMinutes,
    deficitMinutes,
    overtimeMinutes,
    lateMinutes,
    earlyLeaveMinutes,
    isComplete,
    requiresReview: requiresReview || !isComplete,
    warnings,
  };
}


========== FILE: .\modules\attendance\attendance-punch-policy.ts ==========
/** Pure attendance punch policy. No Prisma, database, GPS or camera dependencies. */
export type PunchAction = "CHECK_IN" | "CHECK_OUT";
export type PunchEventType =
  | "CHECK_IN" | "CHECK_OUT" | "BREAK_START" | "BREAK_END" | "AUTO_CHECK_OUT";

export interface PunchRecordState {
  id: string;
  workDate: Date;
  status: string;
  approvedAt: Date | null;
  approvedByUserId: string | null;
  lastEventType: PunchEventType | null;
}

export type PunchDecision =
  | { allowed: true; recordId: string | null; createNew: boolean }
  | { allowed: false; code: string; message: string };

function deny(code: string, message: string): PunchDecision {
  return { allowed: false, code, message };
}

function isOpen(type: PunchEventType | null): boolean {
  return type === "CHECK_IN" || type === "BREAK_START" || type === "BREAK_END";
}

/** All records must belong to the same employee; caller must enforce authorization. */
export function decideAttendancePunch(input: {
  action: PunchAction;
  today: Date;
  records: readonly PunchRecordState[];
}): PunchDecision {
  const { action, today, records } = input;
  const open = records.filter((r) => isOpen(r.lastEventType));
  if (open.length > 1) return deny("MULTIPLE_OPEN", "چند رکورد حضور باز وجود دارد و بررسی مسئول حضور و غیاب لازم است.");

  const todayRecords = records.filter((r) => r.workDate.getTime() === today.getTime());
  if (action === "CHECK_IN" && todayRecords.length > 1) {
    return deny("DUPLICATE_DAY", "چند رکورد برای یک کارمند در یک روز وجود دارد.");
  }
  if (action === "CHECK_IN" && open.length) {
    return deny("ALREADY_OPEN", "ورود قبلی هنوز بسته نشده است.");
  }
  if (action === "CHECK_OUT" && !open.length) {
    return deny("NO_OPEN_SESSION", "برای خروج، ورود باز معتبری یافت نشد.");
  }
  const target = action === "CHECK_OUT" ? open[0] : todayRecords[0];
  if (action === "CHECK_OUT" && target?.lastEventType === "BREAK_START") {
    return deny("BREAK_OPEN", "ابتدا باید استراحت باز پایان یابد.");
  }
  if (!target) return { allowed: true, recordId: null, createNew: true };
  if (target.approvedAt !== null || target.approvedByUserId !== null ||
      (target.status !== "OPEN" && target.status !== "COMPLETED")) {
    return deny("RECORD_LOCKED", "رکورد تأیید یا قفل شده است.");
  }
  if (action === "CHECK_IN" && target.lastEventType !== null &&
      target.lastEventType !== "CHECK_OUT" && target.lastEventType !== "AUTO_CHECK_OUT") {
    return deny("INVALID_CHECK_IN", "ورود قبلی هنوز بسته نشده است.");
  }
  if (action === "CHECK_OUT" && target.lastEventType !== "CHECK_IN" &&
      target.lastEventType !== "BREAK_END") {
    return deny("INVALID_CHECK_OUT", "برای خروج، ورود باز معتبر لازم است.");
  }
  return { allowed: true, recordId: target.id, createNew: false };
}


========== FILE: .\modules\attendance\attendance-review-policy.ts ==========
/** Pure HR attendance review rules. No database or framework dependencies. */
export type AttendanceReviewStatus =
  | "OPEN" | "COMPLETED" | "PENDING_REVIEW" | "APPROVED" | "REJECTED";
export type AdjustmentReviewStatus = "PENDING" | "APPROVED" | "REJECTED" | "CANCELLED";

export interface AttendanceReviewRecord {
  id: string;
  status: AttendanceReviewStatus;
  approvedAt: Date | null;
  approvedByUserId: string | null;
  requiredMinutes: number;
  workedMinutes: number;
  overtimeMinutes: number;
  lateMinutes: number;
  earlyLeaveMinutes: number;
  firstCheckInAt: Date | null;
  lastCheckOutAt: Date | null;
  requiresReview: boolean;
  adjustments: readonly { status: AdjustmentReviewStatus }[];
}

export type AttendanceReviewDecision =
  | { allowed: true; nextStatus: "APPROVED" | "PENDING_REVIEW" | "REJECTED"; approvedOvertimeMinutes: number; deficitMinutes: number }
  | { allowed: false; code: string; message: string };

function deny(code: string, message: string): AttendanceReviewDecision {
  return { allowed: false, code, message };
}
function validMinutes(n: number): boolean {
  return Number.isSafeInteger(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 1440;
}

/**
 * Pure policy only: caller MUST enforce HR authorization, ownership/scope, audit logging,
 * and atomic database writes. APPROVED records must never be overwritten.
 *
 * approvedOvertimeMinutes is a proposed output, NOT persisted by this module.
 * Existing AttendanceRecord has overtimeMinutes but no distinct approved-overtime field.
 */
export function decideAttendanceReview(input: {
  record: AttendanceReviewRecord;
  action: "APPROVE" | "SEND_TO_REVIEW" | "REJECT";
  approvedOvertimeMinutes?: number;
  reason?: string;
}): AttendanceReviewDecision {
  const { record, action } = input;
  if (record.status === "APPROVED" || record.approvedAt !== null || record.approvedByUserId !== null) {
    return deny("ALREADY_APPROVED", "رکورد قبلاً تأیید شده و قفل است.");
  }
  if (![record.requiredMinutes, record.workedMinutes, record.overtimeMinutes,
    record.lateMinutes, record.earlyLeaveMinutes].every(validMinutes)) {
    return deny("INVALID_MINUTES", "مقادیر زمانی رکورد معتبر نیست.");
  }
  if (record.adjustments.some((a) => a.status === "PENDING")) {
    return deny("PENDING_ADJUSTMENT", "ابتدا باید درخواست‌های اصلاح تردد تعیین تکلیف شوند.");
  }
  const deficitMinutes = Math.max(0, record.requiredMinutes - record.workedMinutes);
  if (action === "SEND_TO_REVIEW") {
    if (record.status === "REJECTED") return deny("INVALID_STATE", "رکورد ردشده باید از مسیر اصلاح مجاز بازگشایی شود.");
    return { allowed: true, nextStatus: "PENDING_REVIEW", approvedOvertimeMinutes: 0, deficitMinutes };
  }
  if (action === "REJECT") {
    if (record.status === "REJECTED") return deny("INVALID_STATE", "رکورد قبلاً رد شده است.");
    if (!input.reason?.trim() || input.reason.trim().length < 5) {
      return deny("REASON_REQUIRED", "برای رد رکورد، توضیح حداقل پنج‌حرفی لازم است.");
    }
    return { allowed: true, nextStatus: "REJECTED", approvedOvertimeMinutes: 0, deficitMinutes };
  }
  if (record.status !== "COMPLETED" && record.status !== "PENDING_REVIEW") {
    return deny("NOT_READY", "رکورد برای تأیید نهایی آماده نیست.");
  }
  if (!record.firstCheckInAt || !record.lastCheckOutAt ||
      record.lastCheckOutAt.getTime() <= record.firstCheckInAt.getTime()) {
    return deny("INCOMPLETE_ATTENDANCE", "ورود و خروج کامل و معتبر ثبت نشده است.");
  }
  if (record.requiresReview) {
    return deny("REQUIRES_REVIEW", "ابهام‌های محاسباتی باید قبل از تأیید رفع شوند.");
  }
  const approvedOvertimeMinutes = input.approvedOvertimeMinutes ?? 0;
  if (!validMinutes(approvedOvertimeMinutes) || approvedOvertimeMinutes > record.overtimeMinutes) {
    return deny("INVALID_OVERTIME", "اضافه‌کاری تأییدی باید بین صفر و اضافه‌کاری پیشنهادی باشد.");
  }
  return { allowed: true, nextStatus: "APPROVED", approvedOvertimeMinutes, deficitMinutes };
}


========== FILE: .\modules\attendance\attendance-hr-edit-window.ts ==========
/**
 * Sahand ERP — HR attendance correction deadline.
 * Dates are civil work dates stored as UTC midnight (@db.Date).
 * The correction window ends at the end of the matching Persian-calendar
 * day in the following Persian month, using Asia/Tehran local time.
 * No database or third-party date library is required.
 */

const TEHRAN_TIME_ZONE = "Asia/Tehran";
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

const persianDateFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US-u-ca-persian-nu-latn", {
  timeZone: "UTC",
  year: "numeric",
  month: "numeric",
  day: "numeric",
});

const tehranDateFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US-u-ca-gregory-nu-latn", {
  timeZone: TEHRAN_TIME_ZONE,
  year: "numeric",
  month: "numeric",
  day: "numeric",
});

export interface CivilDate {
  year: number;
  month: number;
  day: number;
}

export interface HrAttendanceEditWindow {
  allowed: boolean;
  reason: "ALLOWED" | "WINDOW_EXPIRED" | "FUTURE_WORK_DATE" | "PAYROLL_CLOSED";
  workDatePersian: CivilDate;
  lastEditableDatePersian: CivilDate;
  todayTehran: CivilDate;
}

function extractDate(formatter: Intl.DateTimeFormat, date: Date): CivilDate {
  const parts = formatter.formatToParts(date);
  const numberPart = (type: "year" | "month" | "day") => {
    const value = Number(parts.find((part) => part.type === type)?.value);
    if (!Number.isInteger(value)) throw new Error(`Invalid ${type} in calendar date`);
    return value;
  };
  return { year: numberPart("year"), month: numberPart("month"), day: numberPart("day") };
}

function compareDates(a: CivilDate, b: CivilDate): number {
  return a.year - b.year || a.month - b.month || a.day - b.day;
}

function parseWorkDate(workDate: Date | string): Date {
  if (typeof workDate === "string" && !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(workDate)) {
    throw new Error("workDate must be YYYY-MM-DD or a Date");
  }
  const date = typeof workDate === "string" ? new Date(`${workDate}T00:00:00.000Z`) : workDate;
  if (!(date instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) throw new Error("Invalid workDate");
  if (date.getUTCHours() !== 0 || date.getUTCMinutes() !== 0 || date.getUTCSeconds() !== 0 || date.getUTCMilliseconds() !== 0) {
    throw new Error("workDate must be a date-only value (UTC midnight)");
  }
  if (typeof workDate === "string" && date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== workDate) {
    throw new Error("Invalid Gregorian workDate");
  }
  return date;
}

function nextPersianMonth(date: CivilDate): Pick<CivilDate, "year" | "month"> {
  return date.month === 12 ? { year: date.year + 1, month: 1 } : { year: date.year, month: date.month + 1 };
}

/**
 * Returns the Persian date of the final editable day (inclusive).
 * For example, 31 Farvardin -> 31 Ordibehesht, and 31 Shahrivar -> 30 Mehr.
 */
export function getLastHrEditablePersianDate(workDate: Date | string): CivilDate {
  const base = parseWorkDate(workDate);
  const original = extractDate(persianDateFormatter, base);
  const target = nextPersianMonth(original);

  // The next Persian month begins 29–31 days after the first day of this month.
  // Searching 65 Gregorian days covers all supported month transitions, including leap years.
  let firstTargetDay: Date | null = null;
  for (let offset = 1; offset <= 65; offset++) {
    const candidate = new Date(base.getTime() + offset * DAY_MS);
    const p = extractDate(persianDateFormatter, candidate);
    if (p.year === target.year && p.month === target.month) {
      firstTargetDay = candidate;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!firstTargetDay) throw new Error("Could not determine next Persian month");

  let lastDay = 0;
  for (let offset = 0; offset < 32; offset++) {
    const p = extractDate(persianDateFormatter, new Date(firstTargetDay.getTime() + offset * DAY_MS));
    if (p.year !== target.year || p.month !== target.month) break;
    lastDay = p.day;
  }
  if (lastDay === 0) throw new Error("Could not determine Persian month length");
  return { ...target, day: Math.min(original.day, lastDay) };
}

/**
 * Checks the civil day in Tehran, not a 24-hour duration.
 * payrollClosed must be supplied from the payroll workflow by the caller.
 */
export function evaluateHrAttendanceEditWindow(input: {
  workDate: Date | string;
  now?: Date;
  payrollClosed?: boolean;
}): HrAttendanceEditWindow {
  const workDate = parseWorkDate(input.workDate);
  const now = input.now ?? new Date();
  if (!(now instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(now.getTime())) throw new Error("Invalid now");

  const workDatePersian = extractDate(persianDateFormatter, workDate);
  const lastEditableDatePersian = getLastHrEditablePersianDate(workDate);
  const todayGregorian = extractDate(tehranDateFormatter, now);
  // Interpret Tehran's civil Gregorian date as a date-only value for Persian conversion.
  const todayAsUtcDate = new Date(Date.UTC(todayGregorian.year, todayGregorian.month - 1, todayGregorian.day));
  const todayTehran = extractDate(persianDateFormatter, todayAsUtcDate);

  let reason: HrAttendanceEditWindow["reason"] = "ALLOWED";
  if (input.payrollClosed) reason = "PAYROLL_CLOSED";
  else if (compareDates(todayTehran, workDatePersian) < 0) reason = "FUTURE_WORK_DATE";
  else if (compareDates(todayTehran, lastEditableDatePersian) > 0) reason = "WINDOW_EXPIRED";

  return { allowed: reason === "ALLOWED", reason, workDatePersian, lastEditableDatePersian, todayTehran };
}


========== FILE: .\modules\attendance\attendance-offline-queue.ts ==========
/** Pure, platform-independent offline attendance queue policy. No biometric data. */
export type OfflinePunchKind = "CHECK_IN" | "CHECK_OUT";
export type OfflinePunchStatus = "PENDING" | "SYNCED" | "REVIEW_REQUIRED";
export interface OfflinePunch {
  id: string;
  deviceId: string;
  employeeId: string;
  kind: OfflinePunchKind;
  occurredAtDevice: string;
  capturedMonotonicMs: number;
  clockOffsetMs: number | null;
  createdAtDevice: string;
  status: OfflinePunchStatus;
  attempts: number;
  serverReceiptId: string | null;
  reviewReason: string | null;
}
export interface NewOfflinePunch {
  id: string;
  deviceId: string;
  employeeId: string;
  kind: OfflinePunchKind;
  occurredAtDevice: string;
  capturedMonotonicMs: number;
  clockOffsetMs?: number | null;
}
export type SyncResponse =
  | { outcome: "ACCEPTED" | "ALREADY_EXISTS"; receiptId: string }
  | { outcome: "REVIEW_REQUIRED"; reason: string }
  | { outcome: "RETRY" };

function validDate(value: string): boolean {
  return typeof value === "string" && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)) && /(?:Z|[+-]\d\d:\d\d)$/.test(value);
}
function validId(value: string): boolean { return typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0 && value.length <= 128; }
export function createOfflinePunch(input: NewOfflinePunch): OfflinePunch {
  if (![input.id, input.deviceId, input.employeeId].every(validId)) throw new Error("INVALID_IDENTIFIER");
  if (input.kind !== "CHECK_IN" && input.kind !== "CHECK_OUT") throw new Error("INVALID_KIND");
  if (!validDate(input.occurredAtDevice)) throw new Error("INVALID_TIME");
  if (!Number.isFinite(input.capturedMonotonicMs) || input.capturedMonotonicMs < 0) throw new Error("INVALID_MONOTONIC_TIME");
  if (input.clockOffsetMs != null && !Number.isFinite(input.clockOffsetMs)) throw new Error("INVALID_CLOCK_OFFSET");
  return {
    ...input,
    clockOffsetMs: input.clockOffsetMs ?? null,
    createdAtDevice: input.occurredAtDevice,
    status: "PENDING", attempts: 0, serverReceiptId: null, reviewReason: null,
  };
}
export function enqueueOfflinePunch(queue: readonly OfflinePunch[], punch: OfflinePunch): OfflinePunch[] {
  if (queue.some(p => p.id === punch.id)) throw new Error("DUPLICATE_PUNCH_ID");
  return [...queue, punch];
}
/** Deterministic order; never group by calendar day, because shifts can cross midnight. */
export function pendingForSync(queue: readonly OfflinePunch[]): OfflinePunch[] {
  return queue.filter(p => p.status === "PENDING").sort((a,b) =>
    Date.parse(a.occurredAtDevice) - Date.parse(b.occurredAtDevice) ||
    a.capturedMonotonicMs - b.capturedMonotonicMs || a.id.localeCompare(b.id)
  );
}
export function applySyncResponse(queue: readonly OfflinePunch[], id: string, response: SyncResponse): OfflinePunch[] {
  if (!queue.some(p => p.id === id)) throw new Error("PUNCH_NOT_FOUND");
  return queue.map(p => {
    if (p.id !== id || p.status !== "PENDING") return p;
    const attempts = p.attempts + 1;
    if (response.outcome === "RETRY") return { ...p, attempts };
    if (response.outcome === "REVIEW_REQUIRED") return { ...p, attempts, status: "REVIEW_REQUIRED", reviewReason: response.reason };
    if (!validId(response.receiptId)) throw new Error("INVALID_RECEIPT");
    return { ...p, attempts, status: "SYNCED", serverReceiptId: response.receiptId };
  });
}
export function countPending(queue: readonly OfflinePunch[]): number {
  return queue.filter(p => p.status === "PENDING").length;
}


========== FILE: .\modules\attendance\attendance-utils.ts ==========
import "server-only";
import { createHash, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";

export function createChallengeNonce() {
  return randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
}
export function sha256(value: string) {
  return createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");
}
export function safeHexEqual(a: string, b: string) {
  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(a) || !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(b)) return false;
  const left = Buffer.from(a, "hex");
  const right = Buffer.from(b, "hex");
  return left.length === right.length && timingSafeEqual(left, right);
}
export function getRequestIp(request: Request) {
  return request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")?.split(",")[0]?.trim()
    || request.headers.get("x-real-ip") || null;
}
export function getWorkDate(now: Date) {
  const timeZone = process.env.ATTENDANCE_TIME_ZONE || "Asia/Tehran";
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
    timeZone, year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
  }).formatToParts(now);
  const value = (type: string) => parts.find(p => p.type === type)?.value;
  const year = Number(value("year")), month = Number(value("month")), day = Number(value("day"));
  if (!year || !month || !day) throw new Error("Unable to calculate attendance date.");
  return new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
}
export function getSaturdayBasedDayOfWeek(workDate: Date) {
  return (workDate.getUTCDay() + 1) % 7;
}
export function calculateDistanceMeters(lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number) {
  const r = 6371000, rad = (v: number) => v * Math.PI / 180;
  const dLat = rad(lat2 - lat1), dLon = rad(lon2 - lon1);
  const a = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 +
    Math.cos(rad(lat1)) * Math.cos(rad(lat2)) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) ** 2;
  return r * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
}
export function isPlainObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}
export function getApiError(error: unknown) {
  const statusCode = typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "statusCode" in error &&
    typeof (error as {statusCode?: unknown}).statusCode === "number"
      ? (error as {statusCode: number}).statusCode : 500;
  const message = error instanceof Error && statusCode < 500
    ? error.message
    : error instanceof Error && statusCode === 503
      ? error.message
      : "خطا در پردازش درخواست حضور و غیاب.";
  return { statusCode, message };
}


========== FILE: .\modules\permissions\access-control.ts ==========
import "server-only";

import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { DataScope } from "@/generated/prisma/client";

type PermissionCheck = {
  userId: string;
  permission: string;
  assignmentId?: string;
};

export type PermissionResult = {
  allowed: boolean;
  scopes: DataScope[];
  isSystemAdmin: boolean;
  activeAssignmentId: string | null;
};

export class PermissionDeniedError extends Error {
  statusCode = 403;

  constructor(
    message = "شما اجازه انجام این عملیات را ندارید."
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "PermissionDeniedError";
  }
}

const emptyResult = (): PermissionResult => ({
  allowed: false,
  scopes: [],
  isSystemAdmin: false,
  activeAssignmentId: null,
});

/**
 * بررسی مجوز در سطح کاربر و مسئولیت سازمانی
 *
 * اگر assignmentId ارسال نشود:
 * فقط مسئولیت اصلی و فعال کاربر بررسی می‌شود.
 *
 * اگر assignmentId ارسال شود:
 * مالکیت و فعال بودن آن مسئولیت بررسی می‌شود.
 */
export async function checkPermission({
  userId,
  permission,
  assignmentId,
}: PermissionCheck): Promise<PermissionResult> {
  const now = new Date();

  const user = await prisma.user.findFirst({
    where: {
      id: userId,
      isActive: true,
    },

    select: {
      id: true,

      roles: {
        where: {
          isActive: true,

          OR: [
            { expiresAt: null },
            { expiresAt: { gt: now } },
          ],

          role: {
            isActive: true,
          },
        },

        select: {
          role: {
            select: {
              key: true,
              isSystem: true,

              permissions: {
                where: {
                  permission: {
                    key: permission,
                  },
                },

                select: {
                  isAllowed: true,
                  scope: true,
                },
              },
            },
          },
        },
      },

      employeeAssignments: {
        where: {
          status: "ACTIVE",

          AND: [
            {
              OR: [
                { endDate: null },
                { endDate: { gt: now } },
              ],
            },

            assignmentId
              ? { id: assignmentId }
              : { isPrimary: true },
          ],
        },

        orderBy: [
          { isPrimary: "desc" },
          { startDate: "desc" },
        ],

        take: 1,

        select: {
          id: true,

          roles: {
            where: {
              isActive: true,

              OR: [
                { expiresAt: null },
                { expiresAt: { gt: now } },
              ],

              role: {
                isActive: true,
                isSystem: false,
              },
            },

            select: {
              role: {
                select: {
                  permissions: {
                    where: {
                      permission: {
                        key: permission,
                      },
                    },

                    select: {
                      isAllowed: true,
                      scope: true,
                    },
                  },
                },
              },
            },
          },

          permissions: {
            where: {
              isActive: true,

              OR: [
                { expiresAt: null },
                { expiresAt: { gt: now } },
              ],

              permission: {
                key: permission,
              },
            },

            select: {
              isAllowed: true,
              scope: true,
            },
          },
        },
      },
    },
  });

  if (!user) {
    return emptyResult();
  }

  const assignment =
    user.employeeAssignments[0] ?? null;

  // مسئولیت ارسالی باید متعلق به همین کاربر و فعال باشد.
  if (assignmentId && !assignment) {
    return emptyResult();
  }

  // مدیر سیستم به تمام مجوزها دسترسی دارد.
  const isSystemAdmin = user.roles.some(
    (userRole) =>
      userRole.role.isSystem &&
      userRole.role.key === "system_admin"
  );

  if (isSystemAdmin) {
    return {
      allowed: true,
      scopes: [DataScope.ALL],
      isSystemAdmin: true,
      activeAssignmentId: assignment?.id ?? null,
    };
  }

  // مجوزهای نقش‌های عمومی کاربر
  const globalGrants = user.roles.flatMap(
    (userRole) => userRole.role.permissions
  );

  // مجوزهای نقش‌های مسئولیت فعال
  const assignmentRoleGrants =
    assignment?.roles.flatMap(
      (assignmentRole) =>
        assignmentRole.role.permissions
    ) ?? [];

  // مجوزهای مستقیم مسئولیت
  const assignmentDirectGrants =
    assignment?.permissions ?? [];

  const grants = [
    ...globalGrants,
    ...assignmentRoleGrants,
    ...assignmentDirectGrants,
  ];

  // منع صریح دسترسی بر اجازه اولویت دارد.
  if (grants.some((grant) => !grant.isAllowed)) {
    return {
      allowed: false,
      scopes: [],
      isSystemAdmin: false,
      activeAssignmentId: assignment?.id ?? null,
    };
  }

  const allowedGrants = grants.filter(
    (grant) => grant.isAllowed
  );

  if (allowedGrants.length === 0) {
    return emptyResult();
  }

  const scopes = [
    ...new Set(
      allowedGrants.map((grant) => grant.scope)
    ),
  ];

  return {
    allowed: true,
    scopes,
    isSystemAdmin: false,
    activeAssignmentId: assignment?.id ?? null,
  };
}

/**
 * بررسی ساده مجوز
 */
export async function hasPermission(
  userId: string,
  permission: string,
  assignmentId?: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  const result = await checkPermission({
    userId,
    permission,
    assignmentId,
  });

  return result.allowed;
}

/**
 * بررسی اجباری مجوز
 */
export async function requirePermission(
  userId: string,
  permission: string,
  assignmentId?: string
): Promise<PermissionResult> {
  const result = await checkPermission({
    userId,
    permission,
    assignmentId,
  });

  if (!result.allowed) {
    throw new PermissionDeniedError();
  }

  return result;
}

/**
 * بررسی دسترسی به اطلاعات متعلق به کاربر
 */
export function canAccessOwnData(
  result: PermissionResult,
  ownerUserId: string,
  currentUserId: string
): boolean {
  if (!result.allowed) {
    return false;
  }

  if (result.scopes.includes(DataScope.ALL)) {
    return true;
  }

  if (result.scopes.includes(DataScope.OWN)) {
    return ownerUserId === currentUserId;
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * دریافت تمام مجوزهای فعال کاربر
 * برای استفاده در Sidebar و رابط کاربری
 */
export async function getUserPermissions(
  userId: string
): Promise<string[]> {
  const now = new Date();

  const user = await prisma.user.findFirst({
    where: {
      id: userId,
      isActive: true,
    },
    select: {
      roles: {
        where: {
          isActive: true,
          OR: [
            { expiresAt: null },
            { expiresAt: { gt: now } },
          ],
          role: {
            isActive: true,
          },
        },
        select: {
          role: {
            select: {
              key: true,
              isSystem: true,
              permissions: {
                select: {
                  isAllowed: true,
                  permission: {
                    select: {
                      key: true,
                    },
                  },
                },
              },
            },
          },
        },
      },

      employeeAssignments: {
        where: {
          status: "ACTIVE",
          isPrimary: true,
          OR: [
            { endDate: null },
            { endDate: { gt: now } },
          ],
        },
        orderBy: {
          startDate: "desc",
        },
        take: 1,
        select: {
          roles: {
            where: {
              isActive: true,
              OR: [
                { expiresAt: null },
                { expiresAt: { gt: now } },
              ],
              role: {
                isActive: true,
                isSystem: false,
              },
            },
            select: {
              role: {
                select: {
                  permissions: {
                    select: {
                      isAllowed: true,
                      permission: {
                        select: {
                          key: true,
                        },
                      },
                    },
                  },
                },
              },
            },
          },
          permissions: {
            where: {
              isActive: true,
              OR: [
                { expiresAt: null },
                { expiresAt: { gt: now } },
              ],
            },
            select: {
              isAllowed: true,
              permission: {
                select: {
                  key: true,
                },
              },
            },
          },
        },
      },
    },
  });

  if (!user) {
    return [];
  }

  // مدیر سیستم تمام مجوزها را دارد.
  const isSystemAdmin = user.roles.some(
    (item) =>
      item.role.isSystem &&
      item.role.key === "system_admin"
  );

  const allPermissions = await prisma.permission.findMany({
    select: {
      key: true,
    },
  });

  if (isSystemAdmin) {
    return allPermissions.map((item) => item.key);
  }

  const assignment = user.employeeAssignments[0];

  const globalGrants = user.roles.flatMap(
    (item) => item.role.permissions
  );

  const assignmentRoleGrants =
    assignment?.roles.flatMap(
      (item) => item.role.permissions
    ) ?? [];

  const assignmentDirectGrants =
    assignment?.permissions ?? [];

  const grants = [
    ...globalGrants,
    ...assignmentRoleGrants,
    ...assignmentDirectGrants,
  ];

  const deniedPermissions = new Set(
    grants
      .filter((item) => !item.isAllowed)
      .map((item) => item.permission.key)
  );

  const allowedPermissions = new Set(
    grants
      .filter((item) => item.isAllowed)
      .map((item) => item.permission.key)
  );

  return [...allowedPermissions].filter(
    (key) => !deniedPermissions.has(key)
  );
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\Ehsan\Desktop\sahand\app\api\attendance\aws\credentials\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { STSClient, AssumeRoleCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-sts";
import { assertSameOriginRequest, authorizeRequest } from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { readSessionToken, region } from "@/libs/attendance/aws-face";

const sts = new STSClient({ region });
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);
    const body: unknown = await request.json();
    if (!body || typeof body !== "object" || typeof (body as {token?:unknown}).token !== "string")
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "توکن لازم است." }, { status: 400 });
    const payload = readSessionToken((body as {token:string}).token);
    const challenge = await prisma.attendanceChallenge.findUnique({ where: { id: payload.cid } });
    if (!challenge) return NextResponse.json({ error: "چالش نامعتبر است." }, { status: 403 });
    const auth = await authorizeRequest(request, "attendance.attendance.create", challenge.employeeAssignmentId);
    if (challenge.userId !== auth.user.id || payload.uid !== auth.user.id || challenge.status !== "OPEN" || challenge.expiresAt <= new Date())
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "چالش نامعتبر است." }, { status: 403 });
    const roleArn = process.env.ATTENDANCE_LIVENESS_ROLE_ARN;
    if (!roleArn) throw new Error("Missing role ARN");
    const result = await sts.send(new AssumeRoleCommand({
      RoleArn: roleArn, RoleSessionName: `sahand-${payload.cid.slice(0, 24)}`,
      DurationSeconds: 900,
      Policy: JSON.stringify({ Version: "2012-10-17", Statement: [{ Effect: "Allow",
        Action: ["rekognition:StartFaceLivenessSession"], Resource: "*" }] }),
    }));
    const c = result.Credentials;
    if (!c?.AccessKeyId || !c.SecretAccessKey || !c.SessionToken) throw new Error("No STS credentials");
    return NextResponse.json({ accessKeyId: c.AccessKeyId, secretAccessKey: c.SecretAccessKey,
      sessionToken: c.SessionToken, expiration: c.Expiration?.toISOString() },
      { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[AWS_LIVENESS_CREDENTIALS]", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "صدور دسترسی موقت ممکن نیست." }, { status: 503 });
  }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\Ehsan\Desktop\sahand\app\api\attendance\aws\session\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { assertSameOriginRequest, authorizeRequest } from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";
import { issueSessionToken, startLivenessSession, region } from "@/libs/attendance/aws-face";
import { sha256 } from "@/modules/attendance/attendance-utils";

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);
    const body: unknown = await request.json();
    if (!body || typeof body !== "object") return NextResponse.json({ error: "درخواست نامعتبر است." }, { status: 400 });
    const { challengeId, nonce } = body as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (typeof challengeId !== "string" || typeof nonce !== "string")
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "شناسه و nonce لازم است." }, { status: 400 });
    const challenge = await prisma.attendanceChallenge.findUnique({ where: { id: challengeId } });
    if (!challenge) return NextResponse.json({ error: "چالش یافت نشد." }, { status: 404 });
    const auth = await authorizeRequest(request, "attendance.attendance.create", challenge.employeeAssignmentId);
    if (challenge.userId !== auth.user.id || challenge.status !== "OPEN" || challenge.expiresAt <= new Date() || challenge.nonceHash !== sha256(nonce))
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "چالش معتبر نیست." }, { status: 403 });
    const sessionId = await startLivenessSession();
    const token = issueSessionToken({ sid: sessionId, cid: challenge.id, uid: auth.user.id, exp: challenge.expiresAt.getTime() });
    return NextResponse.json({ sessionId, liveSessionToken: token, region }, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[AWS_LIVENESS_SESSION]", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "ایجاد نشست چهره ناموفق بود یا دسترسی مجاز نیست." }, { status: 503 });
  }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\Ehsan\Desktop\sahand\app\api\attendance\challenges\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { errorResponse, successResponse } from "@/libs/api-response";
import { assertSameOriginRequest, authorizeRequest, RequestOriginError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";
import { AuthError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.service";
import { PermissionDeniedError } from "@/modules/permissions/access-control";
import { createChallengeNonce, getApiError, getRequestIp, isPlainObject, sha256 } from "@/modules/attendance/attendance-utils";

class ChallengeError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string, public statusCode = 400) { super(message); }
}
function handleError(error: unknown) {
  if (error instanceof ChallengeError || error instanceof RequestOriginError ||
      error instanceof AuthError || error instanceof PermissionDeniedError) {
    return errorResponse(error.message, error.statusCode);
  }
  const parsed = getApiError(error);
  console.error("[ATTENDANCE_CHALLENGE]", error);
  return errorResponse(parsed.message, parsed.statusCode);
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);
    const auth = await authorizeRequest(request, "attendance.attendance.create");
    if (!auth.assignmentId) throw new ChallengeError("برای این حساب مسئولیت سازمانی فعال تعریف نشده است.", 403);

    const body: unknown = await request.json().catch(() => null);
    if (!isPlainObject(body) || (body.action !== "CHECK_IN" && body.action !== "CHECK_OUT")) {
      throw new ChallengeError("نوع عملیات باید CHECK_IN یا CHECK_OUT باشد.");
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const assignment = await prisma.employeeAssignment.findFirst({
      where: {
        id: auth.assignmentId, userId: auth.user.id, status: "ACTIVE",
        startDate: { lte: now }, OR: [{ endDate: null }, { endDate: { gt: now } }],
      },
      include: { location: true, organizationUnit: { include: { location: true } } },
    });
    if (!assignment) throw new ChallengeError("مسئولیت سازمانی فعال پیدا نشد.", 403);

    const location = assignment.location ?? assignment.organizationUnit.location;
    if (!location || location.status !== "ACTIVE" || !location.geofenceEnabled ||
        location.latitude === null || location.longitude === null) {
      throw new ChallengeError("مختصات یا محدوده حضور محل کار تنظیم نشده است.", 409);
    }

    const faceProfile = await prisma.faceIdentityProfile.findUnique({
      where: { userId: auth.user.id },
      select: { isActive: true, consentAcceptedAt: true },
    });
    if (!faceProfile || !faceProfile.isActive || !faceProfile.consentAcceptedAt) {
      throw new ChallengeError(
        "برای این پرسنل تصویر مرجع و رضایت ثبت‌شده احراز چهره وجود ندارد.",
        409
      );
    }

    const nonce = createChallengeNonce();
    const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + 90_000);
    const challenge = await prisma.attendanceChallenge.create({
      data: {
        userId: auth.user.id,
        employeeAssignmentId: assignment.id,
        action: body.action,
        nonceHash: sha256(nonce),
        expiresAt,
        ipAddress: getRequestIp(request),
        userAgentHash: sha256(request.headers.get("user-agent") ?? ""),
      },
      select: { id: true, action: true, expiresAt: true },
    });

    const response = successResponse({
      challengeId: challenge.id,
      nonce,
      action: challenge.action,
      serverTime: now.toISOString(),
      expiresAt: challenge.expiresAt.toISOString(),
      geofenceRadiusMeters: location.geofenceRadiusMeters,
      message: "چالش ایجاد شد؛ احراز زنده چهره و GPS باید پیش از انقضا تکمیل شوند.",
    }, 201);
    response.headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store, private");
    return response;
  } catch (error) { return handleError(error); }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\Ehsan\Desktop\sahand\app\api\attendance\challenges\[challengeId]\verify\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";

import {
  AttendanceChallengeStatus,
  AttendanceEventType,
  AttendanceVerificationStatus,
  AttendanceVerificationType,
  EmployeeShiftScheduleStatus,
  Prisma,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";

import { decideAttendancePunch } from "@/modules/attendance/attendance-punch-policy";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { decryptReferenceImage } from "@/libs/attendance/biometric-crypto";

import {
  FaceProviderUnavailableError,
  FaceVerificationRejectedError,
  verifyLiveFace,
} from "@/libs/attendance/face-verification";

import {
  errorResponse,
  successResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  assertSameOriginRequest,
  authorizeRequest,
  RequestOriginError,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

import { AuthError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.service";
import { PermissionDeniedError } from "@/modules/permissions/access-control";

import {
  calculateDistanceMeters,
  getApiError,
  getRequestIp,
  getSaturdayBasedDayOfWeek,
  getWorkDate,
  isPlainObject,
  safeHexEqual,
  sha256,
} from "@/modules/attendance/attendance-utils";

import {
  calculateAttendance,
  type AttendancePunch,
} from "@/modules/attendance/attendance-calculator";

class VerifyError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public statusCode = 400
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "VerifyError";
  }
}

type VerifyBody = {
  nonce: string;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  accuracyMeters: number;
  liveSessionToken: string;
};

type Transaction = Prisma.TransactionClient;

function parseBody(value: unknown): VerifyBody {
  if (!isPlainObject(value)) {
    throw new VerifyError("اطلاعات درخواست معتبر نیست.");
  }

  const {
    nonce,
    latitude,
    longitude,
    accuracyMeters,
    liveSessionToken,
  } = value;

  if (
    typeof nonce !== "string" ||
    nonce.length < 30 ||
    nonce.length > 100
  ) {
    throw new VerifyError("چالش امنیتی معتبر نیست.");
  }

  if (
    typeof latitude !== "number" ||
    !Number.isFinite(latitude) ||
    latitude < -90 ||
    latitude > 90 ||
    typeof longitude !== "number" ||
    !Number.isFinite(longitude) ||
    longitude < -180 ||
    longitude > 180
  ) {
    throw new VerifyError("مختصات GPS معتبر نیست.");
  }

  if (
    typeof accuracyMeters !== "number" ||
    !Number.isFinite(accuracyMeters) ||
    accuracyMeters <= 0 ||
    accuracyMeters > 30
  ) {
    throw new VerifyError(
      "دقت GPS کافی نیست؛ دقت باید حداکثر ۳۰ متر باشد.",
      422
    );
  }

  if (
    typeof liveSessionToken !== "string" ||
    liveSessionToken.length < 20 ||
    liveSessionToken.length > 5000
  ) {
    throw new VerifyError(
      "توکن نشست زنده چهره معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  return {
    nonce,
    latitude,
    longitude,
    accuracyMeters,
    liveSessionToken,
  };
}

async function failChallenge(
  id: string,
  reason: string
) {
  await prisma.attendanceChallenge.updateMany({
    where: {
      id,
      status: AttendanceChallengeStatus.OPEN,
      consumedAt: null,
    },
    data: {
      status: AttendanceChallengeStatus.FAILED,
      failedAt: new Date(),
      failureReason: reason.slice(0, 200),
    },
  });
}

function getWeekStart(workDate: Date): Date {
  const result = new Date(workDate);
  result.setUTCDate(
    result.getUTCDate() -
      getSaturdayBasedDayOfWeek(result)
  );
  return result;
}

function previousDate(workDate: Date): Date {
  const result = new Date(workDate);
  result.setUTCDate(result.getUTCDate() - 1);
  return result;
}

async function getScheduleSnapshot(
  tx: Transaction,
  userId: string,
  workDate: Date
) {
  const schedule =
    await tx.employeeShiftSchedule.findFirst({
      where: {
        employeeUserId: userId,
        weekStartDate: getWeekStart(workDate),
        status: EmployeeShiftScheduleStatus.APPROVED,
      },
      orderBy: {
        version: "desc",
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
        version: true,
        segments: {
          where: { workDate },
          orderBy: [
            { startsAt: "asc" },
            { segmentOrder: "asc" },
          ],
          select: {
            startsAt: true,
            endsAt: true,
            requiredMinutes: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });

  if (!schedule) {
    return null;
  }

  const segments = schedule.segments;

  return {
    scheduleId: schedule.id,
    scheduleVersion: schedule.version,
    requiredMinutes: segments.reduce(
      (sum, segment) =>
        sum + segment.requiredMinutes,
      0
    ),
    scheduledStartAt:
      segments.length > 0
        ? segments[0].startsAt
        : null,
    scheduledEndAt:
      segments.length > 0
        ? segments.reduce(
            (latest, segment) =>
              segment.endsAt > latest
                ? segment.endsAt
                : latest,
            segments[0].endsAt
          )
        : null,
  };
}

async function getLegacyRequiredMinutes(
  tx: Transaction,
  assignmentId: string,
  organizationUnitId: string,
  workDate: Date
) {
  const dayOfWeek =
    getSaturdayBasedDayOfWeek(workDate);

  const shiftAssignments =
    await tx.shiftAssignment.findMany({
      where: {
        isActive: true,
        validFrom: { lte: workDate },
        AND: [
          {
            OR: [
              { validTo: null },
              { validTo: { gte: workDate } },
            ],
          },
        ],
        OR: [
          {
            targetType: "EMPLOYEE",
            employeeAssignmentId: assignmentId,
          },
          {
            targetType: "UNIT",
            organizationUnitId,
          },
          {
            targetType: "COMPANY",
          },
        ],
      },
      include: {
        shift: {
          include: {
            days: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });

  const rank = (target: string) => {
    switch (target) {
      case "EMPLOYEE":
        return 3;
      case "UNIT":
        return 2;
      case "COMPANY":
        return 1;
      default:
        return 0;
    }
  };

  shiftAssignments.sort(
    (a, b) =>
      rank(b.targetType) - rank(a.targetType)
  );

  const selectedShift =
    shiftAssignments[0] ?? null;

  const days =
    selectedShift?.shift.days.filter(
      (day) =>
        day.dayOfWeek === dayOfWeek &&
        day.dayType === "NORMAL"
    ) ?? [];

  return {
    shiftAssignmentId: selectedShift?.id ?? null,
    requiredMinutes:
      days.length > 0
        ? days.reduce(
            (sum, day) =>
              sum +
              (day.isWorkingDay
                ? day.requiredMinutes
                : 0),
            0
          )
        : 480,
  };
}

function handleError(error: unknown) {
  if (
    error instanceof VerifyError ||
    error instanceof RequestOriginError ||
    error instanceof AuthError ||
    error instanceof PermissionDeniedError ||
    error instanceof FaceProviderUnavailableError ||
    error instanceof FaceVerificationRejectedError
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      error.message,
      error.statusCode
    );
  }

  if (
    error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
    (error.code === "P2034" ||
      error.code === "P2002")
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "اطلاعات تردد هم‌زمان تغییر کرده است. دوباره تلاش کنید.",
      409
    );
  }

  const parsed = getApiError(error);

  console.error("[ATTENDANCE_VERIFY]", error);

  return errorResponse(
    parsed.message,
    parsed.statusCode
  );
}

export async function POST(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: {
    params: Promise<{ challengeId: string }>;
  }
) {
  let challengeId = "";

  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    challengeId =
      (await context.params).challengeId;

    if (
      !challengeId ||
      challengeId.length > 100
    ) {
      throw new VerifyError(
        "شناسه چالش معتبر نیست.",
        404
      );
    }

    const challenge =
      await prisma.attendanceChallenge.findUnique({
        where: { id: challengeId },
      });

    if (!challenge) {
      throw new VerifyError(
        "چالش پیدا نشد.",
        404
      );
    }

    const auth = await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "attendance.attendance.create",
      challenge.employeeAssignmentId
    );

    if (challenge.userId !== auth.user.id) {
      throw new VerifyError(
        "چالش پیدا نشد.",
        404
      );
    }

    if (
      challenge.status !==
        AttendanceChallengeStatus.OPEN ||
      challenge.consumedAt
    ) {
      throw new VerifyError(
        "چالش قبلاً استفاده شده یا معتبر نیست.",
        409
      );
    }

    const now = new Date();

    if (challenge.expiresAt <= now) {
      await prisma.attendanceChallenge.updateMany({
        where: {
          id: challenge.id,
          status: AttendanceChallengeStatus.OPEN,
        },
        data: {
          status: AttendanceChallengeStatus.EXPIRED,
          failureReason: "CHALLENGE_EXPIRED",
        },
      });

      throw new VerifyError(
        "مهلت درخواست تمام شده است؛ دوباره شروع کنید.",
        410
      );
    }

    const body = parseBody(
      await request.json().catch(() => null)
    );

    if (
      !safeHexEqual(
        challenge.nonceHash,
        sha256(body.nonce)
      )
    ) {
      await failChallenge(
        challenge.id,
        "INVALID_NONCE"
      );

      throw new VerifyError(
        "اعتبار چالش امنیتی تأیید نشد.",
        403
      );
    }

    const assignment =
      await prisma.employeeAssignment.findFirst({
        where: {
          id: challenge.employeeAssignmentId,
          userId: auth.user.id,
          status: "ACTIVE",
          startDate: { lte: now },
          OR: [
            { endDate: null },
            { endDate: { gt: now } },
          ],
        },
        include: {
          location: true,
          organizationUnit: {
            include: {
              location: true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!assignment) {
      await failChallenge(
        challenge.id,
        "ASSIGNMENT_INACTIVE"
      );

      throw new VerifyError(
        "مسئولیت سازمانی فعال نیست.",
        403
      );
    }

    const location =
      assignment.location ??
      assignment.organizationUnit.location;

    if (
      !location ||
      location.status !== "ACTIVE" ||
      !location.geofenceEnabled ||
      location.latitude === null ||
      location.longitude === null
    ) {
      await failChallenge(
        challenge.id,
        "LOCATION_NOT_CONFIGURED"
      );

      throw new VerifyError(
        "محدوده حضور معتبر پیکربندی نشده است.",
        409
      );
    }

    const distance = calculateDistanceMeters(
      body.latitude,
      body.longitude,
      Number(location.latitude),
      Number(location.longitude)
    );

    if (
      distance + body.accuracyMeters >
      location.geofenceRadiusMeters
    ) {
      await failChallenge(
        challenge.id,
        "OUTSIDE_OR_UNCERTAIN_GEOFENCE"
      );

      throw new VerifyError(
        `موقعیت مکانی معتبر نیست؛ فاصله ${Math.round(
          distance
        )} متر و خطای GPS برابر ${Math.round(
          body.accuracyMeters
        )} متر است.`,
        403
      );
    }

    const faceProfile =
      await prisma.faceIdentityProfile.findUnique({
        where: {
          userId: auth.user.id,
        },
      });

    if (
      !faceProfile ||
      !faceProfile.isActive ||
      !faceProfile.consentAcceptedAt
    ) {
      throw new VerifyError(
        "تصویر مرجع یا رضایت ثبت‌شده برای احراز چهره وجود ندارد.",
        409
      );
    }

    let referenceImage: Buffer;

    try {
      referenceImage = decryptReferenceImage({
        encrypted:
          faceProfile.referenceImageEncrypted,
        iv: faceProfile.referenceImageIv,
        authTag:
          faceProfile.referenceImageAuthTag,
        keyVersion:
          faceProfile.encryptionKeyVersion,
      });
    } catch {
      throw new VerifyError(
        "بازیابی امن تصویر مرجع ممکن نیست.",
        503
      );
    }

    const face = await verifyLiveFace({
      challengeId: challenge.id,
      challengeNonce: body.nonce,
      userId: auth.user.id,
      action: challenge.action,
      liveSessionToken:
        body.liveSessionToken,
      referenceImage,
      referenceImageMimeType:
        faceProfile.referenceImageMimeType,
    });

    const today = getWorkDate(now);

    const eventType =
      challenge.action === "CHECK_IN"
        ? AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN
        : AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT;

    const saved = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        // Lock the employee, not merely one assignment.
        // All attendance requests for the same user
        // are serialized across organizational roles.
        const lockedUsers =
          await tx.$queryRaw<
            Array<{ id: string }>
          >`
            SELECT id
            FROM "User"
            WHERE id = ${auth.user.id}
            FOR UPDATE
          `;

        if (lockedUsers.length !== 1) {
          throw new VerifyError(
            "حساب کارمند یافت نشد.",
            404
          );
        }

        const consumed =
          await tx.attendanceChallenge.updateMany({
            where: {
              id: challenge.id,
              userId: auth.user.id,
              status:
                AttendanceChallengeStatus.OPEN,
              consumedAt: null,
              expiresAt: { gt: now },
            },
            data: {
              status:
                AttendanceChallengeStatus.CONSUMED,
              consumedAt: now,
            },
          });

        if (consumed.count !== 1) {
          throw new VerifyError(
            "چالش قبلاً مصرف شده یا منقضی شده است.",
            409
          );
        }

        // Search across ALL assignments belonging
        // to this employee, including historical ones.
        // This allows a checkout after a role change.
        const candidateRecords =
          await tx.attendanceRecord.findMany({
            where: {
              employeeAssignment: {
                userId: auth.user.id,
              },
              workDate: {
                gte: previousDate(today),
                lte: today,
              },
            },
            include: {
              events: {
                orderBy: [
                  { occurredAt: "asc" },
                  { createdAt: "asc" },
                ],
                select: {
                  eventType: true,
                  occurredAt: true,
                },
              },
            },
            orderBy: {
              createdAt: "desc",
            },
          });

        const decision = decideAttendancePunch({
          action: challenge.action,
          today,
          records: candidateRecords.map((candidate) => ({
            id: candidate.id,
            workDate: candidate.workDate,
            status: candidate.status,
            approvedAt: candidate.approvedAt,
            approvedByUserId: candidate.approvedByUserId,
            lastEventType: candidate.events.at(-1)?.eventType ?? null,
          })),
        });

        if (!decision.allowed) {
          throw new VerifyError(decision.message, 409);
        }

        let record: Prisma.AttendanceRecordGetPayload<object> | null =
          decision.recordId
            ? candidateRecords.find((item) => item.id === decision.recordId) ?? null
            : null;

        let scheduleSource:
          | "APPROVED_WEEKLY_SCHEDULE"
          | "LEGACY_SHIFT"
          | "EXISTING_RECORD";

        if (record === null) {
          if (challenge.action === "CHECK_OUT") {
            throw new VerifyError(
              "رکورد ورود معتبر پیدا نشد.",
              409
            );
          }

          const approved = await getScheduleSnapshot(
            tx,
            auth.user.id,
            today
          );

          const legacy = approved
            ? null
            : await getLegacyRequiredMinutes(
                tx,
                assignment.id,
                assignment.organizationUnitId,
                today
              );

          scheduleSource = approved
            ? "APPROVED_WEEKLY_SCHEDULE"
            : "LEGACY_SHIFT";

          record = await tx.attendanceRecord.create({
            data: {
              employeeAssignmentId: assignment.id,
              shiftAssignmentId: legacy?.shiftAssignmentId ?? null,
              checkpointLocationId: location.id,
              workDate: today,
              timeZone: "Asia/Tehran",
              requiredMinutes:
                approved?.requiredMinutes ?? legacy!.requiredMinutes,
              scheduledStartAt: approved?.scheduledStartAt ?? null,
              scheduledEndAt: approved?.scheduledEndAt ?? null,
              status: "OPEN",
            },
          });
        } else {
          scheduleSource = "EXISTING_RECORD";
        }

        if (record === null) {
          throw new VerifyError(
            "رکورد حضور و غیاب ایجاد یا بازیابی نشد.",
            500
          );
        }

        // Never modify an approved or otherwise locked record
        // through the employee's ordinary punch endpoint.
        if (
          record.approvedAt !== null ||
          record.approvedByUserId !== null ||
          (record.status !== "OPEN" && record.status !== "COMPLETED")
        ) {
          throw new VerifyError(
            "این رکورد حضور تأیید یا قفل شده است و فقط از مسیر اصلاحات مجاز قابل تغییر است.",
            409
          );
        }

        const latest =
          await tx.attendanceEvent.findFirst({
            where: {
              attendanceRecordId: record.id,
            },
            orderBy: [
              { occurredAt: "desc" },
              { createdAt: "desc" },
            ],
          });

        if (
          challenge.action === "CHECK_IN" &&
          latest &&
          latest.eventType !==
            AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT &&
          latest.eventType !==
            AttendanceEventType.AUTO_CHECK_OUT
        ) {
          throw new VerifyError(
            "ورود قبلی هنوز بسته نشده است.",
            409
          );
        }

        if (
          challenge.action === "CHECK_OUT" &&
          (!latest ||
            (latest.eventType !==
              AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN &&
              latest.eventType !==
                AttendanceEventType.BREAK_END))
        ) {
          throw new VerifyError(
            "برای خروج، ورود باز معتبر لازم است.",
            409
          );
        }

        const event =
          await tx.attendanceEvent.create({
            data: {
              attendanceRecordId:
                record.id,
              eventType,
              occurredAt: now,
              latitude: body.latitude,
              longitude: body.longitude,
              accuracyMeters:
                body.accuracyMeters,
              distanceFromCheckpointMeters:
                distance,
              geofencePassed: true,
              source: "PWA",
              ipAddress:
                getRequestIp(request),
              userAgent:
                request.headers.get(
                  "user-agent"
                ),
              idempotencyKey:
                challenge.id,
              notes:
                `Face provider: ${face.provider}; ` +
                `verification: ${face.verificationId}`,
            },
          });

        await tx.attendanceVerification.createMany({
          data: [
            {
              attendanceEventId: event.id,
              verificationType:
                AttendanceVerificationType.GPS,
              status:
                AttendanceVerificationStatus.PASSED,
              provider:
                "BROWSER_GEOLOCATION",
              details: {
                distanceMeters: distance,
                accuracyMeters:
                  body.accuracyMeters,
                geofenceRadiusMeters:
                  location.geofenceRadiusMeters,
              },
              verifiedAt: now,
            },
            {
              attendanceEventId: event.id,
              verificationType:
                AttendanceVerificationType.FACE,
              status:
                AttendanceVerificationStatus.PASSED,
              score:
                face.similarityScore,
              provider: face.provider,
              details: {
                verificationId:
                  face.verificationId,
              },
              verifiedAt: now,
            },
            {
              attendanceEventId: event.id,
              verificationType:
                AttendanceVerificationType.LIVENESS,
              status:
                AttendanceVerificationStatus.PASSED,
              provider: face.provider,
              details: {
                verificationId:
                  face.verificationId,
              },
              verifiedAt: now,
            },
          ],
        });

        const allEvents =
          await tx.attendanceEvent.findMany({
            where: {
              attendanceRecordId:
                record.id,
            },
            orderBy: [
              { occurredAt: "asc" },
              { createdAt: "asc" },
            ],
            select: {
              eventType: true,
              occurredAt: true,
            },
          });

        const punches: AttendancePunch[] =
          allEvents.map((item) => ({
            type: item.eventType,
            occurredAt: item.occurredAt,
          }));

        const calculation =
          calculateAttendance({
            punches,
            requiredMinutes:
              record.requiredMinutes,
            scheduledStartAt:
              record.scheduledStartAt,
            scheduledEndAt:
              record.scheduledEndAt,
          });

        const updatedRecord =
          await tx.attendanceRecord.update({
            where: {
              id: record.id,
            },
            data: {
              firstCheckInAt:
                calculation.firstCheckInAt,
              lastCheckOutAt:
                calculation.lastCheckOutAt,
              workedMinutes:
                calculation.workedMinutes,
              breakMinutes:
                calculation.breakMinutes,
              overtimeMinutes:
                calculation.overtimeMinutes,
              lateMinutes:
                calculation.lateMinutes,
              earlyLeaveMinutes:
                calculation.earlyLeaveMinutes,
              status:
                calculation.isComplete &&
                !calculation.requiresReview
                  ? "COMPLETED"
                  : "OPEN",
            },
          });

        return {
          event,
          record: updatedRecord,
          calculation: {
            requiredMinutes:
              calculation.requiredMinutes,
            workedMinutes:
              calculation.workedMinutes,
            breakMinutes:
              calculation.breakMinutes,
            deficitMinutes:
              calculation.deficitMinutes,
            proposedOvertimeMinutes:
              calculation.overtimeMinutes,
            lateMinutes:
              calculation.lateMinutes,
            earlyLeaveMinutes:
              calculation.earlyLeaveMinutes,
            isComplete:
              calculation.isComplete,
            requiresReview:
              calculation.requiresReview,
            warnings:
              calculation.warnings,
          },
          scheduleSource,
        };
      },
      {
        isolationLevel:
          Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
        maxWait: 5000,
        timeout: 15000,
      }
    );

    const response = successResponse({
      event: saved.event,
      record: saved.record,
      calculation: saved.calculation,
      scheduleSource:
        saved.scheduleSource,
      message:
        challenge.action === "CHECK_IN"
          ? "ورود با موفقیت ثبت شد."
          : "خروج با موفقیت ثبت شد.",
    });

    response.headers.set(
      "Cache-Control",
      "no-store, private"
    );

    return response;
  } catch (error) {
    if (
      challengeId &&
      (error instanceof
        FaceVerificationRejectedError ||
        error instanceof VerifyError)
    ) {
      await failChallenge(
        challengeId,
        error.message
      ).catch(() => undefined);
    }

    return handleError(error);
  }
}
