import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { encryptReferenceImage } from "@/libs/attendance/biometric-crypto";
import { errorResponse, successResponse } from "@/libs/api-response";
import { assertSameOriginRequest, authorizeGlobalRequest, RequestOriginError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";
import { AuthError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.service";
import { PermissionDeniedError } from "@/modules/permissions/access-control";
import { isPlainObject } from "@/modules/attendance/attendance-utils";

class FaceProfileError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string, public statusCode = 400) { super(message); }
}
function decodeImage(value: unknown, mimeValue: unknown) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || typeof mimeValue !== "string") {
    throw new FaceProfileError("تصویر مرجع و نوع فایل الزامی است.");
  }
  const match = value.match(/^data:(image\/(?:jpeg|png));base64,(.+)$/i);
  const mimeType = (match?.[1] ?? mimeValue).toLowerCase();
  const encoded = match?.[2] ?? value;
  if (mimeType !== "image/jpeg" && mimeType !== "image/png") {
    throw new FaceProfileError("فرمت تصویر فقط می‌تواند JPEG یا PNG باشد.");
  }
  if (encoded.length === 0 || encoded.length > 7_000_000 ||
      !/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$/.test(encoded) || encoded.length % 4 !== 0) {
    throw new FaceProfileError("اطلاعات تصویر معتبر نیست یا حجم آن بیش از حد مجاز است.");
  }
  const bytes = Buffer.from(encoded, "base64");
  if (!bytes.length || bytes.length > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
    throw new FaceProfileError("حجم تصویر باید حداکثر ۵ مگابایت باشد.");
  }
  const jpeg = bytes.length >= 3 && bytes[0] === 0xff && bytes[1] === 0xd8 && bytes[2] === 0xff;
  const png = bytes.length >= 8 && bytes.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10]));
  if ((mimeType === "image/jpeg" && !jpeg) || (mimeType === "image/png" && !png)) {
    throw new FaceProfileError("محتوای فایل با فرمت اعلام‌شده مطابقت ندارد.");
  }
  return { bytes, mimeType };
}
function handleError(error: unknown) {
  if (error instanceof FaceProfileError || error instanceof RequestOriginError ||
      error instanceof AuthError || error instanceof PermissionDeniedError) {
    return errorResponse(error.message, error.statusCode);
  }
  console.error("[ATTENDANCE_FACE_PROFILE]", error);
  return errorResponse("خطا در ثبت تصویر مرجع پرسنل.", 500);
}

/** HR records one consented reference image. Live attendance frames are not sent to this route. */
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);
    const { user: actor } = await authorizeGlobalRequest(request, "hr.personnel.update");
    const body: unknown = await request.json().catch(() => null);
    if (!isPlainObject(body)) throw new FaceProfileError("اطلاعات درخواست معتبر نیست.");

    const userId = typeof body.userId === "string" ? body.userId.trim() : "";
    const consentAccepted = body.consentAccepted === true;
    const consentVersion = typeof body.consentVersion === "string" ? body.consentVersion.trim() : "";
    if (!userId) throw new FaceProfileError("شناسه پرسنل الزامی است.");
    if (!consentAccepted || !consentVersion || consentVersion.length > 50) {
      throw new FaceProfileError("ثبت تصویر مرجع مستلزم ثبت رضایت آگاهانه مستند پرسنل است.");
    }

    const employee = await prisma.user.findUnique({
      where: { id: userId },
      select: { id: true, isActive: true, employeeProfile: { select: { id: true } } },
    });
    if (!employee || !employee.isActive || !employee.employeeProfile) {
      throw new FaceProfileError("حساب فعال و پرونده پرسنلی معتبر پیدا نشد.", 404);
    }

    const { bytes, mimeType } = decodeImage(body.referenceImageBase64, body.referenceImageMimeType);
    const encrypted = encryptReferenceImage(bytes);
    const now = new Date();

    const profile = await prisma.$transaction(async tx => {
      const saved = await tx.faceIdentityProfile.upsert({
        where: { userId },
        create: {
          userId,
          referenceImageEncrypted: encrypted.encrypted,
          referenceImageIv: encrypted.iv,
          referenceImageAuthTag: encrypted.authTag,
          referenceImageMimeType: mimeType,
          encryptionKeyVersion: encrypted.keyVersion,
          consentAcceptedAt: now,
          consentVersion,
          enrolledByUserId: actor.id,
          isActive: true,
        },
        update: {
          referenceImageEncrypted: encrypted.encrypted,
          referenceImageIv: encrypted.iv,
          referenceImageAuthTag: encrypted.authTag,
          referenceImageMimeType: mimeType,
          encryptionKeyVersion: encrypted.keyVersion,
          consentAcceptedAt: now,
          consentVersion,
          enrolledByUserId: actor.id,
          isActive: true,
          enrolledAt: now,
        },
        select: { id: true, userId: true, isActive: true, enrolledAt: true },
      });
      await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
        data: {
          actorUserId: actor.id,
          entityType: "FaceIdentityProfile",
          entityId: saved.id,
          action: "FACE_REFERENCE_ENROLLED",
          afterData: { userId, mimeType, consentVersion },
        },
      });
      return saved;
    });

    const response = successResponse({ profile, message: "تصویر مرجع رمزنگاری‌شده ثبت شد." }, 201);
    response.headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store, private");
    return response;
  } catch (error) { return handleError(error); }
}

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";

import {
  HolidayType,
  Prisma,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";

import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";

import {
  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  authorizeGlobalRequest,
  assertSameOriginRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

import { AuthError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.service";

import {
  PermissionDeniedError,
} from "@/modules/permissions/access-control";

// ==========================================
// TYPES
// ==========================================

class HolidayCalendarError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public statusCode = 400
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "HolidayCalendarError";
  }
}

type HolidayDayInput = {
  date: Date;
  jalaliDate: string;
  title: string;
  holidayType: HolidayType;
  isHoliday: boolean;
  isWorkingDayOverride: boolean | null;
};

// ==========================================
// HELPERS
// ==========================================

function isObject(
  value: unknown
): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

function parseYear(value: unknown): number {
  if (
    typeof value !== "number" ||
    !Number.isInteger(value) ||
    value < 1300 ||
    value > 1600
  ) {
    throw new HolidayCalendarError(
      "سال شمسی معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function parseString(
  value: unknown,
  field: string,
  maxLength: number
): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new HolidayCalendarError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  const result = value.trim();

  if (!result) {
    throw new HolidayCalendarError(
      `${field} الزامی است.`
    );
  }

  if (result.length > maxLength) {
    throw new HolidayCalendarError(
      `${field} بیش از حد مجاز طولانی است.`
    );
  }

  return result;
}

function parseHolidayDays(
  value: unknown
): HolidayDayInput[] {
  if (value === undefined) {
    return [];
  }

  if (
    !Array.isArray(value) ||
    value.length > 400
  ) {
    throw new HolidayCalendarError(
      "فهرست تعطیلات معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  const days: HolidayDayInput[] = [];
  const usedDates = new Set<string>();

  const allowedTypes = Object.values(HolidayType);

  for (const item of value) {
    if (!isObject(item)) {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "اطلاعات روز تعطیل معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const dateString = parseString(
      item.date,
      "تاریخ میلادی",
      10
    );

    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateString)) {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "تاریخ میلادی باید با فرمت YYYY-MM-DD باشد."
      );
    }

    const date = new Date(
      `${dateString}T00:00:00.000Z`
    );

    if (
      Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ||
      date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== dateString
    ) {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "تاریخ میلادی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    if (usedDates.has(dateString)) {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "برای یک تاریخ نمی‌توان بیش از یک رکورد در یک تقویم ثبت کرد."
      );
    }

    usedDates.add(dateString);

    const jalaliDate = parseString(
      item.jalaliDate,
      "تاریخ شمسی",
      10
    );

    if (!/^\d{4}\/\d{2}\/\d{2}$/.test(jalaliDate)) {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "تاریخ شمسی باید با فرمت YYYY/MM/DD باشد."
      );
    }

    const title = parseString(
      item.title,
      "عنوان مناسبت",
      200
    );

    if (
      typeof item.holidayType !== "string" ||
      !allowedTypes.includes(
        item.holidayType as HolidayType
      )
    ) {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "نوع تعطیلی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    if (typeof item.isHoliday !== "boolean") {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "وضعیت تعطیلی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const override = item.isWorkingDayOverride;

    if (
      override !== null &&
      typeof override !== "boolean"
    ) {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "وضعیت استثنای روز کاری معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    days.push({
      date,
      jalaliDate,
      title,
      holidayType: item.holidayType as HolidayType,
      isHoliday: item.isHoliday,
      isWorkingDayOverride: override,
    });
  }

  return days;
}

function getErrorResponse(error: unknown) {
  if (error instanceof HolidayCalendarError) {
    return errorResponse(
      error.message,
      error.statusCode
    );
  }

  if (error instanceof AuthError) {
    return errorResponse(
      "احراز هویت ناموفق است.",
      401
    );
  }

  if (error instanceof PermissionDeniedError) {
    return errorResponse(
      "شما مجوز انجام این عملیات را ندارید.",
      403
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2002"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "برای این سال یا تاریخ قبلاً اطلاعات ثبت شده است.",
      409
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2034"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "اطلاعات هم‌زمان تغییر کرده است. دوباره تلاش کنید.",
      409
    );
  }

  console.error(
    "[HOLIDAY_CALENDAR_ERROR]",
    error
  );

  return errorResponse(
    "خطای داخلی در مدیریت تقویم تعطیلات.",
    500
  );
}

// ==========================================
// GET - LIST HOLIDAY CALENDARS
// ==========================================

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    await authorizeGlobalRequest(
      request,
      "attendance.attendance.view"
    );

    const params =
      request.nextUrl.searchParams;

    const yearParam = params.get("year");
    const activeParam = params.get("active");

    const where: Prisma.HolidayCalendarWhereInput = {};

    if (yearParam !== null) {
      const year = Number(yearParam);

      where.jalaliYear = parseYear(year);
    }

    if (activeParam === "true") {
      where.isActive = true;
    } else if (activeParam === "false") {
      where.isActive = false;
    }

    const calendars =
      await prisma.holidayCalendar.findMany({
        where,

        include: {
          days: {
            orderBy: {
              date: "asc",
            },
          },

          _count: {
            select: {
              days: true,
            },
          },
        },

        orderBy: {
          jalaliYear: "desc",
        },

        take: 100,
      });

    return successResponse({
      calendars,
      count: calendars.length,
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// POST - CREATE HOLIDAY CALENDAR
// ==========================================

export async function POST(
  request: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const { user: actor } =
      await authorizeGlobalRequest(
        request,
        "attendance.attendance.create"
      );

    let body: unknown;

    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "ساختار اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "اطلاعات تقویم معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const jalaliYear = parseYear(
      body.jalaliYear
    );

    const name = parseString(
      body.name,
      "نام تقویم",
      150
    );

    const isActive =
      body.isActive === undefined
        ? true
        : body.isActive;

    if (typeof isActive !== "boolean") {
      throw new HolidayCalendarError(
        "وضعیت فعال بودن تقویم معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const days = parseHolidayDays(
      body.days
    );

    // Ensure every registered date belongs
    // to the selected Jalali year.

    for (const day of days) {
      const dayYear = Number(
        day.jalaliDate.slice(0, 4)
      );

      if (dayYear !== jalaliYear) {
        throw new HolidayCalendarError(
          "سال تاریخ مناسبت با سال تقویم مطابقت ندارد."
        );
      }
    }

    const calendar = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const created =
          await tx.holidayCalendar.create({
            data: {
              jalaliYear,
              name,
              isActive,

              days: {
                create: days,
              },
            },

            include: {
              days: {
                orderBy: {
                  date: "asc",
                },
              },

              _count: {
                select: {
                  days: true,
                },
              },
            },
          });

        await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
          data: {
            actorUserId: actor.id,
            entityType: "HolidayCalendar",
            entityId: created.id,
            action: "HOLIDAY_CALENDAR_CREATED",

            afterData: {
              jalaliYear: created.jalaliYear,
              name: created.name,
              isActive: created.isActive,
              daysCount: created.days.length,
            },

            ipAddress:
              request.headers
                .get("x-forwarded-for")
                ?.split(",")[0]
                ?.trim() ||
              request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
              null,
          },
        });

        return created;
      },
      {
        isolationLevel:
          Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
      }
    );

    return successResponse(
      {
        calendar,
        message:
          "تقویم تعطیلات با موفقیت ایجاد شد.",
      },
      201
    );

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

  HolidayType,
  Prisma,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";

  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  authorizeGlobalRequest,
  assertSameOriginRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

import { AuthError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.service";

import {
  PermissionDeniedError,
} from "@/modules/permissions/access-control";

// ==========================================
// TYPES
// ==========================================

type RouteContext = {
  params: Promise<{
    calendarId: string;
  }>;
};

class HolidayError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public statusCode = 400
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "HolidayError";
  }
}

type HolidayInput = {
  date: Date;
  jalaliDate: string;
  title: string;
  holidayType: HolidayType;
  isHoliday: boolean;
  isWorkingDayOverride: boolean | null;
};

// ==========================================
// HELPERS
// ==========================================

function isObject(
  value: unknown
): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

function parseString(
  value: unknown,
  field: string,
  maxLength: number
): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new HolidayError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  const result = value.trim();

  if (!result) {
    throw new HolidayError(
      `${field} الزامی است.`
    );
  }

  if (result.length > maxLength) {
    throw new HolidayError(
      `${field} بیش از حد مجاز طولانی است.`
    );
  }

  return result;
}

function parseHolidayInput(
  value: unknown,
  jalaliYear: number
): HolidayInput {
  if (!isObject(value)) {
    throw new HolidayError(
      "اطلاعات روز تعطیل معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  const dateString = parseString(
    value.date,
    "تاریخ میلادی",
    10
  );

  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateString)) {
    throw new HolidayError(
      "تاریخ میلادی باید با فرمت YYYY-MM-DD باشد."
    );
  }

  const date = new Date(
    `${dateString}T00:00:00.000Z`
  );

  if (
    Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ||
    date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== dateString
  ) {
    throw new HolidayError(
      "تاریخ میلادی معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  const jalaliDate = parseString(
    value.jalaliDate,
    "تاریخ شمسی",
    10
  );

  if (!/^\d{4}\/\d{2}\/\d{2}$/.test(jalaliDate)) {
    throw new HolidayError(
      "تاریخ شمسی باید با فرمت YYYY/MM/DD باشد."
    );
  }

  const parsedYear = Number(
    jalaliDate.slice(0, 4)
  );

  if (parsedYear !== jalaliYear) {
    throw new HolidayError(
      "سال تاریخ مناسبت با سال تقویم مطابقت ندارد."
    );
  }

  const title = parseString(
    value.title,
    "عنوان مناسبت",
    200
  );

  const allowedTypes = Object.values(HolidayType);

  if (
    typeof value.holidayType !== "string" ||
    !allowedTypes.includes(
      value.holidayType as HolidayType
    )
  ) {
    throw new HolidayError(
      "نوع تعطیلی معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  if (typeof value.isHoliday !== "boolean") {
    throw new HolidayError(
      "وضعیت تعطیلی معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  const override = value.isWorkingDayOverride;

  if (
    override !== null &&
    typeof override !== "boolean"
  ) {
    throw new HolidayError(
      "وضعیت استثنای روز کاری معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  return {
    date,
    jalaliDate,
    title,
    holidayType: value.holidayType as HolidayType,
    isHoliday: value.isHoliday,
    isWorkingDayOverride: override,
  };
}

function getErrorResponse(error: unknown) {
  if (error instanceof HolidayError) {
    return errorResponse(
      error.message,
      error.statusCode
    );
  }

  if (error instanceof AuthError) {
    return errorResponse(
      "احراز هویت ناموفق است.",
      401
    );
  }

  if (error instanceof PermissionDeniedError) {
    return errorResponse(
      "شما مجوز انجام این عملیات را ندارید.",
      403
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2002"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "برای این تاریخ قبلاً مناسبت ثبت شده است.",
      409
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2025"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "اطلاعات موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
      404

  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&

    error
  );

  return errorResponse(
    "خطای داخلی در مدیریت تقویم تعطیلات.",
    500
  );
}

function getClientIp(request: NextRequest) {
  return (
    request.headers
      .get("x-forwarded-for")
      ?.split(",")[0]
      ?.trim() ||
    request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
    null
  );
}

// ==========================================
// GET - GET SINGLE CALENDAR
// ==========================================

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    await authorizeGlobalRequest(
      request,
      "attendance.attendance.view"
    );

    const { calendarId } = await context.params;

    if (!calendarId?.trim()) {
      throw new HolidayError(
        "شناسه تقویم معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const calendar =
      await prisma.holidayCalendar.findUnique({
        where: {
          id: calendarId,
        },

        include: {
          days: {
            orderBy: {
              date: "asc",
            },
          },

          _count: {
            select: {
              days: true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!calendar) {
      throw new HolidayError(
        "تقویم موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
        404
      );
    }

    return successResponse({
      calendar,
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// PATCH - UPDATE CALENDAR
// ==========================================

export async function PATCH(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const { user: actor } =
      await authorizeGlobalRequest(
        request,
        "attendance.attendance.update"
      );

    const { calendarId } = await context.params;

    if (!calendarId?.trim()) {
      throw new HolidayError(
        "شناسه تقویم معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    let body: unknown;

    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      throw new HolidayError(
        "ساختار اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      throw new HolidayError(
        "اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const existing =
      await prisma.holidayCalendar.findUnique({
        where: {
          id: calendarId,
        },

        include: {
          days: true,
        },
      });

    if (!existing) {
      throw new HolidayError(
        "تقویم موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
        404
      );
    }

    const updateData: Prisma.HolidayCalendarUpdateInput = {};

    if (body.name !== undefined) {
      updateData.name = parseString(
        body.name,
        "نام تقویم",
        150
      );
    }

    if (body.isActive !== undefined) {
      if (typeof body.isActive !== "boolean") {
        throw new HolidayError(
          "وضعیت فعال بودن تقویم معتبر نیست."
        );
      }

      updateData.isActive = body.isActive;
    }

    let parsedDays: HolidayInput[] | undefined;

    if (body.days !== undefined) {
      if (
        !Array.isArray(body.days) ||
        body.days.length > 400
      ) {
        throw new HolidayError(
          "فهرست تعطیلات معتبر نیست."
        );
      }

      parsedDays = body.days.map((item) =>
        parseHolidayInput(
          item,
          existing.jalaliYear
        )
      );

      const uniqueDates = new Set(
        parsedDays.map((item) =>
          item.date.toISOString().slice(0, 10)
        )
      );

      if (uniqueDates.size !== parsedDays.length) {
        throw new HolidayError(
          "تاریخ تکراری در فهرست تعطیلات وجود دارد."
        );
      }
    }

    const updated = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        if (parsedDays !== undefined) {
          for (const day of parsedDays) {
            const existingDay =
              await tx.holidayCalendarDay.findUnique({
                where: {
                  calendarId_date: {
                    calendarId,
                    date: day.date,
                  },
                },
              });

            if (existingDay) {
              await tx.holidayCalendarDay.update({
                where: {
                  id: existingDay.id,
                },

                data: {
                  jalaliDate: day.jalaliDate,
                  title: day.title,
                  holidayType: day.holidayType,
                  isHoliday: day.isHoliday,
                  isWorkingDayOverride:
                    day.isWorkingDayOverride,
                },
              });
            } else {
              await tx.holidayCalendarDay.create({
                data: {
                  calendarId,
                  ...day,
                },
              });
            }
          }
        }

        const result =
          await tx.holidayCalendar.update({
            where: {
              id: calendarId,
            },

            data: updateData,

            include: {
              days: {
                orderBy: {
                  date: "asc",
                },
              },

              _count: {
                select: {
                  days: true,
                },
              },
            },
          });

        await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
          data: {
            actorUserId: actor.id,
            entityType: "HolidayCalendar",
            entityId: calendarId,
            action: "HOLIDAY_CALENDAR_UPDATED",

            beforeData: {
              name: existing.name,
              isActive: existing.isActive,
              daysCount: existing.days.length,
            },

            afterData: {
              name: result.name,
              isActive: result.isActive,
              daysCount: result.days.length,
            },

            ipAddress: getClientIp(request),
          },
        });

        return result;
      },
      {
        isolationLevel:
          Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
      }
    );

    return successResponse({
      calendar: updated,
      message:
        "تقویم تعطیلات با موفقیت ویرایش شد.",
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// DELETE - SOFT DEACTIVATE CALENDAR
// ==========================================

export async function DELETE(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const { user: actor } =
      await authorizeGlobalRequest(
        request,
        "attendance.attendance.update"
      );

    const { calendarId } = await context.params;

    if (!calendarId?.trim()) {
      throw new HolidayError(
        "شناسه تقویم معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const existing =
      await prisma.holidayCalendar.findUnique({
        where: {
          id: calendarId,
        },

        include: {
          _count: {
            select: {
              days: true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!existing) {
      throw new HolidayError(
        "تقویم موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
        404
      );
    }

    if (!existing.isActive) {
      return successResponse({
        message:
          "این تقویم قبلاً غیرفعال شده است.",
      });
    }

    await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        await tx.holidayCalendar.update({
          where: {
            id: calendarId,
          },

          data: {
            isActive: false,
          },
        });

        await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
          data: {
            actorUserId: actor.id,
            entityType: "HolidayCalendar",
            entityId: calendarId,
            action: "HOLIDAY_CALENDAR_DEACTIVATED",

            beforeData: {
              name: existing.name,
              isActive: true,
            },

            afterData: {
              name: existing.name,
              isActive: false,
            },

            ipAddress: getClientIp(request),
          },
        });
      }
    );

    return successResponse({
      message:
        "تقویم با موفقیت غیرفعال شد. اطلاعات و سوابق آن حفظ شده‌اند.",
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { assertSameOriginRequest, authenticateRequest } from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import { localFaceRequest } from "@/libs/attendance/local-face-client";

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);
    const user = await authenticateRequest(request);
    if (Number(request.headers.get("content-length") ?? 0) > 1_600_000)
      return Response.json({ error: "فریم بزرگ است." }, { status: 413 });
    const body = await request.json();
    if (typeof body?.sessionToken !== "string" || body.sessionToken.length > 200 ||
        typeof body?.frameBase64 !== "string" || body.frameBase64.length > 1_500_000 ||
        typeof body?.challengeId !== "string" || typeof body?.nonce !== "string")
      return Response.json({ error: "فریم نامعتبر است." }, { status: 400 });
    const challenge = await prisma.attendanceChallenge.findUnique({ where: { id: body.challengeId } });
    if (!challenge || challenge.userId !== user.id || challenge.status !== "OPEN" ||
        challenge.consumedAt || challenge.expiresAt <= new Date())
      return Response.json({ error: "چالش معتبر نیست." }, { status: 403 });
    const digest = createHash("sha256").update(body.nonce).digest();
    const expected = Buffer.from(challenge.nonceHash, "hex");
    if (expected.length !== digest.length || !timingSafeEqual(expected, digest))
      return Response.json({ error: "چالش معتبر نیست." }, { status: 403 });
    const data = await localFaceRequest("/v1/frames", {
      sessionToken: body.sessionToken, challengeId: body.challengeId,
      challengeNonce: body.nonce, subjectId: user.id, frameBase64: body.frameBase64,
    });
    return Response.json(data, { headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });
  } catch {
    return Response.json({ error: "ارسال فریم ناموفق بود." }, { status: 503 });
  }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\Ehsan\Desktop\sahand\app\api\attendance\local\session\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { assertSameOriginRequest, authenticateRequest } from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";
import { localFaceRequest } from "@/libs/attendance/local-face-client";
import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);
    const user = await authenticateRequest(request);
    const body = await request.json();
    if (typeof body?.challengeId !== "string" || typeof body?.nonce !== "string")
      return Response.json({ error: "درخواست نامعتبر است." }, { status: 400 });
    const challenge = await prisma.attendanceChallenge.findUnique({ where: { id: body.challengeId } });
    if (!challenge || challenge.userId !== user.id || challenge.status !== "OPEN" ||
        challenge.consumedAt || challenge.expiresAt <= new Date())
      return Response.json({ error: "چالش معتبر نیست." }, { status: 403 });
    const digest = createHash("sha256").update(body.nonce).digest();
    const expected = Buffer.from(challenge.nonceHash, "hex");
    if (expected.length !== digest.length || !timingSafeEqual(expected, digest))
      return Response.json({ error: "چالش معتبر نیست." }, { status: 403 });
    const data = await localFaceRequest<{ sessionToken: string; expiresInSeconds: number }>("/v1/sessions", {
      challengeId: challenge.id, challengeNonce: body.nonce,
      subjectId: user.id, action: challenge.action,
    });
    return Response.json(data, { headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });
  } catch {
    return Response.json({ error: "ایجاد نشست داخلی ناموفق بود." }, { status: 503 });
  }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\Ehsan\Desktop\sahand\app\api\attendance\shift-assignments\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";

import {
  Prisma,
  ShiftAssignmentTarget,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";

import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";

import {
  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  authorizeGlobalRequest,
  assertSameOriginRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

import { AuthError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.service";

import {
  PermissionDeniedError,
} from "@/modules/permissions/access-control";

// ==========================================
// TYPES
// ==========================================

class ShiftAssignmentError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public statusCode = 400
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ShiftAssignmentError";
  }
}

type AssignmentInput = {
  shiftId: string;
  targetType: ShiftAssignmentTarget;
  organizationUnitId: string | null;
  employeeAssignmentId: string | null;
  validFrom: Date;
  validTo: Date | null;
  notes: string | null;
};

// ==========================================
// HELPERS
// ==========================================

function isObject(
  value: unknown
): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

function parseString(
  value: unknown,
  field: string,
  maxLength = 200
): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  const result = value.trim();

  if (!result) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} الزامی است.`
    );
  }

  if (result.length > maxLength) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} بیش از حد مجاز طولانی است.`
    );
  }

  return result;
}

function parseOptionalString(
  value: unknown,
  field: string,
  maxLength = 1000
): string | null {
  if (
    value === undefined ||
    value === null ||
    value === ""
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  const result = value.trim();

  if (result.length > maxLength) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} بیش از حد مجاز طولانی است.`
    );
  }

  return result || null;
}

function parseDate(
  value: unknown,
  field: string,
  optional = false
): Date | null {
  if (
    value === undefined ||
    value === null ||
    value === ""
  ) {
    if (optional) return null;

    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} الزامی است.`
    );
  }

  if (
    typeof value !== "string" ||
    !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)
  ) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} باید با فرمت YYYY-MM-DD باشد.`
    );
  }

  const date = new Date(
    `${value}T00:00:00.000Z`
  );

  if (
    Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ||
    date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== value
  ) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  return date;
}

function parseAssignment(
  body: Record<string, unknown>
): AssignmentInput {
  const shiftId = parseString(
    body.shiftId,
    "شناسه شیفت",
    100
  );

  const targetType = body.targetType;

  if (
    typeof targetType !== "string" ||
    !Object.values(ShiftAssignmentTarget).includes(
      targetType as ShiftAssignmentTarget
    )
  ) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      "نوع اختصاص شیفت معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  const organizationUnitId =
    parseOptionalString(
      body.organizationUnitId,
      "واحد سازمانی",
      100
    );

  const employeeAssignmentId =
    parseOptionalString(
      body.employeeAssignmentId,
      "پرسنل",
      100
    );

  const validFrom = parseDate(
    body.validFrom,
    "تاریخ شروع"
  )!;

  const validTo = parseDate(
    body.validTo,
    "تاریخ پایان",
    true
  );

  if (
    validTo &&
    validTo.getTime() < validFrom.getTime()
  ) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      "تاریخ پایان نمی‌تواند قبل از تاریخ شروع باشد."
    );
  }

  if (
    targetType === ShiftAssignmentTarget.COMPANY &&
    (organizationUnitId || employeeAssignmentId)
  ) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      "برای اختصاص شیفت به کل شرکت، شناسه واحد و کارمند نباید ارسال شود."
    );
  }

  if (
    targetType === ShiftAssignmentTarget.UNIT &&
    (!organizationUnitId || employeeAssignmentId)
  ) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      "برای اختصاص شیفت به واحد، فقط شناسه واحد سازمانی الزامی است."
    );
  }

  if (
    targetType === ShiftAssignmentTarget.EMPLOYEE &&
    (!employeeAssignmentId || organizationUnitId)
  ) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      "برای اختصاص شیفت به کارمند، فقط شناسه انتصاب کارمند الزامی است."
    );
  }

  return {
    shiftId,
    targetType: targetType as ShiftAssignmentTarget,
    organizationUnitId,
    employeeAssignmentId,
    validFrom,
    validTo,
    notes: parseOptionalString(
      body.notes,
      "توضیحات"
    ),
  };
}

function getErrorResponse(error: unknown) {
  if (error instanceof ShiftAssignmentError) {
    return errorResponse(
      error.message,
      error.statusCode
    );
  }

  if (error instanceof AuthError) {
    return errorResponse(
      "احراز هویت ناموفق است.",
      401
    );
  }

  if (error instanceof PermissionDeniedError) {
    return errorResponse(
      "شما مجوز انجام این عملیات را ندارید.",
      403
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2002"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "اطلاعات تکراری است.",
      409
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2003"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "ارتباط با اطلاعات سازمانی معتبر نیست.",
      400
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2025"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "اطلاعات موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
      404
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2034"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "عملیات هم‌زمان دیگری انجام شده است. دوباره تلاش کنید.",
      409
    );
  }

  console.error(
    "[SHIFT_ASSIGNMENT_ERROR]",
    error
  );

  return errorResponse(
    "خطای داخلی در مدیریت اختصاص شیفت.",
    500
  );
}

function getClientIp(request: NextRequest) {
  return (
    request.headers
      .get("x-forwarded-for")
      ?.split(",")[0]
      ?.trim() ||
    request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
    null
  );
}

// ==========================================
// GET - LIST SHIFT ASSIGNMENTS
// ==========================================

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    await authorizeGlobalRequest(
      request,
      "attendance.shift.view"
    );

    const params =
      request.nextUrl.searchParams;

    const targetTypeParam =
      params.get("targetType");

    const shiftId =
      params.get("shiftId");

    const organizationUnitId =
      params.get("organizationUnitId");

    const employeeAssignmentId =
      params.get("employeeAssignmentId");

    const activeParam =
      params.get("active");

    const page = Number(
      params.get("page") || "1"
    );

    const pageSize = Number(
      params.get("pageSize") || "50"
    );

    if (
      !Number.isInteger(page) ||
      page < 1 ||
      page > 100000
    ) {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "شماره صفحه معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    if (
      !Number.isInteger(pageSize) ||
      pageSize < 1 ||
      pageSize > 100
    ) {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "تعداد رکورد در صفحه باید بین ۱ تا ۱۰۰ باشد."
      );
    }

    const where: Prisma.ShiftAssignmentWhereInput = {};

    if (shiftId) {
      where.shiftId = shiftId;
    }

    if (organizationUnitId) {
      where.organizationUnitId =
        organizationUnitId;
    }

    if (employeeAssignmentId) {
      where.employeeAssignmentId =
        employeeAssignmentId;
    }

    if (targetTypeParam) {
      if (
        !Object.values(
          ShiftAssignmentTarget
        ).includes(
          targetTypeParam as ShiftAssignmentTarget
        )
      ) {
        throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
          "نوع اختصاص شیفت معتبر نیست."
        );
      }

      where.targetType =
        targetTypeParam as ShiftAssignmentTarget;
    }

    if (activeParam === "true") {
      where.isActive = true;
    } else if (activeParam === "false") {
      where.isActive = false;
    } else if (
      activeParam !== null &&
      activeParam !== "all"
    ) {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "فیلتر وضعیت معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const [assignments, total] =
      await prisma.$transaction([
        prisma.shiftAssignment.findMany({
          where,

          include: {
            shift: {
              include: {
                days: {
                  orderBy: [
                    { dayOfWeek: "asc" },
                    { segmentOrder: "asc" },
                  ],
                },
              },
            },

            organizationUnit: {
              select: {
                id: true,
                code: true,
                name: true,
                status: true,
              },
            },

            employeeAssignment: {
              include: {
                user: {
                  select: {
                    id: true,
                    username: true,
                    fullName: true,
                    isActive: true,
                  },
                },

                organizationUnit: {
                  select: {
                    id: true,
                    code: true,
                    name: true,
                  },
                },

                position: {
                  select: {
                    id: true,
                    title: true,
                  },
                },
              },
            },
          },

          orderBy: [
            { validFrom: "desc" },
            { createdAt: "desc" },
          ],

          skip: (page - 1) * pageSize,
          take: pageSize,
        }),

        prisma.shiftAssignment.count({
          where,
        }),
      ]);

    return successResponse({
      assignments,

      pagination: {
        page,
        pageSize,
        total,
        totalPages: Math.ceil(
          total / pageSize
        ),
      },
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// POST - CREATE SHIFT ASSIGNMENT
// ==========================================

export async function POST(
  request: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const { user: actor } =
      await authorizeGlobalRequest(
        request,
        "attendance.shift.create"
      );

    let body: unknown;

    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "ساختار اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const input = parseAssignment(body);

    const assignment = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        // ----------------------------------
        // 1. Check shift
        // ----------------------------------

        const shift =
          await tx.workShift.findUnique({
            where: {
              id: input.shiftId,
            },
          });

        if (!shift) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "شیفت موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
            404
          );
        }

        if (!shift.isActive) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "امکان اختصاص شیفت غیرفعال وجود ندارد."
          );
        }

        // ----------------------------------
        // 2. Check organization target
        // ----------------------------------

        if (
          input.targetType ===
          ShiftAssignmentTarget.UNIT
        ) {
          const unit =
            await tx.organizationUnit.findUnique({
              where: {
                id: input.organizationUnitId!,
              },

              select: {
                id: true,
                status: true,
              },
            });

          if (!unit) {
            throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
              "واحد سازمانی موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
              404
            );
          }

          if (unit.status !== "ACTIVE") {
            throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
              "واحد سازمانی غیرفعال است."
            );
          }
        }

        // ----------------------------------
        // 3. Check employee target
        // ----------------------------------

        if (
          input.targetType ===
          ShiftAssignmentTarget.EMPLOYEE
        ) {
          const employee =
            await tx.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
              where: {
                id: input.employeeAssignmentId!,
              },

              include: {
                user: {
                  select: {
                    isActive: true,
                  },
                },
              },
            });

          if (!employee) {
            throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
              "انتصاب کارمند موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
              404
            );
          }

          if (
            employee.status !== "ACTIVE" ||
            !employee.user.isActive
          ) {
            throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
              "کارمند موردنظر فعال نیست."
            );
          }

          if (
            employee.endDate &&
            new Date(employee.endDate) <
              input.validFrom
          ) {
            throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
              "تاریخ شروع شیفت بعد از پایان انتصاب کارمند است."
            );
          }

          if (
            new Date(employee.startDate) >
            (input.validTo ?? input.validFrom)
          ) {
            throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
              "بازه اختصاص شیفت با تاریخ شروع انتصاب کارمند مطابقت ندارد."
            );
          }
        }

        // ----------------------------------
        // 4. Prevent overlapping assignments
        // ----------------------------------

        const targetWhere:
          Prisma.ShiftAssignmentWhereInput = {
            isActive: true,
            targetType: input.targetType,
          };

        if (
          input.targetType ===
          ShiftAssignmentTarget.UNIT
        ) {
          targetWhere.organizationUnitId =
            input.organizationUnitId;
        }

        if (
          input.targetType ===
          ShiftAssignmentTarget.EMPLOYEE
        ) {
          targetWhere.employeeAssignmentId =
            input.employeeAssignmentId;
        }

        const overlapWhere:
          Prisma.ShiftAssignmentWhereInput = {
            ...targetWhere,

            validFrom: {
              lte:
                input.validTo ??
                new Date("9999-12-31T00:00:00.000Z"),
            },

            OR: [
              {
                validTo: null,
              },
              {
                validTo: {
                  gte: input.validFrom,
                },
              },
            ],
          };

        const overlapping =
          await tx.shiftAssignment.findFirst({
            where: overlapWhere,

            select: {
              id: true,
              shiftId: true,
              validFrom: true,
              validTo: true,
            },
          });

        if (overlapping) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "برای این هدف در بازه زمانی انتخاب‌شده، شیفت فعال دیگری وجود دارد.",
            409
          );
        }

        // ----------------------------------
        // 5. Create assignment
        // ----------------------------------

        const created =
          await tx.shiftAssignment.create({
            data: {
              shiftId: input.shiftId,
              targetType: input.targetType,

              organizationUnitId:
                input.organizationUnitId,

              employeeAssignmentId:
                input.employeeAssignmentId,

              validFrom: input.validFrom,
              validTo: input.validTo,

              isActive: true,

              assignedById: actor.id,
              notes: input.notes,
            },

            include: {
              shift: {
                include: {
                  days: {
                    orderBy: [
                      { dayOfWeek: "asc" },
                      { segmentOrder: "asc" },
                    ],
                  },
                },
              },

              organizationUnit: {
                select: {
                  id: true,
                  code: true,
                  name: true,
                },
              },

              employeeAssignment: {
                include: {
                  user: {
                    select: {
                      id: true,
                      fullName: true,
                      username: true,
                    },
                  },
                },
              },
            },
          });

        // ----------------------------------
        // 6. Audit log
        // ----------------------------------

        await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
          data: {
            actorUserId: actor.id,

            entityType: "ShiftAssignment",
            entityId: created.id,

            action: "SHIFT_ASSIGNED",

            afterData: {
              shiftId: created.shiftId,
              shiftName: created.shift.name,

              targetType: created.targetType,

              organizationUnitId:
                created.organizationUnitId,

              employeeAssignmentId:
                created.employeeAssignmentId,

              validFrom: input.validFrom.toISOString(),

              validTo:
                input.validTo?.toISOString() ?? null,

              isActive: true,
            },

            ipAddress: getClientIp(request),
          },
        });

        return created;
      },
      {
        isolationLevel:
          Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
      }
    );

    return successResponse(
      {
        assignment,

        message:
          "شیفت با موفقیت به هدف موردنظر اختصاص داده شد.",
      },
      201
    );

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";

import {
  Prisma,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";

import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";

import {
  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  authorizeGlobalRequest,
  assertSameOriginRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

import { AuthError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.service";

import {
  PermissionDeniedError,
} from "@/modules/permissions/access-control";

// ==========================================
// TYPES
// ==========================================

type RouteContext = {
  params: Promise<{
    id: string;
  }>;
};

class ShiftAssignmentError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public statusCode = 400
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ShiftAssignmentError";
  }
}

// ==========================================
// HELPERS
// ==========================================

function isObject(
  value: unknown
): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

function parseString(
  value: unknown,
  field: string,
  maxLength = 200
): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  const result = value.trim();

  if (!result) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} الزامی است.`
    );
  }

  if (result.length > maxLength) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} بیش از حد مجاز طولانی است.`
    );
  }

  return result;
}

function parseOptionalString(
  value: unknown,
  field: string,
  maxLength = 1000
): string | null {
  if (
    value === null ||
    value === ""
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  const result = value.trim();

  if (result.length > maxLength) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} بیش از حد مجاز طولانی است.`
    );
  }

  return result || null;
}

function parseDate(
  value: unknown,
  field: string
): Date {
  if (
    typeof value !== "string" ||
    !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)
  ) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} باید با فرمت YYYY-MM-DD باشد.`
    );
  }

  const date = new Date(
    `${value}T00:00:00.000Z`
  );

  if (
    Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ||
    date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== value
  ) {
    throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  return date;
}

function getClientIp(request: NextRequest) {
  return (
    request.headers
      .get("x-forwarded-for")
      ?.split(",")[0]
      ?.trim() ||
    request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
    null
  );
}

function getErrorResponse(error: unknown) {
  if (error instanceof ShiftAssignmentError) {
    return errorResponse(
      error.message,
      error.statusCode
    );
  }

  if (error instanceof AuthError) {
    return errorResponse(
      "احراز هویت ناموفق است.",
      401
    );
  }

  if (error instanceof PermissionDeniedError) {
    return errorResponse(
      "شما مجوز انجام این عملیات را ندارید.",
      403
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2002"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "اطلاعات تکراری است.",
      409
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2003"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "ارتباط با اطلاعات موردنظر معتبر نیست.",
      400
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2025"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "اطلاعات موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
      404
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2034"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "تغییر هم‌زمان دیگری انجام شده است. دوباره تلاش کنید.",
      409
    );
  }

  console.error(
    "[SHIFT_ASSIGNMENT_DETAIL_ERROR]",
    error
  );

  return errorResponse(
    "خطای داخلی در مدیریت انتصاب شیفت.",
    500
  );
}

// ==========================================

const assignmentInclude = {
  shift: {
    include: {
      days: {
        orderBy: [
          { dayOfWeek: "asc" as const },
          { segmentOrder: "asc" as const },
        ],
      },
    },
  },

  organizationUnit: {
    select: {
      id: true,
      code: true,
      name: true,
      status: true,
    },
  },

  employeeAssignment: {
    include: {
      user: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          username: true,
          fullName: true,
          isActive: true,
        },
      },

      organizationUnit: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          code: true,
          name: true,
        },
      },

      position: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          title: true,
        },
      },
    },
  },
} satisfies Prisma.ShiftAssignmentInclude;

// ==========================================

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    await authorizeGlobalRequest(
      request,
      "attendance.shift.view"
    );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    if (!id?.trim()) {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "شناسه انتصاب معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const assignment =
      await prisma.shiftAssignment.findUnique({
        where: {
          id,
        },

        include: assignmentInclude,
      });

    if (!assignment) {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "انتصاب شیفت پیدا نشد.",
        404
      );

      assignment,
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// PATCH - UPDATE ASSIGNMENT
// ==========================================

export async function PATCH(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const { user: actor } =
      await authorizeGlobalRequest(
        request,
        "attendance.shift.update"
      );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    if (!id?.trim()) {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "شناسه انتصاب معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    let body: unknown;

    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "ساختار اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const updated = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const existing =
          await tx.shiftAssignment.findUnique({
            where: {
              id,
            },

            include: {
              shift: true,
            },
          });

        if (!existing) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "انتصاب شیفت پیدا نشد.",
            404
          );
        }

        if (!existing.isActive) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "انتصاب غیرفعال قابل ویرایش نیست."
          );
        }

        // ------------------------------
        // Parse update fields
        // ------------------------------

        let shiftId = existing.shiftId;

        if (body.shiftId !== undefined) {
          shiftId = parseString(
            body.shiftId,
            "شناسه شیفت",
            100
          );
        }

        let validFrom = existing.validFrom;

        if (body.validFrom !== undefined) {
          validFrom = parseDate(
            body.validFrom,
            "تاریخ شروع"
          );
        }

        let validTo = existing.validTo;

        if (body.validTo !== undefined) {
          validTo =
            body.validTo === null
              ? null
              : parseDate(
                  body.validTo,
                  "تاریخ پایان"
                );
        }

        let notes = existing.notes;

        if (body.notes !== undefined) {
          notes = parseOptionalString(
            body.notes,
            "توضیحات"
          );
        }

        if (
          validTo &&
          validTo.getTime() < validFrom.getTime()
        ) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "تاریخ پایان نمی‌تواند قبل از تاریخ شروع باشد."
          );
        }

        // ------------------------------
        // Check shift
        // ------------------------------

        const shift =
          await tx.workShift.findUnique({
            where: {
              id: shiftId,
            },
          });

        if (!shift) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "شیفت انتخاب‌شده پیدا نشد.",
            404
          );
        }

        if (!shift.isActive) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "امکان اختصاص شیفت غیرفعال وجود ندارد."
          );
        }

        // ------------------------------
        // Check target overlap
        // ------------------------------

        const targetWhere:
          Prisma.ShiftAssignmentWhereInput = {
            id: {
              not: id,
            },

            isActive: true,

            targetType: existing.targetType,
          };

        if (
          existing.targetType === "UNIT"
        ) {
          targetWhere.organizationUnitId =
            existing.organizationUnitId;
        }

        if (
          existing.targetType === "EMPLOYEE"
        ) {
          targetWhere.employeeAssignmentId =
            existing.employeeAssignmentId;
        }

        const overlapWhere:
          Prisma.ShiftAssignmentWhereInput = {
            ...targetWhere,

            validFrom: {
              lte:
                validTo ??
                new Date("9999-12-31T00:00:00.000Z"),
            },

            OR: [
              {
                validTo: null,
              },
              {
                validTo: {
                  gte: validFrom,
                },
              },
            ],
          };

        const overlapping =
          await tx.shiftAssignment.findFirst({
            where: overlapWhere,

            select: {
              id: true,
            },
          });

        if (overlapping) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "در بازه زمانی انتخاب‌شده، انتصاب فعال دیگری برای این هدف وجود دارد.",
            409
          );
        }

        // ------------------------------
        // Update
        // ------------------------------

        const result =
          await tx.shiftAssignment.update({
            where: {
              id,
            },

            data: {
              shiftId,
              validFrom,
              validTo,
              notes,
            },

            include: assignmentInclude,
          });

        // ------------------------------
        // Audit log
        // ------------------------------

        await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
          data: {
            actorUserId: actor.id,

            entityType: "ShiftAssignment",
            entityId: id,

            action: "SHIFT_ASSIGNMENT_UPDATED",

            beforeData: {
              shiftId: existing.shiftId,
              validFrom:
                existing.validFrom.toISOString(),

              validTo:
                existing.validTo?.toISOString() ?? null,

              notes: existing.notes,
            },

            afterData: {
              shiftId: result.shiftId,
              validFrom:
                result.validFrom.toISOString(),

              validTo:
                result.validTo?.toISOString() ?? null,

              notes: result.notes,
            },

            ipAddress: getClientIp(request),
          },
        });

        return result;
      },
      {
        isolationLevel:
          Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
      }
    );

    return successResponse({
      assignment: updated,

      message:
        "انتصاب شیفت با موفقیت ویرایش شد.",
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// DELETE - SOFT DEACTIVATE
// ==========================================

export async function DELETE(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const { user: actor } =
      await authorizeGlobalRequest(
        request,
        "attendance.shift.update"
      );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    if (!id?.trim()) {
      throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
        "شناسه انتصاب معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const result = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const existing =
          await tx.shiftAssignment.findUnique({
            where: {
              id,
            },
          });

        if (!existing) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "انتصاب شیفت پیدا نشد.",
            404
          );
        }

        if (!existing.isActive) {
          throw new ShiftAssignmentError(
            "این انتصاب قبلاً غیرفعال شده است."
          );
        }

        const updated =
          await tx.shiftAssignment.update({
            where: {
              id,
            },

            data: {
              isActive: false,
            },
          });

        await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
          data: {
            actorUserId: actor.id,

            entityType: "ShiftAssignment",
            entityId: id,

            action: "SHIFT_ASSIGNMENT_DEACTIVATED",

            beforeData: {
              shiftId: existing.shiftId,
              targetType: existing.targetType,
              validFrom:
                existing.validFrom.toISOString(),

              validTo:
                existing.validTo?.toISOString() ?? null,

              isActive: true,
            },

            afterData: {
              isActive: false,
            },

            ipAddress: getClientIp(request),
          },
        });

        return updated;
      }
    );

    return successResponse({
      assignment: result,

      message:
        "انتصاب شیفت غیرفعال شد و سوابق آن حفظ گردید.",
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}
