import { NextRequest } from "next/server";

import {
  DataScope,
  EmployeeShiftScheduleStatus as Status,
  Prisma,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";

import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { successResponse, errorResponse } from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  assertSameOriginRequest,
  authorizeRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

type Context = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

class SubmitError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public statusCode = 400
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

function respondError(error: unknown) {
  if (error instanceof SubmitError) {
    return errorResponse(error.message, error.statusCode);
  }

  if (error instanceof Error &&
      "statusCode" in error &&
      typeof error.statusCode === "number") {
    return errorResponse(error.message, error.statusCode);
  }

  if (error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
      error.code === "P2034") {
    return errorResponse(
      "درخواست هم‌زمان دیگری ثبت شده است. دوباره تلاش کنید.",
      409
    );
  }

  console.error("[WEEKLY_SCHEDULE_SUBMIT]", error);
  return errorResponse("خطا در ارسال برنامه برای بررسی.", 500);
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest, context: Context) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const auth = await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "attendance.shift.update"
    );

    if (!auth.permission.scopes.includes(DataScope.ALL)) {
      throw new SubmitError(
        "برای ارسال برنامه مجوز سراسری لازم است.",
        403
      );
    }

    const { id } = await context.params;

    if (!id) {
      throw new SubmitError("شناسه برنامه الزامی است.");
    }

    const result = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const locked = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ id: string }>>`
          SELECT id FROM "EmployeeShiftSchedule"
          WHERE id = ${id}
          FOR UPDATE
        `;

        if (locked.length === 0) {
          throw new SubmitError("برنامه پیدا نشد.", 404);
        }

        const current = await tx.employeeShiftSchedule.findUnique({
          where: { id },
          include: {
            segments: true,
          },
        });

        if (!current) {
          throw new SubmitError("برنامه پیدا نشد.", 404);
        }

        if (current.createdByUserId !== auth.user.id) {
          throw new SubmitError(
            "فقط ایجادکننده برنامه می‌تواند آن را ارسال کند.",
            403
          );
        }

        if (current.status !== Status.DRAFT) {
          throw new SubmitError(
            "فقط برنامه پیش‌نویس قابل ارسال است.",
            409
          );
        }

        const latest = await tx.employeeShiftSchedule.findFirst({
          where: {
            employeeUserId: current.employeeUserId,
            weekStartDate: current.weekStartDate,
          },
          orderBy: { version: "desc" },
          select: { id: true },
        });

        if (latest?.id !== id) {
          throw new SubmitError(
            "این برنامه آخرین نسخه نیست.",
            409
          );
        }

        if (current.segments.length === 0) {
          throw new SubmitError(
            "برنامه بدون بازه کاری قابل ارسال نیست."
          );
        }

        const updated = await tx.employeeShiftSchedule.update({
          where: { id },
          data: {
            status: Status.SUBMITTED,
            submittedByUserId: auth.user.id,
            submittedAt: new Date(),
          },
          include: {
            segments: {
              orderBy: [
                { workDate: "asc" },
                { segmentOrder: "asc" },
              ],
            },
          },
        });

        await tx.employeeShiftScheduleRevision.create({
          data: {
            scheduleId: id,
            actorUserId: auth.user.id,
            action: "SUBMITTED",
            beforeData: {
              status: current.status,
            },
            afterData: {
              status: updated.status,
              submittedByUserId: auth.user.id,
              submittedAt: updated.submittedAt?.toISOString(),
              segmentCount: updated.segments.length,
            },
          },
        });

        return updated;
      },
      {
        isolationLevel: Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
        timeout: 10000,
      }
    );

    return successResponse({
      schedule: result,
      message: "برنامه برای بررسی منابع انسانی ارسال شد.",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return respondError(error);
  }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\Ehsan\Desktop\sahand\app\api\organization\assignments\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";

import {
  EmployeeAssignmentType,
  EmployeeAssignmentStatus,
  UserAccessLevel,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";

import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";

import {
  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  assertSameOriginRequest,
  authorizeGlobalRequest,
  RequestOriginError,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

import { AuthError } from "@/modules/auth/auth.service";

import {
  PermissionDeniedError,
} from "@/modules/permissions/access-control";

// ==========================================
// TYPES
// ==========================================

class AssignmentError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public statusCode = 400
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "AssignmentError";
  }
}

type AssignmentInput = {
  userId: string;
  organizationUnitId: string;
  positionId: string | null;
  responsibilityTitle: string | null;
  locationId: string | null;
  managerAssignmentId: string | null;
  assignmentType: EmployeeAssignmentType;
  startDate: Date;
  endDate: Date | null;
  notes: string | null;
};

// ==========================================
// HELPERS
// ==========================================

function isObject(
  value: unknown
): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

function readString(
  value: unknown,
  field: string,
  required = true
): string | null {
  if (
    value === undefined ||
    value === null ||
    value === ""
  ) {
    if (required) {
      throw new AssignmentError(
        `${field} الزامی است.`
      );
    }

    return null;
  }

  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  const result = value.trim();

  if (!result && required) {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      `${field} الزامی است.`
    );
  }

  return result || null;
}

function readDate(
  value: unknown,
  field: string,
  required = true
): Date | null {
  if (
    value === undefined ||
    value === null ||
    value === ""
  ) {
    if (required) {
      throw new AssignmentError(
        `${field} الزامی است.`
      );
    }

    return null;
  }

  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      `${field} معتبر نیست.`
    );
  }

  return date;
}

function getIpAddress(request: NextRequest) {
  return (
    request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")
      ?.split(",")[0]
      ?.trim() ||
    request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
    null
  );
}

function getErrorResponse(error: unknown) {
  if (
    error instanceof AssignmentError ||
    error instanceof RequestOriginError ||
    error instanceof AuthError ||
    error instanceof PermissionDeniedError
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      error.message,
      error.statusCode
    );
  }

  if (
    isObject(error) &&
    error.code === "P2002"
  ) {
    return errorResponse(
      "این اطلاعات قبلاً ثبت شده است.",
      409
    );
  }

  console.error(
    "[ORGANIZATION_ASSIGNMENT_ERROR]",
    error
  );

  return errorResponse(
    "خطای داخلی در پردازش انتصاب کارکنان.",
    500
  );
}

// ==========================================
// PARSE INPUT
// ==========================================

function parseAssignmentInput(
  body: Record<string, unknown>
): AssignmentInput {
  const userId = readString(
    body.userId,
    "کارمند"
  )!;

  const organizationUnitId = readString(
    body.organizationUnitId,
    "واحد سازمانی"
  )!;

  const positionId = readString(
    body.positionId,
    "سمت",
    false
  );

  const responsibilityTitle = readString(
    body.responsibilityTitle,
    "عنوان مسئولیت",
    false
  );

  // باید دقیقاً یکی از دو روش انتخاب شود.
  if (
    Boolean(positionId) ===
    Boolean(responsibilityTitle)
  ) {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      "یک سمت رسمی یا یک عنوان مسئولیت سفارشی انتخاب کنید."
    );
  }

  if (
    responsibilityTitle &&
    (
      responsibilityTitle.length < 2 ||
      responsibilityTitle.length > 120
    )
  ) {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      "عنوان مسئولیت باید بین ۲ تا ۱۲۰ کاراکتر باشد."
    );
  }

  const locationId = readString(
    body.locationId,
    "محل خدمت",
    false
  );

  const managerAssignmentId = readString(
    body.managerAssignmentId,
    "مدیر مستقیم",
    false
  );

  const allowedTypes = Object.values(
    EmployeeAssignmentType
  ) as string[];

  const assignmentType =
    body.assignmentType === undefined
      ? EmployeeAssignmentType.PRIMARY
      : body.assignmentType;

  if (
    typeof assignmentType !== "string" ||
    !allowedTypes.includes(assignmentType)
  ) {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      "نوع انتصاب معتبر نیست."
    );
  }

  const isPrimary =
    assignmentType ===
    EmployeeAssignmentType.PRIMARY;

  if (
    body.isPrimary !== undefined &&
    body.isPrimary !== isPrimary
  ) {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      "نوع انتصاب و وضعیت مسئولیت اصلی با هم مطابقت ندارند."
    );
  }

  const startDate = readDate(
    body.startDate,
    "تاریخ شروع"
  )!;

  const endDate = readDate(
    body.endDate,
    "تاریخ پایان",
    false
  );

  if (
    endDate &&
    endDate < startDate
  ) {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      "تاریخ پایان نمی‌تواند قبل از تاریخ شروع باشد."
    );
  }

  const notes = readString(
    body.notes,
    "توضیحات",
    false
  );

  if (notes && notes.length > 2000) {
    throw new AssignmentError(
      "توضیحات نمی‌تواند بیشتر از ۲۰۰۰ کاراکتر باشد."
    );
  }

  return {
    userId,
    organizationUnitId,
    positionId,
    responsibilityTitle,
    locationId,
    managerAssignmentId,
    assignmentType:
      assignmentType as EmployeeAssignmentType,
    startDate,
    endDate,
    notes,
  };
}

// ==========================================
// GET - LIST ASSIGNMENTS
// ==========================================

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    await authorizeGlobalRequest(
      request,
      "organization.chart.view"
    );

    const assignments =
      await prisma.employeeAssignment.findMany({
        include: {
          user: {
            select: {
              id: true,
              username: true,
              fullName: true,
              isActive: true,
            },
          },

          organizationUnit: {
            select: {
              id: true,
              code: true,
              name: true,
              parentId: true,
            },
          },

          position: {
            select: {
              id: true,
              code: true,
              title: true,
              level: true,
              isManagerial: true,
            },
          },

          location: {
            select: {
              id: true,
              code: true,
              name: true,
              city: true,
            },
          },

          managerAssignment: {
            select: {
              id: true,
              user: {
                select: {
                  id: true,
                  fullName: true,
                },
              },
              position: {
                select: {
                  title: true,
                },
              },
              responsibilityTitle: true,
              organizationUnit: {
                select: {
                  name: true,
                },
              },
            },
          },
        },

        orderBy: [
          { isPrimary: "desc" },
          { startDate: "desc" },
          { createdAt: "desc" },
        ],
      });

    return successResponse({
      assignments,
      count: assignments.length,
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// POST - CREATE ASSIGNMENT
// ==========================================

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    // 1. بررسی مبدأ درخواست
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    // 2. بررسی مجوز سراسری مدیریت انتصاب‌ها
    const { user: actor } =
      await authorizeGlobalRequest(
        request,
        "organization.assignments.manage"
      );

    // 3. خواندن اطلاعات
    let body: unknown;

    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      throw new AssignmentError(
        "ساختار درخواست معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      throw new AssignmentError(
        "اطلاعات انتصاب معتبر نیست."
      );
    }

    const input =
      parseAssignmentInput(body);

    // 4. بررسی کارمند
    const employee = await prisma.user.findFirst({
      where: {
        id: input.userId,
        isActive: true,
        accessLevel: UserAccessLevel.ERP,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
        username: true,
        fullName: true,
      },
    });

    if (!employee) {
      throw new AssignmentError(
        "کارمند فعال ERP پیدا نشد.",
        404
      );
    }

    // 5. بررسی واحد سازمانی
    const unit =
      await prisma.organizationUnit.findFirst({
        where: {
          id: input.organizationUnitId,
          status: "ACTIVE",
        },

        select: {
          id: true,
          name: true,
        },
      });

    if (!unit) {
      throw new AssignmentError(
        "واحد سازمانی پیدا نشد یا غیرفعال است.",
        404
      );
    }

    // 6. بررسی سمت رسمی
    if (input.positionId) {
      const position =
        await prisma.organizationPosition.findFirst({
          where: {
            id: input.positionId,
            isActive: true,
          },

          select: {
            id: true,
            title: true,
          },
        });

      if (!position) {
        throw new AssignmentError(
          "سمت انتخاب‌شده پیدا نشد یا غیرفعال است.",
          404
        );
      }
    }

    // 7. بررسی محل خدمت
    if (input.locationId) {
      const location =
        await prisma.organizationLocation.findFirst({
          where: {
            id: input.locationId,
            status: "ACTIVE",
          },

          select: {
            id: true,
          },
        });

      if (!location) {
        throw new AssignmentError(
          "محل خدمت انتخاب‌شده پیدا نشد یا غیرفعال است.",
          404
        );
      }
    }

    // 8. بررسی مدیر مستقیم
    if (input.managerAssignmentId) {
      const manager =
        await prisma.employeeAssignment.findFirst({
          where: {
            id: input.managerAssignmentId,
            status: EmployeeAssignmentStatus.ACTIVE,
            OR: [
              { endDate: null },
              { endDate: { gt: new Date() } },
            ],
          },

          select: {
            id: true,
            userId: true,
          },
        });

      if (!manager) {
        throw new AssignmentError(
          "انتصاب مدیر مستقیم معتبر یا فعال نیست.",
          404
        );
      }

      if (manager.userId === employee.id) {
        throw new AssignmentError(
          "یک کارمند نمی‌تواند مدیر مستقیم مسئولیت خودش باشد."
        );
      }
    }

    // 9. جلوگیری از ثبت چند مسئولیت اصلی فعال
    if (
      input.assignmentType ===
      EmployeeAssignmentType.PRIMARY
    ) {
      const existingPrimary =
        await prisma.employeeAssignment.findFirst({
          where: {
            userId: employee.id,
            status: EmployeeAssignmentStatus.ACTIVE,
            isPrimary: true,

            OR: [
              { endDate: null },
              { endDate: { gt: new Date() } },
            ],
          },

          select: {
            id: true,
            responsibilityTitle: true,
            position: {
              select: {
                title: true,
              },
            },
            organizationUnit: {
              select: {
                name: true,
              },
            },
          },
        });

      if (existingPrimary) {
        const currentTitle =
          existingPrimary.position?.title ||
          existingPrimary.responsibilityTitle ||
          "مسئولیت اصلی قبلی";

        throw new AssignmentError(
          `این کارمند قبلاً مسئولیت اصلی «${currentTitle}» در واحد «${existingPrimary.organizationUnit.name}» را دارد. ابتدا مسئولیت قبلی را تعیین تکلیف کنید.`,
          409
        );
      }
    }

    // 10. ثبت انتصاب و تاریخچه در یک تراکنش
    const created = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const assignment =
          await tx.employeeAssignment.create({
            data: {
              userId: input.userId,

              organizationUnitId:
                input.organizationUnitId,

              positionId: input.positionId,

              responsibilityTitle:
                input.responsibilityTitle,

              locationId: input.locationId,

              managerAssignmentId:
                input.managerAssignmentId,

              assignmentType:
                input.assignmentType,

              isPrimary:
                input.assignmentType ===
                EmployeeAssignmentType.PRIMARY,

              status:
                EmployeeAssignmentStatus.ACTIVE,

              startDate: input.startDate,

              endDate: input.endDate,

              notes: input.notes,
            },
          });

        // تاریخچه تغییرات انتصاب
        await tx.organizationAssignmentHistory.create({
          data: {
            assignmentId: assignment.id,

            action: "ASSIGNMENT_CREATED",

            changedByUserId: actor.id,

            newData: {
              userId: input.userId,
              organizationUnitId:
                input.organizationUnitId,
              positionId: input.positionId,
              responsibilityTitle:
                input.responsibilityTitle,
              locationId: input.locationId,
              managerAssignmentId:
                input.managerAssignmentId,
              assignmentType:
                input.assignmentType,
              isPrimary:
                assignment.isPrimary,
              startDate:
                input.startDate.toISOString(),
              endDate:
                input.endDate?.toISOString() ?? null,
            },
          },
        });

        // ثبت گزارش حسابرسی
        await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
          data: {
            actorUserId: actor.id,

            entityType: "EmployeeAssignment",

            entityId: assignment.id,

            action: "EMPLOYEE_ASSIGNMENT_CREATED",

            ipAddress: getIpAddress(request),

            afterData: {
              userId: input.userId,
              employeeName: employee.fullName,
              organizationUnitId:
                input.organizationUnitId,
              organizationUnitName: unit.name,
              positionId: input.positionId,
              responsibilityTitle:
                input.responsibilityTitle,
              assignmentType:
                input.assignmentType,
              isPrimary:
                assignment.isPrimary,
            },
          },
        });

        return tx.employeeAssignment.findUniqueOrThrow({
          where: {
            id: assignment.id,
          },

          include: {
            user: {
              select: {
                id: true,
                username: true,
                fullName: true,
              },
            },

            organizationUnit: {
              select: {
                id: true,
                name: true,
                code: true,
              },
            },

            position: {
              select: {
                id: true,
                title: true,
                code: true,
              },
            },

            location: {
              select: {
                id: true,
                name: true,
                code: true,
              },
            },
          },
        });
      }
    );

    return successResponse(
      {
        assignment: created,
        message:
          "انتصاب کارمند با موفقیت ثبت شد.",
      },
      201
    );

  } catch (error) {
    return getErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";

import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";

import {
  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  assertSameOriginRequest,
  authorizeRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

type Context = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

function getStatus(error: unknown) {
  if (
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    "statusCode" in error &&
    typeof error.statusCode === "number"
  ) {
    return error.statusCode;
  }

  return 500;
}

function getIp(request: NextRequest) {
  return (
    request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
    request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")
      ?.split(",")[0]
      .trim() ||
    undefined
  );
}

function isObject(
  value: unknown
): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

// ==========================================
// GET - GET ASSIGNMENT
// ==========================================

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: Context
) {
  try {
    await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "organization.chart.view"
    );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    const assignment =
      await prisma.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
        where: { id },

        include: {
          user: {
            select: {
              id: true,
              username: true,
              fullName: true,
            },
          },

          organizationUnit: true,

          position: true,

          location: true,

          managerAssignment: {
            select: {
              id: true,

              user: {
                select: {
                  fullName: true,
                },
              },

              position: {
                select: {
                  title: true,
                },
              },

              responsibilityTitle: true,
            },
          },

          roles: {
            include: {
              role: true,
            },
          },

          permissions: {
            include: {
              permission: true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!assignment) {
      return errorResponse(
        "انتصاب پیدا نشد.",
        404
      );
    }

    return successResponse({
      assignment,
    });

  } catch (error) {
    const status = getStatus(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "دسترسی مجاز نیست.",
        status
      );
    }

    console.error("[ASSIGNMENT_GET]", error);

    return errorResponse(
      "خطا در دریافت انتصاب.",
      500
    );
  }
}

// ==========================================
// PATCH - UPDATE ASSIGNMENT
// ==========================================

export async function PATCH(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: Context
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const authorization =
      await authorizeRequest(
        request,
        "organization.assignments.manage"
      );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    let body: unknown;

    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      return errorResponse(
        "اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست.",
        400
      );
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      return errorResponse(
        "ساختار اطلاعات معتبر نیست.",
        400
      );
    }

    // فقط فیلدهای مجاز
    const allowed = [
      "organizationUnitId",
      "positionId",
      "responsibilityTitle",
      "locationId",
      "managerAssignmentId",
      "assignmentType",
      "isPrimary",
      "startDate",
      "endDate",
      "notes",
      "status",
    ];

    if (
      Object.keys(body).some(
        (key) => !allowed.includes(key)
      )
    ) {
      return errorResponse(
        "فیلد غیرمجاز ارسال شده است.",
        400
      );
    }

    // اعتبارسنجی مسئولیت اصلی
    if (
      body.isPrimary !== undefined &&
      typeof body.isPrimary !== "boolean"
    ) {
      return errorResponse(
        "مقدار انتصاب اصلی معتبر نیست.",
        400
      );
    }

    // اعتبارسنجی توضیحات
    if (
      body.notes !== undefined &&
      body.notes !== null &&
      typeof body.notes !== "string"
    ) {
      return errorResponse(
        "یادداشت معتبر نیست.",
        400
      );
    }

    if (
      typeof body.notes === "string" &&
      body.notes.length > 2000
    ) {
      return errorResponse(
        "یادداشت نمی‌تواند بیشتر از ۲۰۰۰ کاراکتر باشد.",
        400
      );
    }

    // اعتبارسنجی تاریخ‌ها
    const dateFields = [
      "startDate",
      "endDate",
    ];

    for (const field of dateFields) {
      const value = body[field];

      if (value === undefined || value === null) {
        continue;
      }

      if (
        typeof value !== "string" ||
        Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value))
      ) {
        return errorResponse(
          `تاریخ ${field} معتبر نیست.`,
          400
        );
      }
    }

    // اعتبارسنجی وضعیت
    if (
      body.status !== undefined &&
      !["ACTIVE", "ENDED", "SUSPENDED"].includes(
        body.status as string
      )
    ) {
      return errorResponse(
        "وضعیت انتصاب معتبر نیست.",
        400
      );
    }

    // اعتبارسنجی نوع انتصاب
    if (
      body.assignmentType !== undefined &&
      ![
        "PRIMARY",
        "SECONDARY",
        "TEMPORARY",
        "PROJECT",
      ].includes(body.assignmentType as string)
    ) {
      return errorResponse(
        "نوع انتصاب معتبر نیست.",
        400
      );
    }

    const result = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        // دریافت انتصاب فعلی
        const existing =
          await tx.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
            where: { id },
          });

        if (!existing) {
          throw new Error("ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND");
        }

        // ==================================
        // دریافت مقادیر نهایی
        // ==================================

        const organizationUnitId =
          body.organizationUnitId !== undefined
            ? body.organizationUnitId as string
            : existing.organizationUnitId;

        const positionId =
          body.positionId !== undefined
            ? body.positionId as string | null
            : existing.positionId;

        const responsibilityTitle =
          body.responsibilityTitle !== undefined
            ? body.responsibilityTitle as string | null
            : existing.responsibilityTitle;

        const locationId =
          body.locationId !== undefined
            ? body.locationId as string | null
            : existing.locationId;

        // ==================================
        // اعتبارسنجی شناسه‌ها
        // ==================================

        if (
          body.organizationUnitId !== undefined &&
          typeof body.organizationUnitId !== "string"
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_UNIT");
        }

        if (
          body.positionId !== undefined &&
          body.positionId !== null &&
          typeof body.positionId !== "string"
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_POSITION");
        }

        if (
          body.responsibilityTitle !== undefined &&
          body.responsibilityTitle !== null &&
          typeof body.responsibilityTitle !== "string"
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_RESPONSIBILITY");
        }

        if (
          body.locationId !== undefined &&
          body.locationId !== null &&
          typeof body.locationId !== "string"
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_LOCATION");
        }

        // ==================================
        // اعتبارسنجی عنوان مسئولیت
        // ==================================

        const normalizedTitle =
          responsibilityTitle?.trim() || null;

        if (
          normalizedTitle &&
          (
            normalizedTitle.length < 2 ||
            normalizedTitle.length > 120
          )
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_RESPONSIBILITY");
        }

        // باید سمت رسمی یا عنوان سفارشی وجود داشته باشد
        if (
          Boolean(positionId) ===
          Boolean(normalizedTitle)
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_ASSIGNMENT_TITLE");
        }

        // ==================================
        // بررسی واحد، سمت و محل خدمت
        // ==================================

        const [unit, position, location] =
          await Promise.all([
            tx.organizationUnit.findUnique({
              where: {
                id: organizationUnitId,
              },
            }),

            positionId
              ? tx.organizationPosition.findUnique({
                  where: {
                    id: positionId,
                  },
                })
              : Promise.resolve(null),

            locationId
              ? tx.organizationLocation.findUnique({
                  where: {
                    id: locationId,
                  },
                })
              : Promise.resolve(null),
          ]);

        if (!unit || unit.status !== "ACTIVE") {
          throw new Error("INVALID_UNIT");
        }

        if (
          positionId &&
          (!position || !position.isActive)
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_POSITION");
        }

        if (
          locationId &&
          (!location || location.status !== "ACTIVE")
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_LOCATION");
        }

        // ==================================
        // بررسی مدیر مستقیم
        // ==================================

        const managerAssignmentId =
          body.managerAssignmentId !== undefined
            ? body.managerAssignmentId as string | null
            : existing.managerAssignmentId;

        if (
          body.managerAssignmentId !== undefined &&
          managerAssignmentId !== null &&
          typeof body.managerAssignmentId !== "string"
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_MANAGER");
        }

        if (managerAssignmentId) {
          if (managerAssignmentId === id) {
            throw new Error("INVALID_MANAGER");
          }

          const manager =
            await tx.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
              where: {
                id: managerAssignmentId,
              },
            });

          if (
            !manager ||
            manager.status !== "ACTIVE" ||
            (
              manager.endDate &&
              manager.endDate <= new Date()
            )
          ) {
            throw new Error("INVALID_MANAGER");
          }
        }

        // ==================================
        // بررسی تاریخ‌ها
        // ==================================

        const startDate =
          body.startDate !== undefined
            ? new Date(body.startDate as string)
            : existing.startDate;

        const endDate =
          body.endDate !== undefined
            ? body.endDate === null
              ? null
              : new Date(body.endDate as string)
            : existing.endDate;

        if (endDate && endDate < startDate) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_DATE_RANGE");
        }

        // ==================================
        // تعیین وضعیت مسئولیت اصلی
        // ==================================

        const assignmentType =
          body.assignmentType !== undefined
            ? body.assignmentType as
                | "PRIMARY"
                | "SECONDARY"
                | "TEMPORARY"
                | "PROJECT"
            : existing.assignmentType;

        
const isPrimary: boolean =
  body.isPrimary !== undefined
    ? (body.isPrimary as boolean)
    : body.assignmentType !== undefined
      ? assignmentType === "PRIMARY"
      : existing.isPrimary;


        if (
          isPrimary &&
          assignmentType !== "PRIMARY"
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_PRIMARY_TYPE");
        }

        if (
          !isPrimary &&
          assignmentType === "PRIMARY"
        ) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_PRIMARY_TYPE");
        }

        // جلوگیری از چند مسئولیت اصلی فعال
        const nextStatus =
          body.status !== undefined
            ? body.status as
                | "ACTIVE"
                | "ENDED"
                | "SUSPENDED"
            : existing.status;

        if (
          nextStatus === "ACTIVE" &&
          isPrimary
        ) {
          const existingPrimary =
            await tx.employeeAssignment.findFirst({
              where: {
                userId: existing.userId,
                id: {
                  not: id,
                },
                status: "ACTIVE",
                isPrimary: true,
                OR: [
                  { endDate: null },
                  { endDate: { gt: new Date() } },
                ],
              },
            });

          if (existingPrimary) {
            throw new Error("PRIMARY_EXISTS");
          }
        }

        // ==================================
        // به‌روزرسانی انتصاب
        // ==================================

        const updated =
          await tx.employeeAssignment.update({
            where: { id },

            
data: {
  ...(body.organizationUnitId !== undefined
    ? {
        organizationUnit: {
          connect: {
            id: organizationUnitId,
          },
        },
      }
    : {}),

  ...(body.positionId !== undefined
    ? positionId
      ? {
          position: {
            connect: {
              id: positionId,
            },
          },
        }
      : {
          position: {
            disconnect: true,
          },
        }
    : {}),

  ...(body.responsibilityTitle !== undefined
    ? {
        responsibilityTitle: normalizedTitle,
      }
    : {}),

  ...(body.locationId !== undefined
    ? locationId
      ? {
          location: {
            connect: {
              id: locationId,
            },
          },
        }
      : {
          location: {
            disconnect: true,
          },
        }
    : {}),

  ...(body.managerAssignmentId !== undefined
    ? managerAssignmentId
      ? {
          managerAssignment: {
            connect: {
              id: managerAssignmentId,
            },
          },
        }
      : {
          managerAssignment: {
            disconnect: true,
          },
        }
    : {}),

  ...(body.assignmentType !== undefined ||
  body.isPrimary !== undefined
    ? {
        assignmentType,
        isPrimary,
      }
    : {}),

  ...(body.startDate !== undefined
    ? { startDate }
    : {}),

  ...(body.endDate !== undefined
    ? { endDate }
    : {}),

  ...(body.notes !== undefined
    ? {
        notes: body.notes as string | null,
      }
    : {}),

  ...(body.status !== undefined
    ? { status: nextStatus }
    : {}),
},

          });

        // ==================================
        // ثبت تاریخچه
        // ==================================

        await tx.organizationAssignmentHistory.create({
          data: {
            assignmentId: id,

            action: "ASSIGNMENT_UPDATED",

            previousData: {
              organizationUnitId:
                existing.organizationUnitId,
              positionId: existing.positionId,
              responsibilityTitle:
                existing.responsibilityTitle,
              locationId: existing.locationId,
              managerAssignmentId:
                existing.managerAssignmentId,
              status: existing.status,
              isPrimary: existing.isPrimary,
              assignmentType:
                existing.assignmentType,
              startDate:
                existing.startDate.toISOString(),
              endDate:
                existing.endDate?.toISOString() ?? null,
            },

            newData: {
              organizationUnitId:
                updated.organizationUnitId,
              positionId: updated.positionId,
              responsibilityTitle:
                updated.responsibilityTitle,
              locationId: updated.locationId,
              managerAssignmentId:
                updated.managerAssignmentId,
              status: updated.status,
              isPrimary: updated.isPrimary,
              assignmentType:
                updated.assignmentType,
              startDate:
                updated.startDate.toISOString(),
              endDate:
                updated.endDate?.toISOString() ?? null,
            },

            changedByUserId:
              authorization.user.id,
          },
        });

        // ==================================
        // ثبت گزارش حسابرسی
        // ==================================

        await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
          data: {
            actorUserId: authorization.user.id,

            entityType: "EmployeeAssignment",

            entityId: id,

            action: "EMPLOYEE_ASSIGNMENT_UPDATED",

            ipAddress: getIp(request),

            beforeData: existing,

            afterData: updated,
          },
        });

        return updated;
      }
    );

    return successResponse({
      assignment: result,

      message:
        "انتصاب با موفقیت به‌روزرسانی شد.",
    });

  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      const messages: Record<string, string> = {
        ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND:
          "انتصاب پیدا نشد.",

        INVALID_UNIT:
          "واحد سازمانی معتبر یا فعال نیست.",

        INVALID_POSITION:
          "سمت سازمانی معتبر یا فعال نیست.",

        INVALID_RESPONSIBILITY:
          "عنوان مسئولیت سفارشی معتبر نیست.",

        INVALID_ASSIGNMENT_TITLE:
          "باید دقیقاً یک سمت رسمی یا عنوان مسئولیت سفارشی انتخاب شود.",

        INVALID_LOCATION:
          "محل سازمانی معتبر یا فعال نیست.",

        INVALID_MANAGER:
          "مسئول بالادستی معتبر نیست.",

        INVALID_DATE_RANGE:
          "تاریخ پایان نمی‌تواند قبل از تاریخ شروع باشد.",

        INVALID_PRIMARY_TYPE:
          "نوع انتصاب و وضعیت مسئولیت اصلی با هم مطابقت ندارند.",

        PRIMARY_EXISTS:
          "این کارمند قبلاً یک مسئولیت اصلی فعال دارد.",
      };

      if (messages[error.message]) {
        return errorResponse(
          messages[error.message],
          error.message === "ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND"
            ? 404
            : 400
        );
      }
    }

    const status = getStatus(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "درخواست معتبر نیست.",
        status
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[ASSIGNMENT_PATCH]",
      error
    );

    return errorResponse(
      "خطا در ویرایش انتصاب.",
      500
    );
  }
}

// ==========================================
// DELETE - END ASSIGNMENT
// ==========================================

export async function DELETE(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: Context
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const authorization =
      await authorizeRequest(
        request,
        "organization.assignments.manage"
      );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    const result = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const existing =
          await tx.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
            where: { id },
          });

        if (!existing) {
          throw new Error("ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND");
        }

        if (existing.status === "ENDED") {
          throw new Error("ASSIGNMENT_ALREADY_ENDED");
        }

        const updated =
          await tx.employeeAssignment.update({
            where: { id },

            data: {
              status: "ENDED",
              endDate: new Date(),
              isPrimary: false,
            },
          });

        await tx.organizationAssignmentHistory.create({
          data: {
            assignmentId: id,

            action: "ASSIGNMENT_ENDED",

            previousData: existing,

            newData: updated,

            changedByUserId:
              authorization.user.id,
          },
        });

        await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
          data: {
            actorUserId: authorization.user.id,

            entityType: "EmployeeAssignment",

            entityId: id,

            action: "EMPLOYEE_ASSIGNMENT_ENDED",

            ipAddress: getIp(request),

            beforeData: existing,

            afterData: updated,
          },
        });

        return updated;
      }
    );

    return successResponse({
      assignment: result,

      message:
        "انتصاب با موفقیت پایان یافت.",
    });

  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      if (
        error.message === "ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND"
      ) {
        return errorResponse(
          "انتصاب پیدا نشد.",
          404
        );
      }

      if (
        error.message === "ASSIGNMENT_ALREADY_ENDED"
      ) {
        return errorResponse(
          "این انتصاب قبلاً پایان یافته است.",
          409
        );
      }
    }

    const status = getStatus(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "درخواست معتبر نیست.",
        status
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[ASSIGNMENT_DELETE]",
      error
    );

    return errorResponse(
      "خطا در پایان انتصاب.",
      500
    );
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import {
  audit,
  jsonError,
  OrganizationApiError,
  requireOrganizationAccess,
  parseDate,
} from "@/modules/organization/organization-api";

type Context = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

const VALID_SCOPES = [
  "ALL",
  "OWN",
  "UNIT",
  "SUBTREE",
  "DEPARTMENT",
  "ASSIGNED",
] as const;

// GET - ACCESS LIST
export async function GET(_request: NextRequest, context: Context) {
  try {
    await requireOrganizationAccess("organization.access.view");
    const { id } = await context.params;

    const assignment = await prisma.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
      where: { id },
      select: {
        id: true,
        roles: {
          where: { isActive: true },
          include: {
            role: {
              select: {
                id: true,
                key: true,
                name: true,
                isSystem: true,
              },
            },
          },
          orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
        },
        permissions: {
          where: { isActive: true },
          include: {
            permission: {
              select: {
                id: true,
                key: true,
                name: true,
                resource: true,
                action: true,
                module: {
                  select: { key: true, name: true },
                },
              },
            },
          },
          orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
        },
      },
    });

    if (!assignment) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError("انتصاب پیدا نشد.", 404);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: assignment });
  } catch (error) {
    return jsonError(error);
  }
}

// POST - ASSIGN ROLE OR MULTIPLE PERMISSIONS
export async function POST(request: NextRequest, context: Context) {
  try {
    const { actor } = await requireOrganizationAccess(
      "organization.access.manage"
    );
    const { id } = await context.params;
    const body = await request.json();

    const assignment = await prisma.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
      where: { id },
      select: { id: true },
    });

    if (!assignment) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError("انتصاب پیدا نشد.", 404);
    }

    const expiresAt = parseDate(body.expiresAt, "تاریخ انقضا", true);

    // ASSIGN ROLE
    if (typeof body.roleId === "string" && body.roleId.trim()) {
      const role = await prisma.role.findUnique({
        where: { id: body.roleId },
        select: { id: true, isActive: true, isSystem: true },
      });

      if (!role || !role.isActive) {
        throw new OrganizationApiError(
          "نقش انتخاب‌شده معتبر یا فعال نیست."
        );
      }

      if (role.isSystem) {
        throw new OrganizationApiError(
          "نقش سیستمی را نمی‌توان مستقیماً به انتصاب اختصاص داد.",
          403
        );
      }

      const saved = await prisma.employeeAssignmentRole.upsert({
        where: {
          assignmentId_roleId: { assignmentId: id, roleId: role.id },
        },
        create: {
          assignmentId: id,
          roleId: role.id,
          assignedById: actor.id,
          expiresAt,
        },
        update: {
          isActive: true,
          assignedById: actor.id,
          expiresAt,
        },
        include: {
          role: { select: { id: true, key: true, name: true } },
        },
      });

      await audit(
        actor.id,
        "EmployeeAssignmentRole",
        saved.id,
        "ASSIGNMENT_ROLE_GRANTED",
        null,
        { assignmentId: id, roleId: role.id, expiresAt }
      );

      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: saved }, { status: 201 });
    }

    // MULTIPLE PERMISSIONS
    const rawPermissionIds: unknown =
      body.permissionIds ??
      (body.permissionId ? [body.permissionId] : []);

    if (
      !Array.isArray(rawPermissionIds) ||
      rawPermissionIds.length === 0 ||
      !rawPermissionIds.every(
        (value: unknown) =>
          typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0
      )
    ) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError("حداقل یک مجوز معتبر انتخاب کنید.");
    }

    const uniquePermissionIds = [
      ...new Set(
        (rawPermissionIds as string[]).map((value) => value.trim())
      ),
    ];

    // The old 200-item limit rejected valid bulk assignments.
    // Validate the unique IDs against the database instead.
    if (body.scope !== undefined) {
      if (
        typeof body.scope !== "string" ||
        !VALID_SCOPES.includes(
          body.scope as (typeof VALID_SCOPES)[number]
        )
      ) {
        throw new OrganizationApiError("محدوده دسترسی معتبر نیست.");
      }
    }

    if (
      body.isAllowed !== undefined &&
      typeof body.isAllowed !== "boolean"
    ) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError("وضعیت مجوز معتبر نیست.");
    }

    const isAllowed = body.isAllowed ?? true;
    const scope = body.scope ?? "OWN";

    // Check that every selected permission exists before changing anything.
    const permissions = await prisma.permission.findMany({
      where: { id: { in: uniquePermissionIds } },
      select: { id: true, key: true },
    });

    if (permissions.length !== uniquePermissionIds.length) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError(
        "یک یا چند مجوز انتخاب‌شده در سیستم وجود ندارد."
      );
    }

    // Preserve the previous state for audit records.
    const previous = await prisma.employeeAssignmentPermission.findMany({
      where: {
        assignmentId: id,
        permissionId: { in: uniquePermissionIds },
      },
    });

    const previousMap = new Map(
      previous.map((item) => [item.permissionId, item])
    );

    // Persist the entire selection atomically.
    const saved = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const results = [];

      for (const permission of permissions) {
        const item = await tx.employeeAssignmentPermission.upsert({
          where: {
            assignmentId_permissionId: {
              assignmentId: id,
              permissionId: permission.id,
            },
          },
          create: {
            assignmentId: id,
            permissionId: permission.id,
            isAllowed,
            scope,
            assignedById: actor.id,
            expiresAt,
          },
          update: {
            isActive: true,
            isAllowed,
            scope,
            assignedById: actor.id,
            expiresAt,
          },
          include: {
            permission: {
              select: { id: true, key: true, name: true },
            },
          },
        });

        results.push(item);
      }

      return results;
    });

    for (const item of saved) {
      const before = previousMap.get(item.permissionId) ?? null;
      await audit(
        actor.id,
        "EmployeeAssignmentPermission",
        item.id,
        "ASSIGNMENT_PERMISSION_UPSERTED",
        before,
        item
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: true,
        message: `${saved.length} مجوز با موفقیت ثبت شد.`,
        data: saved,
        count: saved.length,
      },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return jsonError(error);
  }
}

// DELETE - REVOKE ACCESS
export async function DELETE(request: NextRequest, context: Context) {
  try {
    const { actor } = await requireOrganizationAccess(
      "organization.access.manage"
    );
    const { id } = await context.params;
    const body = await request.json();

    if (
      typeof body.id !== "string" ||
      !["role", "permission"].includes(body.kind)
    ) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError("شناسه و نوع دسترسی الزامی است.");
    }

    if (body.kind === "role") {
      const before = await prisma.employeeAssignmentRole.findFirst({
        where: { id: body.id, assignmentId: id },
      });

      if (!before) {
        throw new OrganizationApiError("نقش انتصاب پیدا نشد.", 404);
      }

      const after = await prisma.employeeAssignmentRole.update({
        where: { id: body.id },
        data: { isActive: false },
      });

      await audit(
        actor.id,
        "EmployeeAssignmentRole",
        body.id,
        "ASSIGNMENT_ROLE_REVOKED",
        before,
        after
      );

      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: after });
    }

    const before = await prisma.employeeAssignmentPermission.findFirst({
      where: { id: body.id, assignmentId: id },
    });

    if (!before) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError("مجوز انتصاب پیدا نشد.", 404);
    }

    const after = await prisma.employeeAssignmentPermission.update({
      where: { id: body.id },
      data: { isActive: false },
    });

    await audit(
      actor.id,
      "EmployeeAssignmentPermission",
      body.id,
      "ASSIGNMENT_PERMISSION_REVOKED",
      before,
      after
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: after });
  } catch (error) {
    return jsonError(error);
  }
}

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import {
  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";
import {
  assertSameOriginRequest,
  authorizeRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

type Context = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

function getStatus(error: unknown) {
  if (
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    "statusCode" in error &&
    typeof error.statusCode === "number"
  ) return error.statusCode;

  return 500;
}

function getIp(request: NextRequest) {
  return (
    request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
    request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")?.split(",")[0].trim() ||
    undefined
  );
}

function isObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

export async function POST(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: Context
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const authorization = await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "organization.assignments.manage"
    );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    let body: unknown;
    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      return errorResponse("اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      return errorResponse("ساختار اطلاعات معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    const allowed = [
      "organizationUnitId",
      "positionId",
      "locationId",
      "managerAssignmentId",
      "assignmentType",
      "startDate",
      "notes",
    ];

    if (Object.keys(body).some(key => !allowed.includes(key))) {
      return errorResponse("فیلد غیرمجاز ارسال شده است.", 400);
    }

    if (
      typeof body.organizationUnitId !== "string" ||
      typeof body.positionId !== "string"
    ) {
      return errorResponse(
        "واحد و سمت جدید الزامی هستند.",
        400
      );
    }

    const startDate =
      body.startDate === undefined
        ? new Date()
        : typeof body.startDate === "string" &&
          !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(body.startDate))
          ? new Date(body.startDate)
          : null;

    if (!startDate) {
      return errorResponse("تاریخ شروع معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    const assignmentType =
      body.assignmentType === undefined
        ? "PRIMARY"
        : body.assignmentType;

    if (
      !["PRIMARY", "SECONDARY", "TEMPORARY", "PROJECT"].includes(
        assignmentType as string
      )
    ) {
      return errorResponse("نوع انتصاب معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (
      body.locationId !== undefined &&
      body.locationId !== null &&
      typeof body.locationId !== "string"
    ) {
      return errorResponse("محل جدید معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (
      body.managerAssignmentId !== undefined &&
      body.managerAssignmentId !== null &&
      typeof body.managerAssignmentId !== "string"
    ) {
      return errorResponse("مسئول بالادستی معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (
      body.notes !== undefined &&
      body.notes !== null &&
      typeof body.notes !== "string"
    ) {
      return errorResponse("یادداشت معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    const result = await prisma.$transaction(async tx => {
      const previous = await tx.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
        where: { id },
      });

      if (!previous) throw new Error("ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND");

      if (previous.status !== "ACTIVE") {
        throw new Error("ASSIGNMENT_NOT_ACTIVE");
      }

      if (startDate < previous.startDate) {
        throw new Error("INVALID_TRANSFER_DATE");
      }

      const [unit, position, location] = await Promise.all([
        tx.organizationUnit.findUnique({
          where: { id: body.organizationUnitId as string },
        }),
        tx.organizationPosition.findUnique({
          where: { id: body.positionId as string },
        }),
        body.locationId
          ? tx.organizationLocation.findUnique({
              where: { id: body.locationId as string },
            })
          : Promise.resolve(null),
      ]);

      if (!unit || unit.status !== "ACTIVE") {
        throw new Error("INVALID_UNIT");
      }

      if (!position || !position.isActive) {
        throw new Error("INVALID_POSITION");
      }

      if (body.locationId && (!location || location.status !== "ACTIVE")) {
        throw new Error("INVALID_LOCATION");
      }

      const managerAssignmentId =
        body.managerAssignmentId === undefined
          ? null
          : body.managerAssignmentId as string | null;

      if (managerAssignmentId) {
        if (managerAssignmentId === id) {
          throw new Error("INVALID_MANAGER");
        }

        const manager = await tx.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
          where: { id: managerAssignmentId },
        });

        if (!manager || manager.status !== "ACTIVE") {
          throw new Error("INVALID_MANAGER");
        }
      }

      if (assignmentType === "PRIMARY") {
        await tx.employeeAssignment.updateMany({
          where: {
            userId: previous.userId,
            status: "ACTIVE",
            isPrimary: true,
          },
          data: {
            isPrimary: false,
            assignmentType: "SECONDARY",
          },
        });
      }

      const endedPrevious = await tx.employeeAssignment.update({
        where: { id },
        data: {
          status: "ENDED",
          endDate: startDate,
          isPrimary: false,
        },
      });

      const created = await tx.employeeAssignment.create({
        data: {
          userId: previous.userId,
          organizationUnitId: body.organizationUnitId as string,
          positionId: body.positionId as string,
          locationId: body.locationId as string | null | undefined,
          managerAssignmentId,
          assignmentType: assignmentType as
            "PRIMARY" | "SECONDARY" | "TEMPORARY" | "PROJECT",
          isPrimary: assignmentType === "PRIMARY",
          startDate,
          notes: body.notes as string | null | undefined,
          status: "ACTIVE",
        },
      });

      await tx.organizationAssignmentHistory.createMany({
        data: [
          {
            assignmentId: id,
            action: "ASSIGNMENT_TRANSFERRED_OUT",
            previousData: previous,
            newData: endedPrevious,
            changedByUserId: authorization.user.id,
          },
          {
  assignmentId: created.id,
  action: "ASSIGNMENT_TRANSFERRED_IN",
  previousData: Prisma.DbNull,
  newData: created,
  changedByUserId: authorization.user.id,
},
        ],
      });

      await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
        data: {
          actorUserId: authorization.user.id,
          entityType: "EmployeeAssignment",
          entityId: id,
          action: "EMPLOYEE_ASSIGNMENT_TRANSFERRED",
          ipAddress: getIp(request),
          beforeData: previous,
          afterData: {
            endedAssignment: endedPrevious,
            newAssignment: created,
          },
        },
      });

      return {
        previous: endedPrevious,
        current: created,
      };
    }, {
      isolationLevel: "Serializable",
    });

    return successResponse({
      ...result,
      message: "انتقال سازمانی با موفقیت انجام شد.",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      const messages: Record<string, string> = {
        ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND: "انتصاب قبلی پیدا نشد.",
        ASSIGNMENT_NOT_ACTIVE: "انتصاب قبلی فعال نیست.",
        INVALID_TRANSFER_DATE:
          "تاریخ انتقال نمی‌تواند قبل از شروع انتصاب قبلی باشد.",
        INVALID_UNIT: "واحد جدید معتبر یا فعال نیست.",
        INVALID_POSITION: "سمت جدید معتبر یا فعال نیست.",
        INVALID_LOCATION: "محل جدید معتبر یا فعال نیست.",
        INVALID_MANAGER: "مسئول بالادستی معتبر نیست.",
      };

      if (messages[error.message]) {
        return errorResponse(
          messages[error.message],
          error.message === "ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND" ? 404 : 400
        );
      }
    }

    if (
      typeof error === "object" &&
      error !== null &&
      "code" in error &&
      error.code === "P2034"
    ) {
      return errorResponse(
        "اطلاعات هم‌زمان تغییر کرده است. دوباره تلاش کنید.",
        409
      );
    }

    const status = getStatus(error);
    if (status !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "درخواست معتبر نیست.",
        status
      );
    }

    console.error("[ASSIGNMENT_TRANSFER]", error);
    return errorResponse("خطا در انتقال انتصاب.", 500);
  }
}

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";

import {
  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  assertSameOriginRequest,
  authorizeRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

type RouteContext = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

function isObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function getStatusCode(error: unknown): number {
  if (
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    "statusCode" in error &&
    typeof error.statusCode === "number"
  ) return error.statusCode;

  return 500;
}

function getIp(request: NextRequest) {
  return (
    request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
    request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")?.split(",")[0].trim() ||
    undefined
  );
}

const allowedFields = [
  "code", "name", "locationType", "description",
  "country", "province", "city", "address", "phone",
  "status",
];

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    await authorizeRequest(request, "organization.chart.view");

    const { id } = await context.params;

    const location = await prisma.organizationLocation.findUnique({
      where: { id },
      include: {
        units: {
          select: {
            id: true,
            code: true,
            name: true,
            status: true,
          },
          orderBy: { name: "asc" },
        },
        _count: {
          select: {
            units: true,
            assignments: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });

    if (!location) {
      return errorResponse("محل سازمانی پیدا نشد.", 404);
    }

    return successResponse({ location });
  } catch (error) {
    const status = getStatusCode(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "دسترسی مجاز نیست.",
        status
      );
    }

    console.error("[ORGANIZATION_LOCATION_GET]", error);
    return errorResponse("خطا در دریافت محل سازمانی.", 500);
  }
}

export async function PATCH(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const authorization = await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "organization.locations.manage"
    );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    let body: unknown;

    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      return errorResponse("اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      return errorResponse("ساختار اطلاعات معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (Object.keys(body).some(key => !allowedFields.includes(key))) {
      return errorResponse("فیلد غیرمجاز در درخواست وجود دارد.", 400);
    }

    if (Object.keys(body).length === 0) {
      return errorResponse("اطلاعاتی برای ویرایش ارسال نشده است.", 400);
    }

    for (const field of ["code", "name", "locationType"]) {
      if (body[field] === undefined) continue;

      if (
        typeof body[field] !== "string" ||
        (body[field] as string).trim().length < 2 ||
        (body[field] as string).trim().length > 150
      ) {
        return errorResponse(`فیلد ${field} معتبر نیست.`, 400);
      }
    }

    const optionalFields = [
      "description", "country", "province",
      "city", "address", "phone",
    ];

    for (const field of optionalFields) {
      if (
        body[field] !== undefined &&
        body[field] !== null &&
        typeof body[field] !== "string"
      ) {
        return errorResponse(`مقدار ${field} معتبر نیست.`, 400);
      }
    }

    if (
      body.status !== undefined &&
      body.status !== "ACTIVE" &&
      body.status !== "INACTIVE"
    ) {
      return errorResponse("وضعیت محل معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    const result = await prisma.$transaction(async tx => {
      const existing = await tx.organizationLocation.findUnique({
        where: { id },
      });

      if (!existing) {
        throw new Error("LOCATION_NOT_FOUND");
      }

      if (body.status === "INACTIVE") {
        const now = new Date();

        const [activeUnits, activeAssignments] = await Promise.all([
          tx.organizationUnit.count({
            where: {
              locationId: id,
              status: "ACTIVE",
            },
          }),
          tx.employeeAssignment.count({
            where: {
              locationId: id,
              status: "ACTIVE",
              OR: [
                { endDate: null },
                { endDate: { gt: now } },
              ],
            },
          }),
        ]);

        if (activeUnits > 0 || activeAssignments > 0) {
          throw new Error("LOCATION_IN_USE");
        }
      }

      const data = {
        ...(body.code !== undefined
          ? { code: (body.code as string).trim().toUpperCase() }
          : {}),
        ...(body.name !== undefined
          ? { name: (body.name as string).trim() }
          : {}),
        ...(body.locationType !== undefined
          ? { locationType: (body.locationType as string).trim() }
          : {}),
        ...Object.fromEntries(
          optionalFields
            .filter(field => body[field] !== undefined)
            .map(field => [
              field,
              typeof body[field] === "string"
                ? (body[field] as string).trim() || null
                : null,
            ])
        ),
        ...(body.status !== undefined
          ? { status: body.status as "ACTIVE" | "INACTIVE" }
          : {}),
      };

      const updated = await tx.organizationLocation.update({
        where: { id },
        data,
      });

      await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
        data: {
          actorUserId: authorization.user.id,
          entityType: "OrganizationLocation",
          entityId: id,
          action: body.status === "INACTIVE"
            ? "ORGANIZATION_LOCATION_DEACTIVATED"
            : body.status === "ACTIVE"
              ? "ORGANIZATION_LOCATION_ACTIVATED"
              : "ORGANIZATION_LOCATION_UPDATED",
          ipAddress: getIp(request),
          beforeData: existing,
          afterData: updated,
        },
      });

      return updated;
    });

    return successResponse({
      location: result,
      message: "محل سازمانی با موفقیت به‌روزرسانی شد.",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      if (error.message === "LOCATION_NOT_FOUND") {
        return errorResponse("محل سازمانی پیدا نشد.", 404);
      }

      if (error.message === "LOCATION_IN_USE") {
        return errorResponse(
          "این محل دارای واحد یا انتصاب فعال است.",
          409
        );
      }
    }

    if (
      typeof error === "object" &&
      error !== null &&
      "code" in error &&
      error.code === "P2002"
    ) {
      return errorResponse("کد محل قبلاً ثبت شده است.", 409);
    }

    const status = getStatusCode(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "درخواست معتبر نیست.",
        status
      );
    }

    console.error("[ORGANIZATION_LOCATION_PATCH]", error);
    return errorResponse("خطا در ویرایش محل سازمانی.", 500);
  }
}

export async function DELETE(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const authorization = await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "organization.locations.manage"
    );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    const result = await prisma.$transaction(async tx => {
      const existing = await tx.organizationLocation.findUnique({
        where: { id },
      });

      if (!existing) {
        throw new Error("LOCATION_NOT_FOUND");
      }

      if (existing.status === "INACTIVE") {
        throw new Error("LOCATION_ALREADY_INACTIVE");
      }

      const now = new Date();

      const [activeUnits, activeAssignments] = await Promise.all([
        tx.organizationUnit.count({
          where: {
            locationId: id,
            status: "ACTIVE",
          },
        }),
        tx.employeeAssignment.count({
          where: {
            locationId: id,
            status: "ACTIVE",
            OR: [
              { endDate: null },
              { endDate: { gt: now } },
            ],
          },
        }),
      ]);

      if (activeUnits > 0 || activeAssignments > 0) {
        throw new Error("LOCATION_IN_USE");
      }

      const updated = await tx.organizationLocation.update({
        where: { id },
        data: { status: "INACTIVE" },
      });

      await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
        data: {
          actorUserId: authorization.user.id,
          entityType: "OrganizationLocation",
          entityId: id,
          action: "ORGANIZATION_LOCATION_DEACTIVATED",
          ipAddress: getIp(request),
          beforeData: {
            code: existing.code,
            name: existing.name,
            status: existing.status,
          },
          afterData: {
            code: updated.code,
            name: updated.name,
            status: updated.status,
          },
        },
      });

      return updated;
    });

    return successResponse({
      location: result,
      message: "محل سازمانی غیرفعال شد.",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      const messages: Record<string, string> = {
        LOCATION_NOT_FOUND: "محل سازمانی پیدا نشد.",
        LOCATION_ALREADY_INACTIVE: "محل قبلاً غیرفعال شده است.",
        LOCATION_IN_USE:
          "این محل دارای واحد یا انتصاب فعال است و قابل غیرفعال‌سازی نیست.",
      };

      if (messages[error.message]) {
        return errorResponse(
          messages[error.message],
          error.message === "LOCATION_NOT_FOUND" ? 404 : 409
        );
      }
    }

    const status = getStatusCode(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "درخواست معتبر نیست.",
        status
      );
    }

    console.error("[ORGANIZATION_LOCATION_DELETE]", error);
    return errorResponse("خطا در غیرفعال‌سازی محل.", 500);
  }
}
