import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import {
  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";
import {
  assertSameOriginRequest,
  authorizeRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

type Context = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

function getStatus(error: unknown) {
  if (
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    "statusCode" in error &&
    typeof error.statusCode === "number"
  ) return error.statusCode;

  return 500;
}

function getIp(request: NextRequest) {
  return (
    request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
    request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")?.split(",")[0].trim() ||
    undefined
  );
}

function isObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: Context
) {
  try {
    await authorizeRequest(request, "organization.chart.view");

    const { id } = await context.params;

    const position = await prisma.organizationPosition.findUnique({
      where: { id },
      include: {
        _count: {
          select: { assignments: true },
        },
        assignments: {
          where: { status: "ACTIVE" },
          select: {
            id: true,
            userId: true,
            organizationUnitId: true,
            status: true,
            isPrimary: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });

    if (!position) {
      return errorResponse("سمت سازمانی پیدا نشد.", 404);
    }

    return successResponse({ position });
  } catch (error) {
    const status = getStatus(error);
    if (status !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "دسترسی مجاز نیست.",
        status
      );
    }

    console.error("[POSITION_GET]", error);
    return errorResponse("خطا در دریافت سمت سازمانی.", 500);
  }
}

export async function PATCH(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: Context
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const authorization = await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "organization.positions.manage"
    );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    let body: unknown;
    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      return errorResponse("اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      return errorResponse("ساختار اطلاعات معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    const allowed = [
      "code",
      "title",
      "description",
      "level",
      "isManagerial",
      "isActive",
    ];

    if (Object.keys(body).some(key => !allowed.includes(key))) {
      return errorResponse("فیلد غیرمجاز ارسال شده است.", 400);
    }

    if (
      body.code !== undefined &&
      (
        typeof body.code !== "string" ||
        body.code.trim().length < 2 ||
        body.code.trim().length > 50 ||
        !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.test(body.code.trim())
      )
    ) {
      return errorResponse("کد سمت معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (
      body.title !== undefined &&
      (
        typeof body.title !== "string" ||
        body.title.trim().length < 2 ||
        body.title.trim().length > 150
      )
    ) {
      return errorResponse("عنوان سمت معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (
      body.description !== undefined &&
      body.description !== null &&
      typeof body.description !== "string"
    ) {
      return errorResponse("توضیحات معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (
      body.level !== undefined &&
      (
        typeof body.level !== "number" ||
        !Number.isSafeInteger(body.level) ||
        body.level < 1 ||
        body.level > 100
      )
    ) {
      return errorResponse("سطح سمت معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (
      body.isManagerial !== undefined &&
      typeof body.isManagerial !== "boolean"
    ) {
      return errorResponse("مقدار مدیریتی بودن معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (
      body.isActive !== undefined &&
      typeof body.isActive !== "boolean"
    ) {
      return errorResponse("وضعیت سمت معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    if (Object.keys(body).length === 0) {
      return errorResponse("اطلاعاتی برای ویرایش ارسال نشده است.", 400);
    }

    const result = await prisma.$transaction(async tx => {
      const existing = await tx.organizationPosition.findUnique({
        where: { id },
      });

      if (!existing) throw new Error("POSITION_NOT_FOUND");

      if (body.isActive === false) {
        const activeCount = await tx.employeeAssignment.count({
          where: {
            positionId: id,
            status: "ACTIVE",
          },
        });

        if (activeCount > 0) {
          throw new Error("POSITION_IN_USE");
        }
      }

      const updated = await tx.organizationPosition.update({
        where: { id },
        data: {
          ...(body.code !== undefined
            ? { code: (body.code as string).trim().toUpperCase() }
            : {}),
          ...(body.title !== undefined
            ? { title: (body.title as string).trim() }
            : {}),
          ...(body.description !== undefined
            ? {
                description:
                  typeof body.description === "string"
                    ? body.description.trim() || null
                    : null,
              }
            : {}),
          ...(body.level !== undefined
            ? { level: body.level as number }
            : {}),
          ...(body.isManagerial !== undefined
            ? { isManagerial: body.isManagerial as boolean }
            : {}),
          ...(body.isActive !== undefined
            ? { isActive: body.isActive as boolean }
            : {}),
        },
      });

      await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
        data: {
          actorUserId: authorization.user.id,
          entityType: "OrganizationPosition",
          entityId: id,
          action: "ORGANIZATION_POSITION_UPDATED",
          ipAddress: getIp(request),
          beforeData: existing,
          afterData: updated,
        },
      });

      return updated;
    });

    return successResponse({
      position: result,
      message: "سمت سازمانی به‌روزرسانی شد.",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      if (error.message === "POSITION_NOT_FOUND") {
        return errorResponse("سمت پیدا نشد.", 404);
      }
      if (error.message === "POSITION_IN_USE") {
        return errorResponse(
          "این سمت دارای انتصاب فعال است.",
          409
        );
      }
    }

    if (
      typeof error === "object" &&
      error !== null &&
      "code" in error &&
      error.code === "P2002"
    ) {
      return errorResponse("کد سمت تکراری است.", 409);
    }

    const status = getStatus(error);
    if (status !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "درخواست نامعتبر است.",
        status
      );
    }

    console.error("[POSITION_PATCH]", error);
    return errorResponse("خطا در ویرایش سمت.", 500);
  }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\Ehsan\Desktop\sahand\app\api\organization\relationships\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";
import {
  audit,
  jsonError,
  OrganizationApiError,
  parseDate,
  requireOrganizationAccess,
} from "@/modules/organization/organization-api";

const RELATIONSHIP_TYPES = [
  "REPORTS_TO",
  "FUNCTIONAL_REPORTS_TO",
  "SUPERVISES",
  "COLLABORATES_WITH",
  "ADVISES",
  "DELEGATES_TO",
] as const;

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    await requireOrganizationAccess("organization.relationships.view");

    const status = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get("status");
    if (status && !["ACTIVE", "ENDED", "SUSPENDED"].includes(status)) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError("فیلتر وضعیت معتبر نیست.");
    }

    const relationships = await prisma.organizationRelationship.findMany({
      where: status ? { status: status as "ACTIVE" | "ENDED" | "SUSPENDED" } : {},
      include: {
        sourceAssignment: {
          include: {
            user: { select: { id: true, username: true, fullName: true } },
            position: { select: { title: true } },
            organizationUnit: { select: { name: true } },
          },
        },
        targetAssignment: {
          include: {
            user: { select: { id: true, username: true, fullName: true } },
            position: { select: { title: true } },
            organizationUnit: { select: { name: true } },
          },
        },
      },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: { relationships, count: relationships.length },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return jsonError(error);
  }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { actor } = await requireOrganizationAccess(
      "organization.relationships.manage"
    );
    const body = await request.json();

    const { sourceAssignmentId, targetAssignmentId, relationshipType } = body;

    if (
      typeof sourceAssignmentId !== "string" ||
      !sourceAssignmentId.trim() ||
      typeof targetAssignmentId !== "string" ||
      !targetAssignmentId.trim()
    ) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError("هر دو انتصاب الزامی هستند.");
    }

    if (sourceAssignmentId === targetAssignmentId) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError(
        "مبدأ و مقصد رابطه نمی‌توانند یک انتصاب باشند."
      );
    }

    if (!RELATIONSHIP_TYPES.includes(relationshipType)) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError("نوع رابطه معتبر نیست.");
    }

    const [source, target] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
        where: { id: sourceAssignmentId },
        select: { id: true, status: true },
      }),
      prisma.employeeAssignment.findUnique({
        where: { id: targetAssignmentId },
        select: { id: true, status: true },
      }),
    ]);

    if (!source || !target) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError(
        "یکی از انتصاب‌های انتخاب‌شده پیدا نشد.",
        404
      );
    }

    if (source.status !== "ACTIVE" || target.status !== "ACTIVE") {
      throw new OrganizationApiError(
        "فقط برای انتصاب فعال می‌توان رابطه ایجاد کرد."
      );
    }

    const startDate = parseDate(
      body.startDate ?? new Date().toISOString(),
      "تاریخ شروع"
    )!;

    const endDate = parseDate(body.endDate, "تاریخ پایان", true);

    if (endDate && endDate < startDate) {
      throw new OrganizationApiError(
        "تاریخ پایان نمی‌تواند قبل از تاریخ شروع باشد."
      );
    }

    const created = await prisma.organizationRelationship.create({
      data: {
        sourceAssignmentId,
        targetAssignmentId,
        relationshipType,
        status: "ACTIVE",
        startDate,
        endDate,
        description:
          typeof body.description === "string"
            ? body.description.trim() || null
            : null,
        createdByUserId: actor.id,
      },
      include: {
        sourceAssignment: {
          include: {
            user: { select: { fullName: true, username: true } },
            position: { select: { title: true } },
          },
        },
        targetAssignment: {
          include: {
            user: { select: { fullName: true, username: true } },
            position: { select: { title: true } },
          },
        },
      },
    });

    await audit(
      actor.id,
      "OrganizationRelationship",
      created.id,
      "ORGANIZATION_RELATIONSHIP_CREATED",
      undefined,
      created
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: true, data: created },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return jsonError(error);
  }
}

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";

import { prisma } from "@/libs/prisma";

import {
  successResponse,
  errorResponse,
} from "@/libs/api-response";

import {
  assertSameOriginRequest,
  authorizeRequest,
} from "@/modules/auth/auth.middleware";

type RouteContext = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

type UnitInput = {
  code?: string;
  name?: string;
  description?: string | null;
  locationId?: string | null;
  sortOrder?: number;
  status?: "ACTIVE" | "INACTIVE";
};

function isObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

function getErrorCode(error: unknown): string | null {
  if (
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    "code" in error &&
    typeof error.code === "string"
  ) {
    return error.code;
  }

  return null;
}

function getStatusCode(error: unknown): number {
  if (
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    "statusCode" in error &&
    typeof error.statusCode === "number"
  ) {
    return error.statusCode;
  }

  return 500;
}

function getRequestIp(request: NextRequest) {
  return (
    request.headers.get("x-real-ip") ||
    request.headers
      .get("x-forwarded-for")
      ?.split(",")[0]
      .trim() ||
    undefined
  );
}

function validateInput(
  body: Record<string, unknown>
): {
  success: boolean;
  data?: UnitInput;
  message?: string;
} {
  const allowedFields = new Set([
    "code",
    "name",
    "description",
    "locationId",
    "sortOrder",
    "status",
  ]);

  for (const key of Object.keys(body)) {
    if (!allowedFields.has(key)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `فیلد غیرمجاز: ${key}`,
      };
    }
  }

  if (body.code !== undefined) {
    if (
      typeof body.code !== "string" ||
      body.code.trim().length < 2 ||
      body.code.trim().length > 50 ||
      !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.test(body.code.trim())
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "کد واحد معتبر نیست.",
      };
    }
  }

  if (body.name !== undefined) {
    if (
      typeof body.name !== "string" ||
      body.name.trim().length < 2 ||
      body.name.trim().length > 150
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "نام واحد باید بین ۲ تا ۱۵۰ کاراکتر باشد.",
      };
    }
  }

  if (
    body.description !== undefined &&
    body.description !== null &&
    (
      typeof body.description !== "string" ||
      body.description.length > 1000
    )
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "توضیحات واحد معتبر نیست.",
    };
  }

  if (
    body.locationId !== undefined &&
    body.locationId !== null &&
    (
      typeof body.locationId !== "string" ||
      !body.locationId.trim()
    )
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "شناسه محل سازمانی معتبر نیست.",
    };
  }

  if (
    body.sortOrder !== undefined &&
    (
      typeof body.sortOrder !== "number" ||
      !Number.isSafeInteger(body.sortOrder) ||
      body.sortOrder < 0 ||
      body.sortOrder > 100000
    )
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "ترتیب نمایش واحد معتبر نیست.",
    };
  }

  if (
    body.status !== undefined &&
    body.status !== "ACTIVE" &&
    body.status !== "INACTIVE"
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "وضعیت واحد معتبر نیست.",
    };
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    data: {
      ...(body.code !== undefined
        ? { code: (body.code as string).trim().toUpperCase() }
        : {}),
      ...(body.name !== undefined
        ? { name: (body.name as string).trim() }
        : {}),
      ...(body.description !== undefined
        ? {
            description:
              typeof body.description === "string"
                ? body.description.trim() || null
                : null,
          }
        : {}),
      ...(body.locationId !== undefined
        ? {
            locationId:
              typeof body.locationId === "string"
                ? body.locationId.trim()
                : null,
          }
        : {}),
      ...(body.sortOrder !== undefined
        ? { sortOrder: body.sortOrder as number }
        : {}),
      ...(body.status !== undefined
        ? { status: body.status as "ACTIVE" | "INACTIVE" }
        : {}),
    },
  };
}

async function getUnit(id: string) {
  return prisma.organizationUnit.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    include: {
      parent: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          code: true,
          name: true,
        },
      },
      children: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          code: true,
          name: true,
          status: true,
        },
        orderBy: {
          sortOrder: "asc",
        },
      },
      location: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          code: true,
          name: true,
          status: true,
        },
      },
      _count: {
        select: {
          assignments: true,
          children: true,
        },
      },
    },
  });
}

// =====================================================
// GET: دریافت جزئیات واحد سازمانی
// =====================================================

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "organization.chart.view"
    );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    if (!id?.trim()) {
      return errorResponse("شناسه واحد معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    const unit = await getUnit(id);

    if (!unit) {
      return errorResponse("واحد موردنظر پیدا نشد.", 404);
    }

    return successResponse({ unit });

  } catch (error) {
    const statusCode = getStatusCode(error);

    if (statusCode !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "دریافت اطلاعات واحد امکان‌پذیر نیست.",
        statusCode
      );
    }

    console.error("[ORGANIZATION_UNIT_GET_ERROR]", error);

    return errorResponse(
      "خطا در دریافت اطلاعات واحد سازمانی.",
      500
    );
  }
}

// =====================================================
// PATCH: ویرایش واحد سازمانی
// =====================================================

export async function PATCH(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const authorization = await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "organization.units.manage"
    );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    if (!id?.trim()) {
      return errorResponse("شناسه واحد معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    let body: unknown;

    try {
      body = await request.json();
    } catch {
      return errorResponse(
        "ساختار اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست.",
        400
      );
    }

    if (!isObject(body)) {
      return errorResponse("اطلاعات واحد معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    const validation = validateInput(body);

    if (!validation.success || !validation.data) {
      return errorResponse(
        validation.message || "اطلاعات واحد معتبر نیست.",
        400
      );
    }

    const data = validation.data;

    const updated = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const existing = await tx.organizationUnit.findUnique({
        where: { id },
      });

      if (!existing) {
        throw new Error("UNIT_NOT_FOUND");
      }

      // غیرفعال‌سازی فقط زمانی مجاز است که
      // زیرواحد فعال یا انتصاب فعال وجود نداشته باشد.

      if (data.status === "INACTIVE") {
        const now = new Date();

        const [activeChildren, activeAssignments] =
          await Promise.all([
            tx.organizationUnit.count({
              where: {
                parentId: id,
                status: "ACTIVE",
              },
            }),

            tx.employeeAssignment.count({
              where: {
                organizationUnitId: id,
                status: "ACTIVE",
                OR: [
                  { endDate: null },
                  { endDate: { gt: now } },
                ],
              },
            }),
          ]);

        if (activeChildren > 0) {
          throw new Error("UNIT_HAS_ACTIVE_CHILDREN");
        }

        if (activeAssignments > 0) {
          throw new Error("UNIT_HAS_ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENTS");
        }
      }

      const changes = {
        ...(data.code !== undefined
          ? { code: data.code }
          : {}),
        ...(data.name !== undefined
          ? { name: data.name }
          : {}),
        ...(data.description !== undefined
          ? { description: data.description }
          : {}),
        ...(data.locationId !== undefined
          ? { locationId: data.locationId }
          : {}),
        ...(data.sortOrder !== undefined
          ? { sortOrder: data.sortOrder }
          : {}),
        ...(data.status !== undefined
          ? { status: data.status }
          : {}),
      };

      const unit = await tx.organizationUnit.update({
        where: { id },
        data: changes,
      });

      await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
        data: {
          actorUserId: authorization.user.id,
          entityType: "OrganizationUnit",
          entityId: unit.id,
          action:
            data.status === "INACTIVE"
              ? "ORGANIZATION_UNIT_DEACTIVATED"
              : "ORGANIZATION_UNIT_UPDATED",
          ipAddress: getRequestIp(request),
          beforeData: {
            code: existing.code,
            name: existing.name,
            description: existing.description,
            locationId: existing.locationId,
            sortOrder: existing.sortOrder,
            status: existing.status,
          },
          afterData: {
            code: unit.code,
            name: unit.name,
            description: unit.description,
            locationId: unit.locationId,
            sortOrder: unit.sortOrder,
            status: unit.status,
          },
        },
      });

      return unit;
    });

    return successResponse({
      unit: updated,
      message: "اطلاعات واحد سازمانی با موفقیت به‌روزرسانی شد.",
    });

  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      const messages: Record<string, string> = {
        UNIT_NOT_FOUND: "واحد موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
        UNIT_HAS_ACTIVE_CHILDREN:
          "ابتدا باید زیرواحدهای فعال را تعیین تکلیف کنید.",
        UNIT_HAS_ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENTS:
          "این واحد دارای انتصاب فعال است و فعلاً قابل غیرفعال‌سازی نیست.",
      };

      if (messages[error.message]) {
        return errorResponse(
          messages[error.message],
          error.message === "UNIT_NOT_FOUND" ? 404 : 409
        );
      }
    }

    const code = getErrorCode(error);

    if (code === "P2002") {
      return errorResponse(
        "کد این واحد قبلاً ثبت شده است.",
        409
      );
    }

    if (code === "P2003" || code === "P2025") {
      return errorResponse(
        "اطلاعات وابسته به واحد معتبر نیست.",
        409
      );
    }

    const statusCode = getStatusCode(error);

    if (statusCode !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "درخواست معتبر نیست.",
        statusCode
      );
    }

    console.error("[ORGANIZATION_UNIT_PATCH_ERROR]", error);

    return errorResponse(
      "خطا در ویرایش واحد سازمانی.",
      500
    );
  }
}

// =====================================================
// DELETE: غیرفعال‌سازی امن واحد سازمانی
// =====================================================

export async function DELETE(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    assertSameOriginRequest(request);

    const authorization = await authorizeRequest(
      request,
      "organization.units.manage"
    );

    const { id } = await context.params;

    if (!id?.trim()) {
      return errorResponse("شناسه واحد معتبر نیست.", 400);
    }

    const unit = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const existing = await tx.organizationUnit.findUnique({
        where: { id },
      });

      if (!existing) {
        throw new Error("UNIT_NOT_FOUND");
      }

      if (existing.status === "INACTIVE") {
        throw new Error("UNIT_ALREADY_INACTIVE");
      }

      const now = new Date();

      const [activeChildren, activeAssignments] =
        await Promise.all([
          tx.organizationUnit.count({
            where: {
              parentId: id,
              status: "ACTIVE",
            },
          }),

          tx.employeeAssignment.count({
            where: {
              organizationUnitId: id,
              status: "ACTIVE",
              OR: [
                { endDate: null },
                { endDate: { gt: now } },
              ],
            },
          }),
        ]);

      if (activeChildren > 0) {
        throw new Error("UNIT_HAS_ACTIVE_CHILDREN");
      }

      if (activeAssignments > 0) {
        throw new Error("UNIT_HAS_ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENTS");
      }

      const updated = await tx.organizationUnit.update({
        where: { id },
        data: {
          status: "INACTIVE",
        },
      });

      await tx.organizationAuditLog.create({
        data: {
          actorUserId: authorization.user.id,
          entityType: "OrganizationUnit",
          entityId: updated.id,
          action: "ORGANIZATION_UNIT_DEACTIVATED",
          ipAddress: getRequestIp(request),
          beforeData: {
            code: existing.code,
            name: existing.name,
            status: existing.status,
          },
          afterData: {
            code: updated.code,
            name: updated.name,
            status: updated.status,
          },
        },
      });

      return updated;
    });

    return successResponse({
      unit,
      message: "واحد سازمانی با موفقیت غیرفعال شد.",
    });

  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      const messages: Record<string, string> = {
        UNIT_NOT_FOUND: "واحد موردنظر پیدا نشد.",
        UNIT_ALREADY_INACTIVE: "این واحد قبلاً غیرفعال شده است.",
        UNIT_HAS_ACTIVE_CHILDREN:
          "ابتدا باید زیرواحدهای فعال را تعیین تکلیف کنید.",
        UNIT_HAS_ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENTS:
          "این واحد دارای انتصاب فعال است و قابل غیرفعال‌سازی نیست.",
      };

      if (messages[error.message]) {
        return errorResponse(
          messages[error.message],
          error.message === "UNIT_NOT_FOUND" ? 404 : 409
        );
      }
    }

    const statusCode = getStatusCode(error);

    if (statusCode !== 500) {
      return errorResponse(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "درخواست معتبر نیست.",
        statusCode
      );
    }

    console.error("[ORGANIZATION_UNIT_DELETE_ERROR]", error);

    return errorResponse(
      "خطا در غیرفعال‌سازی واحد سازمانی.",
      500
    );
  }
}
